  <script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const loader = document.getElementById("loading-overlay");
  
    const unlockScroll = () => {
      document.documentElement.classList.add("loaded");
      document.body.classList.add("loaded");
    };
  
    if (!loader) {
      unlockScroll();
      return;
    }
  
    const removeLoader = () => {
      loader.remove();
    };
  
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      loader.style.opacity = "0";
      unlockScroll();
  
      loader.addEventListener("transitionend", removeLoader, { once: true });
      setTimeout(removeLoader, 180);
    });
  });
  </script>
  
  <script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {

    // 1. DEFINE TARGET NODE FIRST
    const targetNode = document.querySelector('#commentsPluginCtn654732') || document.body;
  
    // 2. RUN FUNCTION WITH FULLY REPLACED QUERIES
    const updateButtonHTML = () => {
      const b1 = targetNode.querySelector('.tabbed-comments-show-auto-comment-area-btn')
      if (b1 && b1.textContent.trim() === '🧝 speak via character')
        b1.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-user-astronaut" style="font-size:0.75rem;margin-right:0.35em;"></i> AI Character'
  
      const b2 = targetNode.querySelector('.tabbed-comments-auto-comment-char-btn')
      if (b2 && b2.textContent.trim() === '🧝 write as character')
        b2.textContent = 'Generate Comment ➔'
  
      targetNode.querySelectorAll('.tabbed-comments-plugin-close-panel-btn').forEach(btn => {
        if (btn.dataset._patched) return
        if (btn.textContent.trim() === '❌') {
          btn.dataset._patched = 1
          btn.innerHTML = `Close<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1.3em" height="1.3em" viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="vertical-align: middle; margin-left:1px;">
  <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="11" fill="#AB0E0E"/>
  <path fill="#fff" d="M8.818 7.403L12 10.586l3.182-3.182l1.414 1.414L13.414 12l3.182 3.182l-1.415 1.414L12 13.414l-3.182 3.182l-1.415-1.414L10.586 12L7.403 8.818z"/>
</svg>`
        }
      })
  
      targetNode.querySelectorAll('div[data-channel-to-delete]').forEach(btn => {
        if (btn.dataset._patched) return
        if (btn.textContent.trim() === '🗑️') {
          btn.dataset._patched = 1
          btn.innerHTML = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1.3em" height="1.3em" viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="vertical-align: middle;">
  <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="11" fill="#AB0E0E"/>
  <path fill="#fff" d="M8.818 7.403L12 10.586l3.182-3.182l1.414 1.414L13.414 12l3.182 3.182l-1.415 1.414L12 13.414l-3.182 3.182l-1.415-1.414L10.586 12L7.403 8.818z"/>
</svg>`
        }
      })

      targetNode.querySelectorAll('.tabbed-comments-plugin-channel-control-btn').forEach(btn => {
        if (btn.dataset._patchedArrow) return;
        
        const text = btn.textContent.trim();
        if (text === '➡️') {
          // Switched textContent to innerHTML and used Font Awesome caret
          btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-caret-right"></i>';
          btn.dataset._patchedArrow = 1;
        } else if (text === '⬅️') {
          btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-caret-left"></i>';
          btn.dataset._patchedArrow = 1;
        }
      })
    }
  
    // 3. EXECUTE
    updateButtonHTML()
  
    let isThrottled = false;
      const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
        // If we recently scheduled an update, ignore the new chat spam completely
        if (isThrottled) return;
  
        isThrottled = true;
        
        // Wait 150ms before allowing another UI patch.
        // This drops execution from ~60 frames per second down to ~6 times a second.
        setTimeout(() => {
          requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            updateButtonHTML();
            isThrottled = false;
          });
        }, 150); 
      });
        
        observer.observe(targetNode, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    
      if (!localStorage.getItem("userConsent")) {
      const consentOverlay = document.createElement("div")
      consentOverlay.id = "blur-overlay"
      consentOverlay.innerHTML = `
        <div id="consent-popup">
          <h3 style="text-align:center;color:inherit">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info" style="margin-right:0.3em;"></i>
            <span>Usage Notice</span>
          </h3>
          <span style="color:inherit;font-size:0.9em">
            • This site includes an <b>18+ content filter</b> and may feature mature or sensitive material.<br>
            • <b>Users aged 13-17</b>: Access is limited to safe content only.<br>

            • By using this site, you agree to follow the <b>Site Rules</b>.<br>
          </span><br><br>
          <div id="consent-buttons">
            <button id="consent-btn" style="background:#5b5b5b;color:#d8d4cf;font-size:0.85em">I Agree</button>
            <button id="leave-btn" style="background:#5b5b5b;color:#d8d4cf;font-size:0.85em">Leave</button>
          </div>
        </div>`
      document.body.appendChild(consentOverlay)
  
      document.getElementById("consent-btn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        localStorage.setItem("userConsent", "true")
        consentOverlay.remove()
      })
  
      document.getElementById("leave-btn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        location.href = "https://perchance.org/hub"
      })
    }
  
  })
  </script>

<!-- clickable random banner image -->
<script>
  const bannerImages = [bannerImageList];

  let currentBannerIndex = -1;

  function cycleBannerImage() {
    const banner = document.getElementById('pageTitleBanner');
    if (!banner) return;

    currentBannerIndex = (currentBannerIndex + 1) % bannerImages.length;
    const nextImageUrl = bannerImages[currentBannerIndex];

    const tempImg = new Image();
    tempImg.onload = () => {
      const gradient = "linear-gradient(rgba(0,0,0,0.05), rgba(0,0,0,0.3))"; // 0.25/0.4 ; 0.1/0.3
      banner.style.backgroundImage = `${gradient}, url(${nextImageUrl})`;
      banner.setAttribute("role", "img"); 
      banner.setAttribute("aria-label", "AI Generated Furry Art Showcase - Flux Model");
      banner.title = "Click to see next art piece";
      // --------------------------------------
    };
    tempImg.src = nextImageUrl;
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    // Randomize start
    currentBannerIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * bannerImages.length) - 1;
    cycleBannerImage();
  });
</script>

<script>
  (() => {
    const defaults = settings.defaultCommentOptions;
    const announcementOptions = {};

    for (const key of defaults.getAllKeys) {
      announcementOptions[key] = defaults[key];
    }

    announcementOptions.channel = "announcement";
    window.aifgAnnouncementCommentOptions = announcementOptions;
    update(document.getElementById("chatContainerannouncement"));
  })();
</script>

                <label class="aifg-entry-gate-field-label" for="accUidField">Account Name (Unlock Key)</label>
                <input id="accUidField"
                       type="text"
                       placeholder="Enter a name..."
                       autocomplete="off"
                       oninput="window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation()"
                       class="aifg-account-input">

                  <label class="aifg-entry-gate-field-label" for="accUidConfirmField">Confirm Account Name</label>

                         type="text"
                         placeholder="Re-enter account name..."
                         autocomplete="off"
                         oninput="window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation()"
                         class="aifg-account-input">
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label class="aifg-entry-gate-field-label" for="accSafeCodeField">Delete Code (Pseudo-Password)</label>
                <input id="accSafeCodeField"
                       type="password"
                       placeholder="Set delete code..."
                       autocomplete="off"
                       oninput="window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation()"
                       class="aifg-account-input">

                <div id="accSafeCodeConfirmWrap" class="aifg-entry-gate-confirm-field" hidden>
                  <label class="aifg-entry-gate-field-label" for="accSafeCodeConfirmField">Confirm Delete Code</label>
                  <input id="accSafeCodeConfirmField"
                         type="password"
                         placeholder="Re-enter delete code..."
                         autocomplete="off"
                         oninput="window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation()"
                         class="aifg-account-input">
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div id="entryGateCredentialStatus"
                 class="aifg-entry-gate-credential-status"
                 role="status"
                 aria-live="polite"
                 hidden></div>

            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; margin-top:4px; padding:12px 16px; background:var(--box-color,#1e1e1e); border-radius:8px; border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333);">
              <span style="font-weight:600; font-size:0.9rem;">Require verification on page entry</span>
              <input type="checkbox"
                     id="lockModeToggle"
                     onchange="window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation()"
                     style="width:18px; height:18px; cursor:pointer; accent-color:var(--primary-color,#007bff); flex-shrink:0;">
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="aifg-account-section" data-account-section="data" style="padding:20px;">
        <div class="aifg-account-section-title" style="font-weight:600; font-size:1.05rem; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; margin-bottom:16px;">
          <i class="fa-solid fa-database"></i> Data Management
        </div>

        <div style="background:var(--box-color,#1e1e1e); border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333); border-radius:8px; padding:14px; text-align:left; margin-bottom:12px;">
          <div style="margin-bottom:10px;">
            <strong style="font-size:0.92rem; color:var(--text-color,#ddd); display:block; margin-bottom:4px;">Gallery Layout Backup</strong>
            <span style="font-size:0.8rem; opacity:0.72; line-height:1.35; display:block;">
              Copy or paste your gallery folder arrangement between devices. Layout only — not gallery images.
            </span>
          </div>
        
          <textarea
            id="galleryLayoutTransferBox"
            placeholder="Paste layout text here"
            style="width:100%; min-height:92px; padding:8px; background:var(--background-color,#111); color:var(--text-color,#fff); border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333); border-radius:6px; box-sizing:border-box; font-family:monospace; font-size:11.5px; line-height:1.4; margin-bottom:10px; resize:vertical;"
          ></textarea>
        
          <div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px;">
            <button
              onclick="window.__copyGalleryLayout()"
              style="padding:8px 10px; background:transparent; color:var(--text-color,#ddd); border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333); border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-size:0.82rem; font-weight:600; flex:1 1 110px; transition:0.2s;"
              onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(255,255,255,0.05)'"
              onmouseout="this.style.background='transparent'"
            >Copy Layout</button>
        
            <button
              onclick="window.__importGalleryLayout('replace')"
              style="padding:8px 10px; background:transparent; color:var(--text-color,#ddd); border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333); border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-size:0.82rem; font-weight:600; flex:1 1 110px; transition:0.2s;"
              onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(255,255,255,0.05)'"
              onmouseout="this.style.background='transparent'"
            >Replace</button>
        
            <button
              onclick="window.__importGalleryLayout('merge')"
              style="padding:8px 10px; background:transparent; color:var(--text-color,#ddd); border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333); border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-size:0.82rem; font-weight:600; flex:1 1 110px; transition:0.2s;"
              onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(255,255,255,0.05)'"
              onmouseout="this.style.background='transparent'"
            >Merge</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style="background:var(--box-color,#1e1e1e); border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333); border-radius:8px; padding:16px; text-align:left;">
          <div style="margin-bottom:14px;">
            <strong style="font-size:0.95rem; color:var(--text-color,#ddd);">Wipe Local Storage</strong>
                        
            <div style="font-size:0.85rem; opacity:0.7; margin-top:4px; line-height:1.4;">Deletes all locally stored chats, galleries, and settings. This action cannot be undone.</div>
          </div>
          
          <button id="manualWipeBtn" onclick="window.triggerReset()" style="width:100%; padding:12px; background:#8b2e2e; color:#fff; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-weight:600; font-size:0.95rem; transition:background 0.2s; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:8px;" onmouseover="this.style.background='#a63636'" onmouseout="this.style.background='#8b2e2e'">
            <i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i> Reset Furry AI Generator
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>

    <div style="padding:16px 20px; border-top:1px solid var(--border-color,#333); background:var(--box-color,#1e1e1e); display:flex; justify-content:flex-end; flex-shrink:0;">
      <button id="accountSaveSettingsBtn" onclick="window.saveAccSettings()" style="padding:10px 24px; background:var(--special-color,#007bff); color:#fff; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer; font-weight:600; font-size:0.95rem; transition:opacity 0.2s;" onmouseover="this.style.opacity='0.85'" onmouseout="this.style.opacity='1'">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk" style="margin-right:6px;"></i> Save Settings
      </button>
    </div>

  </div>
</div>
  

<script id="aifg-language-system">
(() => {
  'use strict';

  const LANGUAGE_KEY = 'furai_ui_language_v1';
  const CACHE_KEY = 'aifg_ui_translation_cache_v1';
  const LANGUAGE_CODES = '|en|ru|es|pt|de|fr|pl|zh-TW|zh-CN|it|id|hi|ja|vi|tr|ko|ar|fa|bn|ur|th|nl|sv|da|no|fi|ms|tl|';

  let preference = normalizeLanguage(localStorage.getItem(LANGUAGE_KEY) || 'auto');
  let pendingPreference = null;
  let optionsCache = null;
  let controlReady = false;
  let engine = null;

  function normalizeLanguage(value) {
    value = String(value || '').trim();
    if (!value || value === 'auto') return 'auto';
    return LANGUAGE_CODES.includes('|' + value + '|') ? value : 'en';
  }

  function detectLanguage() {
    const languages = navigator.languages?.length
      ? navigator.languages
      : [navigator.language || 'en'];

    for (const item of languages) {
      const value = String(item || '').toLowerCase();
      if (value.startsWith('zh-tw') || value.startsWith('zh-hk') || value.includes('hant')) return 'zh-TW';
      if (value.startsWith('zh')) return 'zh-CN';

      const base = value.split('-')[0];
      if (base === 'nb' || base === 'nn') return 'no';
      if (base === 'fil') return 'tl';
      if ('|ru|es|pt|de|fr|pl|it|id|hi|ja|vi|tr|ko|ar|fa|bn|ur|th|nl|sv|da|no|fi|ms|tl|en|'.includes('|' + base + '|')) {
        return base;
      }
    }
    return 'en';
  }

  function resolveLanguage(value = preference) {
    const normalized = normalizeLanguage(value);
    return normalized === 'auto' ? detectLanguage() : normalized;
  }

  window.getAifgGuideUrl = () => {
    let selected = preference;
    try {
      selected = normalizeLanguage(localStorage.getItem(LANGUAGE_KEY) || preference);
    } catch (error) {}

    const language = resolveLanguage(selected);
    const url = new URL('https://null.perchance.org/ai-furry-generator-guide');
    url.searchParams.set('lang', language);
    return url.toString();
  };

  function getOptionGroups() {
    return [
      { label: '', items: [
        ['auto', 'Auto (Browser)', '']
      ]},
      { label: 'Americas & Western Europe', items: [
        ['en', 'English', 'us'],
        ['es', 'Español', 'es'],
        ['pt', 'Português', 'br'],
        ['de', 'Deutsch', 'de'],
        ['fr', 'Français', 'fr'],
        ['it', 'Italiano', 'it'],
        ['nl', 'Nederlands', 'nl']
      ]},
      { label: 'Eastern Europe & Eurasia', items: [
        ['ru', 'Русский', 'ru'],
        ['pl', 'Polski', 'pl'],
        ['tr', 'Türkçe', 'tr']
      ]},
      { label: 'Southeast Asia', items: [
        ['id', 'Bahasa Indonesia', 'id'],
        ['vi', 'Tiếng Việt', 'vn'],
        ['th', 'ไทย', 'th'],
        ['ms', 'Bahasa Melayu', 'my'],
        ['tl', 'Filipino', 'ph']
      ]},
      { label: 'South Asia & Middle East', items: [
        ['hi', 'हिन्दी', 'in'],
        ['ar', 'العربية', 'sa'],
        ['fa', 'فارسی', 'ir'],
        ['bn', 'বাংলা', 'bd'],
        ['ur', 'اردو', 'pk']
      ]},
      { label: 'East Asia', items: [
        ['zh-TW', '繁體中文', 'tw'],
        ['zh-CN', '简体中文', 'cn'],
        ['ja', '日本語', 'jp'],
        ['ko', '한국어', 'kr']
      ]},
      { label: 'Northern Europe', items: [
        ['sv', 'Svenska', 'se'],
        ['da', 'Dansk', 'dk'],
        ['no', 'Norsk', 'no'],
        ['fi', 'Suomi', 'fi']
      ]}
    ];
  }

  function getOptions() {
    if (optionsCache) return optionsCache;
    optionsCache = getOptionGroups().flatMap(group => group.items);
    return optionsCache;
  }

  function getOption(value) {
    return getOptions().find(option => option[0] === value) ||
      getOptions().find(option => option[0] === 'en');
  }

  function flagUrl(code) {
    return code ? `https://flagcdn.com/w20/${code}.webp` : '';
  }

  function escapeHtml(value) {
    return String(value || '')
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
  }

  function renderOptions() {
    const menu = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageMenu');
    if (!menu || menu.dataset.ready === '1') return;

    menu.innerHTML = getOptionGroups().map(group => {
      const heading = group.label
        ? `<div class="aifg-language-group" role="presentation" data-aifg-ui-translate>${escapeHtml(group.label)}</div>`
        : '';

      const items = group.items.map(([value, label, flag]) => {
        const icon = flag
          ? `<img class="aifg-language-flag" src="${flagUrl(flag)}" alt="" loading="lazy" decoding="async" referrerpolicy="no-referrer" onerror="this.hidden=true">`
          : '<span class="aifg-language-globe" aria-hidden="true"><i class="fa-solid fa-globe"></i></span>';

        return `<button class="aifg-language-option"
                        type="button"
                        role="option"
                        data-aifg-no-translate
                        data-aifg-language="${escapeHtml(value)}"
                        aria-selected="false"
                        onclick="window.selectAifgUiLanguage('${escapeHtml(value)}')">
                  ${icon}
                  <span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span>
                  <i class="fa-solid fa-check aifg-language-option-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                </button>`;
      }).join('');

      return heading + items;
    }).join('');

    menu.dataset.ready = '1';
    syncControl();
  }

  function syncControl() {
    const input = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageSelect');
    const label = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageLabel');
    const flag = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageFlag');
    const globe = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageGlobe');
    if (!input || !label || !flag || !globe) return;

    const selectedValue = normalizeLanguage(pendingPreference || preference);
    const selected = getOption(selectedValue);
    const detected = getOption(resolveLanguage('auto'));

    input.value = selectedValue;
    label.textContent = selectedValue === 'auto'
      ? `Auto (${detected[1]})`
      : selected[1];

    if (selected[2]) {
      flag.src = flagUrl(selected[2]);
      flag.hidden = false;
      globe.hidden = true;
      flag.onerror = () => {
        flag.hidden = true;
        globe.hidden = false;
      };
    } else {
      flag.hidden = true;
      flag.removeAttribute('src');
      globe.hidden = false;
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('#aifgUiLanguageMenu [data-aifg-language]').forEach(button => {
      const selectedNow = button.dataset.aifgLanguage === selectedValue;
      button.classList.toggle('is-selected', selectedNow);
      button.setAttribute('aria-selected', selectedNow ? 'true' : 'false');
    });

  }

  function closeMenu() {
    const menu = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageMenu');
    const trigger = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageTrigger');
    if (menu) menu.hidden = true;
    if (trigger) trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  }

  window.toggleAifgUiLanguageMenu = event => {
    event?.preventDefault?.();
    event?.stopPropagation?.();

    const menu = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageMenu');
    const trigger = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageTrigger');
    if (!menu || !trigger) return;

    renderOptions();
    const opening = menu.hidden;
    menu.hidden = !opening;
    trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', opening ? 'true' : 'false');
    if (opening) menu.querySelector('.is-selected')?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
  };

  window.selectAifgUiLanguage = value => {
    pendingPreference = normalizeLanguage(value);
    syncControl();
    closeMenu();
  };

  window.prepareAifgUiLanguageControl = () => {
    preference = normalizeLanguage(localStorage.getItem(LANGUAGE_KEY) || 'auto');
    pendingPreference = preference;
    syncControl();

    if (controlReady) return;
    controlReady = true;

    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
      const picker = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguagePicker');
      if (picker && !picker.contains(event.target)) closeMenu();

      const help = document.querySelector('.aifg-language-help');
      if (help?.open && !help.contains(event.target)) help.open = false;
    }, { passive: true });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') closeMenu();
    });
  };

  window.closeAifgUiLanguageControl = () => {
    closeMenu();
    const help = document.querySelector('.aifg-language-help');
    if (help) help.open = false;
  };

  window.commitAifgUiLanguage = () => {
    const selected = normalizeLanguage(
      document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageSelect')?.value || preference
    );
    const changed = selected !== preference;
    localStorage.setItem(LANGUAGE_KEY, selected);
    preference = selected;
    pendingPreference = selected;
    return changed;
  };

  function openLanguageControl(openPicker) {
    // This is an option-level target, not merely a section shortcut:
    // open General Preferences alone, reveal Interface Language, and focus it.
    window.openAccountModal?.('general', 'language');

    if (!openPicker) return;

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const menu = document.getElementById('aifgUiLanguageMenu');
        if (menu?.hidden) window.toggleAifgUiLanguageMenu?.();
      });
    });
  }

  window.openAifgLanguageSettings = () => openLanguageControl(false);
  window.openAifgLanguagePortal = event => {
    event?.preventDefault?.();
    event?.stopPropagation?.();
    openLanguageControl(true);
  };

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', syncControl, { once:true });

  function shouldTranslate(value, maxLength = 360, maxWords = 60) {
    const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (text.length < 2 || text.length > maxLength || text.split(/\s+/).length > maxWords) return false;
    if (!/[A-Za-z]/.test(text)) return false;
    if (/^(AI Furry Generator|FurAI|FLUX(?:\.1)?)$/i.test(text)) return false;
    if (/https?:\/\/|www\.|^[\w.+-]+@[\w.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$/.test(text)) return false;
    return true;
  }

  function initTranslation() {
    const language = resolveLanguage();
    document.documentElement.lang = language;

    // English/default users stop here. No translation cache, queue, observer,
    // scan, network request, or translation event listener is created.
    if (language === 'en') return;

    const ROOT_SELECTOR = [
      '#pageTitleEl48759084793', '#userInputsCtn8462746262', '#outputAreaEl > span',
      '#shareLinkOuterCtn2893827', '#generateButtonEl', '#leftClickHelperEl',
      '#rightClickHelperEl', '#bottomClickHelperEl', '#showSocialsButtonCtn9000274628',
      '#floatingBtnHint', '#floatingBtn', '#menuCtn', '#backToTopBtn',
      '#accountSettingsOverlay', '#privacyLockOverlay', '#blur-overlay',
      '#eighteenConsentOverlay', '#rules18PlusOverlay', '#bannerStack',
      '#guidePopup', '#banlistPopup', '#eventsLogsPopup', '#submissionPopup',
      '.vs-modal-header', '.vs-grid-pane.active', '.pre-actions-overlay',
      '.pre-actions-card', '.popup-actions', '[id$="Overlay"]', '[id$="Popup"]',
      '[role="dialog"]', '.modal', '[data-aifg-ui-translate]'
    ].join(',');

    const FORCE_SELECTOR = '[data-aifg-ui-translate]';
    const HARD_NO_TRANSLATE = [
      '[data-aifg-no-translate]', '[contenteditable="true"]',
      'pre', 'code', 'script', 'style', 'noscript', 'svg', 'iframe',
      '.material-icons', '.material-symbols-outlined', 'i[class*="fa-"]'
    ].join(',');

    const NO_TRANSLATE = [
      '#commentsPluginCtn654732', '#chatContainerannouncement',
      '#customAppsWrapper', '#customAppBtnsSlot', '#linkAutoSaveUsersList',
      '#linkHiddenUsersList', '#outputAreaEl .t2i-image-ctn', '.t2i-image-ctn',
      '.imageCtn', '.image-ctn', '.generated-image-card',
      '#localMediaGallery_root [data-local-media-gallery-item]',
      '.localMediaGallery-folder-row', '.localMediaGallery-item-menu-header',
      '[data-gallery-item]', '[data-comment-id]', '[data-channel-to-delete]',
      '.tabbed-comments-plugin-comment', '.tabbed-comments-comment',
      '.tabbed-comments-plugin-message', '.character-name-output',
      '.character-description-output', '.visual-select-btn',
      '[id^="customAppNameDisplay"]', '[data-user-content]', '[data-aifg-user-content]'
    ].join(',');

    engine = {
      language,
      cache: Object.create(null),
      cacheTimer: 0,
      addedTimer: 0,
      wakeTimer: 0,
      priorityUntil: 0,
      addedNodes: new Set(),
      settledTimers: new Map(),
      observedRoots: new WeakSet(),
      observedSurfaces: [],
      visibilityState: new WeakMap(),
      textState: new WeakMap(),
      attrState: new WeakMap(),
      pending: new Map(),
      retries: Object.create(null),
      queue: [],
      active: 0,
      failures: 0,
      pausedUntil: 0,
      provider: 'google',
      observer: null,
      surfaceObserver: null
    };

    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY) || 'null');
      if (saved?.language === language && saved.entries) {
        Object.assign(engine.cache, saved.entries);
      }
    } catch (error) {}

    const isVisible = element => !!element && element.isConnected &&
      !!(element.offsetWidth || element.offsetHeight || element.getClientRects().length);

    const isExcluded = node => {
      const element = node?.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE ? node : node?.parentElement;
      if (!element) return true;
      const hardBoundary = element.closest(HARD_NO_TRANSLATE);
      const forceBoundary = element.closest(FORCE_SELECTOR);
      if (hardBoundary && (!forceBoundary || forceBoundary.contains(hardBoundary))) return true;
      if (forceBoundary) return false;
      return !!element.closest(NO_TRANSLATE);
    };

    const saveCacheSoon = () => {
      clearTimeout(engine.cacheTimer);
      engine.cacheTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        try {
          const keys = Object.keys(engine.cache);
          if (keys.length > 700) {
            keys.slice(0, keys.length - 600).forEach(key => delete engine.cache[key]);
          }
          localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
            language: engine.language,
            entries: engine.cache
          }));
        } catch (error) {}
      }, 500);
    };

    const fetchJson = async (url, timeoutMs) => {
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
      try {
        const response = await fetch(url, {
          signal: controller.signal,
          credentials: 'omit',
          referrerPolicy: 'no-referrer',
          cache: 'force-cache'
        });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Translation unavailable');
        return await response.json();
      } finally {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
      }
    };

    const fetchTranslation = async source => {
      if (engine.provider === 'google') {
        try {
          const data = await fetchJson(
            'https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single?client=gtx&sl=en&tl=' +
            encodeURIComponent(engine.language) + '&dt=t&q=' + encodeURIComponent(source),
            4500
          );
          const translated = Array.isArray(data?.[0])
            ? data[0].map(part => part?.[0] || '').join('').trim()
            : '';
          if (translated) return translated;
          engine.provider = 'mymemory';
        } catch (error) {
          engine.provider = 'mymemory';
        }
      }

      const fallback = await fetchJson(
        'https://api.mymemory.translated.net/get?q=' + encodeURIComponent(source) +
        '&langpair=' + encodeURIComponent('en|' + engine.language),
        6000
      );
      const translated = String(fallback?.responseData?.translatedText || '').trim();
      if (!translated || /^MYMEMORY WARNING/i.test(translated)) {
        throw new Error('Translation unavailable');
      }
      return translated;
    };

    const wakeQueueAfterPause = () => {
      if (engine.wakeTimer) return;
      const wait = Math.max(30, engine.pausedUntil - Date.now() + 30);
      engine.wakeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        engine.wakeTimer = 0;
        engine.provider = 'google';
        // Pending callbacks and the retained queue resume directly. A whole-page
        // rescan here would duplicate work after a temporary provider pause.
        runQueue();
      }, wait);
    };

    const runQueue = () => {
      if (Date.now() < engine.pausedUntil) {
        wakeQueueAfterPause();
        return;
      }

      while (engine.active < 2 && engine.queue.length) {
        const source = engine.queue.shift();
        const callbacks = engine.pending.get(source) || [];
        let keepPending = false;
        engine.active += 1;

        fetchTranslation(source)
          .then(translated => {
            if (!translated) return;
            engine.failures = 0;
            delete engine.retries[source];
            engine.cache[source] = translated;
            saveCacheSoon();
            callbacks.forEach(apply => apply(translated));
          })
          .catch(() => {
            engine.failures += 1;
            const retryCount = (engine.retries[source] || 0) + 1;
            engine.retries[source] = retryCount;

            // Retry each phrase once. If the provider is temporarily throttled,
            // retain the rest of the queue instead of throwing unfinished UI away.
            if (retryCount <= 1 && callbacks.length) {
              keepPending = true;
              engine.queue.push(source);
            } else {
              delete engine.retries[source];
            }

            if (engine.failures >= 6) {
              engine.failures = 0;
              engine.pausedUntil = Date.now() + 15 * 1000;
              wakeQueueAfterPause();
            }
          })
          .finally(() => {
            if (!keepPending) engine.pending.delete(source);
            engine.active -= 1;
            runQueue();
          });
      }
    };

    const requestText = (source, apply) => {
      if (engine.cache[source]) {
        apply(engine.cache[source]);
        return;
      }
      if (engine.pending.has(source)) {
        engine.pending.get(source).push(apply);
        return;
      }

      engine.pending.set(source, [apply]);
      if (Date.now() < engine.priorityUntil) {
        engine.queue.unshift(source);
      } else {
        engine.queue.push(source);
      }

      if (Date.now() < engine.pausedUntil) {
        wakeQueueAfterPause();
      } else {
        runQueue();
      }
    };

    const translateTextNode = node => {
      if (!node?.isConnected || node.parentElement?.closest('input, textarea') || isExcluded(node)) return;

      const raw = String(node.nodeValue || '');
      const match = raw.match(/^(\s*)([\s\S]*?)(\s*)$/);
      const current = match?.[2] || raw;
      let state = engine.textState.get(node);

      if (state && current !== state.source && current !== state.translated) state = null;
      const source = state?.source || current;
      const forced = !!node.parentElement?.closest(FORCE_SELECTOR);
      if (!shouldTranslate(source, forced ? 700 : 360, forced ? 120 : 60)) return;

      state ||= {
        source,
        translated: '',
        before: match?.[1] || '',
        after: match?.[3] || ''
      };
      engine.textState.set(node, state);

      requestText(source, translated => {
        const latest = engine.textState.get(node);
        if (!latest || latest.source !== source || !node.isConnected) return;
        latest.translated = translated;
        node.nodeValue = latest.before + translated + latest.after;
      });
    };

    const translateAttribute = (element, name) => {
      if (!element?.isConnected || isExcluded(element)) return;

      const current = String(element.getAttribute(name) || '').trim();
      if (!shouldTranslate(current)) return;

      let states = engine.attrState.get(element);
      if (!states) engine.attrState.set(element, states = Object.create(null));

      let state = states[name];
      if (!state || (current !== state.source && current !== state.translated)) {
        state = states[name] = { source: current, translated: '' };
      }

      requestText(state.source, translated => {
        const latest = engine.attrState.get(element)?.[name];
        if (!latest || latest.source !== state.source || !element.isConnected) return;
        latest.translated = translated;
        element.setAttribute(name, translated);
      });
    };

    const addVisibleBodyChildren = roots => {
      if (!document.body) return;
      Array.from(document.body.children).forEach(element => {
        if (/^(SCRIPT|STYLE|LINK|META|NOSCRIPT)$/i.test(element.tagName)) return;
        if (isVisible(element) && !isExcluded(element)) roots.add(element);
      });
    };

    const compactRoots = roots => {
      const list = Array.from(roots).filter(root => root?.isConnected);
      return list.filter(root => !list.some(other => other !== root && other.contains?.(root)));
    };

    const getContainers = (root = document) => {
      const roots = new Set();

      if (root === document) {
        addVisibleBodyChildren(roots);
        document.querySelectorAll(ROOT_SELECTOR).forEach(element => {
          if (isVisible(element)) roots.add(element);
        });
        return compactRoots(roots);
      }

      if (!root?.isConnected) return [];
      if (root.matches?.(FORCE_SELECTOR) || root.matches?.(ROOT_SELECTOR)) roots.add(root);

      const surface = root.closest?.('[role="dialog"], .modal, .pre-actions-card, #menuCtn, #bannerStack, body > *');
      if (surface) roots.add(surface);

      root.querySelectorAll?.(ROOT_SELECTOR).forEach(element => {
        if (isVisible(element)) roots.add(element);
      });

      return compactRoots(roots);
    };

    const scanRoot = (root = document) => {
      getContainers(root).forEach(container => {
        if (!isVisible(container) || isExcluded(container)) return;

        const walker = document.createTreeWalker(container, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
        let node;
        while ((node = walker.nextNode())) translateTextNode(node);

        container.querySelectorAll('[placeholder],[title],[aria-label]').forEach(element => {
          ['placeholder', 'title', 'aria-label'].forEach(name => {
            if (element.hasAttribute(name)) translateAttribute(element, name);
          });
        });
      });
    };

    const VISIBILITY_SURFACE_SELECTOR = [
      '[role="dialog"]', '.modal', '.pre-actions-overlay', '.pre-actions-card',
      '#menuCtn', '#bannerStack', '[id$="Overlay"]', '[id$="Popup"]',
      'details', '[data-aifg-ui-translate]'
    ].join(',');

    const INTERACTION_SURFACE_SELECTOR = [
      '[role="dialog"]', '.modal', '.pre-actions-overlay', '.pre-actions-card',
      '#menuCtn', '#bannerStack', '#accountSettingsOverlay', '#privacyLockOverlay',
      '#guidePopup', '#banlistPopup', '#eventsLogsPopup', '#submissionPopup',
      '[id$="Overlay"]', '[id$="Popup"]', 'details'
    ].join(',');

    // Exact-subtree scan for newly inserted UI. Unlike scanRoot(), this does not
    // promote a small mutation into a scan of the entire surrounding modal.
    const scanExactElement = element => {
      if (!element?.isConnected || !isVisible(element) || isExcluded(element)) return;

      const walker = document.createTreeWalker(element, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
      let node;
      while ((node = walker.nextNode())) translateTextNode(node);

      const translateOwnAttributes = target => {
        ['placeholder', 'title', 'aria-label'].forEach(name => {
          if (target.hasAttribute?.(name)) translateAttribute(target, name);
        });
      };

      translateOwnAttributes(element);
      element.querySelectorAll?.('[placeholder],[title],[aria-label]').forEach(translateOwnAttributes);
    };

    const scanExactNode = node => {
      if (!node?.isConnected) return;
      if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
        const parent = node.parentElement;
        if (parent && isVisible(parent) && !isExcluded(node)) translateTextNode(node);
        return;
      }
      if (node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) scanExactElement(node);
    };

    const scheduleAddedNode = node => {
      if (!node) return;
      engine.addedNodes.add(node);
      if (engine.addedTimer) return;

      engine.addedTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        engine.addedTimer = 0;
        const nodes = Array.from(engine.addedNodes);
        engine.addedNodes.clear();

        // Remove descendants when their ancestor is already in the batch.
        const compact = nodes.filter(node => !nodes.some(other =>
          other !== node && other.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE && other.contains?.(node)
        ));
        compact.forEach(scanExactNode);
      }, 35);
    };

    const scheduleSettledScan = (root, delay = 260) => {
      if (!root?.isConnected) return;
      const existing = engine.settledTimers.get(root);
      if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);

      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        engine.settledTimers.delete(root);
        if (isVisible(root) && !isExcluded(root)) scheduleAddedNode(root);
      }, delay);
      engine.settledTimers.set(root, timer);
    };

    const markSurfaceVisible = root => {
      if (!root?.isConnected || !isVisible(root) || isExcluded(root)) return;
      engine.priorityUntil = Date.now() + 900;
      scheduleAddedNode(root);

      // One finite targeted late pass catches async labels added after an opening
      // animation. It never escalates to a document-wide scan.
      scheduleSettledScan(root);
    };

    const SURFACE_OBSERVER_OPTIONS = {
      subtree: true,
      characterData: true,
      attributes: true,
      attributeFilter: ['class', 'style', 'hidden', 'open', 'aria-hidden']
    };

    const rebuildLiveSurfaceObservers = () => {
      const live = compactRoots(new Set(
        engine.observedSurfaces.filter(item => item?.isConnected)
      ));

      engine.surfaceObserver.disconnect();
      engine.observedRoots = new WeakSet();
      engine.observedSurfaces = live;

      live.forEach(item => {
        engine.observedRoots.add(item);
        if (!engine.visibilityState.has(item)) {
          engine.visibilityState.set(item, isVisible(item));
        }
        engine.surfaceObserver.observe(item, SURFACE_OBSERVER_OPTIONS);
      });
    };

    const observeUiRoot = root => {
      if (!root?.isConnected || engine.observedRoots.has(root)) return;

      // Clean disconnected dynamic popups before registering another surface,
      // so repeated open/remove cycles cannot accumulate observed dead nodes.
      if (engine.observedSurfaces.some(item => !item?.isConnected)) {
        rebuildLiveSurfaceObservers();
      }

      // A newly discovered nested surface is already covered by an observed
      // ancestor's subtree. Avoid overlapping MutationObserver coverage.
      if (engine.observedSurfaces.some(item => item.contains?.(root))) return;

      engine.observedRoots.add(root);
      engine.observedSurfaces.push(root);
      engine.visibilityState.set(root, isVisible(root));
      engine.surfaceObserver.observe(root, SURFACE_OBSERVER_OPTIONS);
    };

    const observeUiRootsIn = (scope = document) => {
      const roots = new Set();
      if (scope?.matches?.(ROOT_SELECTOR)) roots.add(scope);
      scope?.querySelectorAll?.(ROOT_SELECTOR).forEach(root => roots.add(root));
      compactRoots(roots).forEach(observeUiRoot);
    };

    // This observer adds only the two capabilities missing from the older build:
    // exact existing-text updates and real hidden->visible UI transitions.
    // Child insertion remains owned by the single document child-list observer.
    engine.surfaceObserver = new MutationObserver(mutations => {
      mutations.forEach(mutation => {
        if (mutation.type === 'characterData') {
          const node = mutation.target;
          const state = engine.textState.get(node);
          const current = String(node.nodeValue || '').trim();

          // Ignore the mutation generated by applying our own cached translation.
          if (state?.translated && current === String(state.translated).trim()) return;
          if (node.parentElement && isVisible(node.parentElement) && !isExcluded(node)) {
            translateTextNode(node);
          }
          return;
        }

        const target = mutation.target;
        if (!target?.isConnected || isExcluded(target)) return;

        // Ignore class/style churn on ordinary descendants (icons, sliders,
        // animations). Only recognized reusable UI surfaces can trigger a scan.
        if (!target.matches?.(VISIBILITY_SURFACE_SELECTOR)) return;

        const wasVisible = engine.visibilityState.get(target);
        const nowVisible = isVisible(target);
        engine.visibilityState.set(target, nowVisible);

        const explicitlyOpened =
          (mutation.attributeName === 'open' && !!target.open) ||
          (mutation.attributeName === 'hidden' && !target.hidden) ||
          (mutation.attributeName === 'aria-hidden' && target.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true');

        // Class/style animation churn does nothing unless visibility truly changed.
        if (nowVisible && (wasVisible === false || wasVisible === undefined || explicitlyOpened)) {
          markSurfaceVisible(target);
        }
      });
    });

    observeUiRootsIn(document);

    const intro = document.querySelector('#outputAreaEl > span');
    if (intro) intro.setAttribute('data-aifg-ui-translate', '');

    // Visible UI translates immediately. Cached phrases apply synchronously.
    scanRoot(document);

    // One general child-list observer remains more eager than the old version,
    // but scans only the inserted subtree and registers any new reusable panels.
    engine.observer = new MutationObserver(mutations => {
      mutations.forEach(mutation => {
        mutation.addedNodes.forEach(node => {
          const element = node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE ? node : node.parentElement;
          if (!element || isExcluded(element)) return;

          observeUiRootsIn(element);
          scheduleAddedNode(node);
        });
      });
    });
    engine.observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

    const rescanAfterInteraction = event => {
      const root = event.target?.closest?.(INTERACTION_SURFACE_SELECTOR);
      if (root && isVisible(root) && !isExcluded(root)) scheduleSettledScan(root);
    };

    // These are targeted fallback signals, not broad page rescans. They help with
    // legacy controls that update a reused panel asynchronously without adding a node.
    document.addEventListener('click', rescanAfterInteraction, { passive: true });
    document.addEventListener('change', rescanAfterInteraction, true);

    document.addEventListener('toggle', event => {
      if (!event.target?.open) return;
      markSurfaceVisible(event.target);
    }, true);
  }

  function init() {
    initTranslation();
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init, { once: true });
  } else {
    init();
  }
})();
</script>

</style>
<script>
(function initAifgIframeLoader() {
  if (window.aifgLoadIframe) return;

  const states = new WeakMap();
  const containerObservers = new WeakMap();
  let generatedFrameId = 0;

  const resolveElement = value => {
    if (!value) return null;
    if (typeof value === 'string') return document.getElementById(value);
    return value;
  };

  const normalizeUrl = value => {
    const raw = String(value || '').trim();
    if (!raw) return '';
    try {
      return new URL(raw, window.location.href).href;
    } catch (error) {
      return raw;
    }
  };

  const isBlankFrame = frame => {
    const raw = String(frame?.getAttribute?.('src') || frame?.src || '').trim();
    return !raw || raw === 'about:blank' || raw.startsWith('about:blank#');
  };

  const getDefaultReadyDelayMs = src => {
    const normalized = normalizeUrl(src);
    if (!normalized) return 0;

    try {
      const url = new URL(normalized);
      const hostname = url.hostname.toLowerCase();
      const isHuggingFaceSpace =
        hostname === 'hf.space' ||
        hostname.endsWith('.hf.space') ||
        (hostname === 'huggingface.co' && url.pathname.toLowerCase().startsWith('/spaces/'));
      const isTldraw = hostname === 'tldraw.com' || hostname.endsWith('.tldraw.com');

      return isHuggingFaceSpace || isTldraw ? 800 : 0;
    } catch (error) {
      return 0;
    }
  };

  const resolveReadyDelayMs = (options, src) => {
    const requested = Number(options?.readyDelayMs);
    if (Number.isFinite(requested) && requested >= 0) return Math.min(requested, 5000);
    return getDefaultReadyDelayMs(src);
  };

  const clearReadyTimer = state => {
    if (!state) return;

    if (state.readyTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.readyTimer);
      state.readyTimer = null;
    }

    if (state.readyFallbackTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.readyFallbackTimer);
      state.readyFallbackTimer = null;
    }

    if (state.readyRaf) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(state.readyRaf);
      state.readyRaf = 0;
    }
  };

  const ensureFrameId = frame => {
    if (frame.id) return frame.id;
    generatedFrameId += 1;
    frame.id = `aifgManagedIframe${generatedFrameId}`;
    return frame.id;
  };

  const getState = frame => {
    let state = states.get(frame);
    if (!state) {
      state = {
        bound: false,
        token: 0,
        expectedSrc: '',
        readySrc: '',
        label: 'Loading…',
        mask: null,
        host: null,
        fullHost: false,
        reloading: false,
        readyDelayMs: 0,
        readyTimer: null,
        readyFallbackTimer: null,
        readyRaf: 0,
        hideTimer: null
      };
      states.set(frame, state);
    }
    return state;
  };

  const ensureHostPosition = host => {
    if (!host) return;
    if (getComputedStyle(host).position === 'static') {
      host.classList.add('aifg-iframe-loader-host');
    }
  };

  const setMaskGeometry = (frame, mask, host, fullHost) => {
    if (!mask || !host) return;

    if (fullHost || !frame || frame.parentElement !== host) {
      mask.style.inset = '0';
      mask.style.removeProperty('width');
      mask.style.removeProperty('height');
      return;
    }

    mask.style.removeProperty('inset');
    const left = Math.max(0, frame.offsetLeft || 0);
    const top = Math.max(0, frame.offsetTop || 0);
    const right = Math.max(0, host.clientWidth - left - frame.offsetWidth);
    const bottom = Math.max(0, host.clientHeight - top - frame.offsetHeight);

    mask.style.left = `${left}px`;
    mask.style.top = `${top}px`;
    mask.style.right = `${right}px`;
    mask.style.bottom = `${bottom}px`;
  };

  const ensureContainerLoaderMask = (container, label = 'Loading…') => {
    if (!container) return null;

    ensureHostPosition(container);
    let mask = container.querySelector(':scope > .aifg-iframe-loader-mask[data-aifg-container-loader="true"]');

    if (!mask) {
      mask = document.createElement('div');
      mask.className = 'aifg-iframe-loader-mask';
      mask.dataset.aifgContainerLoader = 'true';
      mask.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      mask.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      mask.innerHTML = `
        <div class="aifg-gallery-transition-content">
          <span class="aifg-iframe-loader-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span>
          <span class="aifg-iframe-loader-label"></span>
        </div>
      `;
      container.appendChild(mask);
    } else if (!mask.querySelector(':scope > .aifg-gallery-transition-content')) {
      const spinner = mask.querySelector('.aifg-iframe-loader-spinner');
      const labelEl = mask.querySelector('.aifg-iframe-loader-label');
      mask.innerHTML = '';
      const content = document.createElement('div');
      content.className = 'aifg-gallery-transition-content';
      if (spinner) content.appendChild(spinner);
      if (labelEl) content.appendChild(labelEl);
      mask.appendChild(content);
    }

    mask.hidden = false;
    mask.style.inset = '0';
    mask.style.removeProperty('width');
    mask.style.removeProperty('height');
    mask.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
    mask.style.opacity = '1';
    mask.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';

    const labelEl = mask.querySelector('.aifg-iframe-loader-label');
    if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = String(label || 'Loading…');
    mask.setAttribute('aria-label', String(label || 'Loading…'));
    return mask;
  };

  const isMainGalleryLoader = (frame, host) =>
    !!frame?.classList?.contains('text-to-image-plugin-gallery') &&
    host?.id === 'imageGalleryLoaderHost8569284';

  const ensureMask = (frame, options = {}) => {
    const state = getState(frame);
    const host = resolveElement(options.host) || state.host || frame.parentElement;
    if (!host) return null;

    ensureHostPosition(host);
    state.host = host;

    const label = String(options.label || state.label || 'Loading…');
    state.label = label;

    // The main Gallery always owns one canonical host-level mask. This is reused
    // by startup, folder/search reloads, first open, and resume after iframe deload.
    // It prevents the spinner from jumping between sticky and full-host-centered positions.
    if (isMainGalleryLoader(frame, host)) {
      const mask = ensureContainerLoaderMask(host, label);
      state.mask = mask;
      state.fullHost = true;
      return mask;
    }

    state.fullHost = options.fullHost === true || state.fullHost === true;

    const frameId = ensureFrameId(frame);
    let mask = state.mask;

    if (!mask || !mask.isConnected || mask.parentElement !== host) {
      mask = host.querySelector(`.aifg-iframe-loader-mask[data-aifg-loader-for="${frameId}"]`);
    }

    if (!mask) {
      mask = document.createElement('div');
      mask.className = 'aifg-iframe-loader-mask';
      mask.dataset.aifgLoaderFor = frameId;
      mask.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      mask.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      mask.innerHTML = `
        <span class="aifg-iframe-loader-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span>
        <span class="aifg-iframe-loader-label"></span>
      `;
      host.appendChild(mask);
    }

    state.mask = mask;
    const labelEl = mask.querySelector('.aifg-iframe-loader-label');
    if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = label;
    mask.setAttribute('aria-label', label);

    setMaskGeometry(frame, mask, host, state.fullHost);
    mask.hidden = false;
    mask.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
    mask.style.opacity = '1';
    mask.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
    return mask;
  };

  const hideMask = (frame, immediate = false) => {
    const state = states.get(frame);
    const mask = state?.mask;
    if (!mask) return;

    if (state.hideTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.hideTimer);
      state.hideTimer = null;
    }

    if (immediate) {
      mask.hidden = true;
      mask.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
      mask.style.removeProperty('opacity');
      mask.style.removeProperty('pointer-events');
      return;
    }

    mask.classList.add('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
    const finish = () => {
      if (!mask.isConnected) return;
      mask.hidden = true;
      mask.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
      mask.style.removeProperty('opacity');
      mask.style.removeProperty('pointer-events');
      if (state) state.hideTimer = null;
    };

    mask.addEventListener('transitionend', finish, { once: true });
    state.hideTimer = setTimeout(finish, 220);
  };

  const markLoading = (frame, options = {}) => {
    const state = getState(frame);
    state.label = String(options.label || state.label || 'Loading…');
    state.host = resolveElement(options.host) || state.host || frame.parentElement;
    state.fullHost = options.fullHost === true;

    frame.classList.add('aifg-managed-iframe', 'aifg-iframe-loading');
    frame.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-ready');
    frame.dataset.aifgIframeReady = '0';
    ensureMask(frame, options);
  };

  const markReady = (frame, token) => {
    const state = states.get(frame);
    if (!state || state.token !== token || state.reloading || isBlankFrame(frame)) return;

    clearReadyTimer(state);
    state.readySrc = state.expectedSrc || normalizeUrl(frame.getAttribute('src'));
    frame.dataset.aifgReadySrc = state.readySrc;
    frame.dataset.aifgIframeReady = '1';
    frame.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loading');
    frame.classList.add('aifg-iframe-ready');
    hideMask(frame, false);

    frame.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('aifg:iframe-ready', {
      detail: { src: state.readySrc }
    }));
  };

  const bindLoadEvent = frame => {
    const state = getState(frame);
    if (state.bound) return;
    state.bound = true;

    frame.addEventListener('load', () => {
      const currentState = states.get(frame);
      if (!currentState) return;

      if (isBlankFrame(frame)) {
        if (!currentState.reloading) {
          frame.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loading', 'aifg-iframe-ready');
          hideMask(frame, true);
        }
        return;
      }

      const currentSrc = normalizeUrl(frame.getAttribute('src'));
      if (currentState.expectedSrc && currentSrc !== currentState.expectedSrc) return;

      const token = currentState.token;
      const gallerySettleDelay = frame.classList.contains('text-to-image-plugin-gallery') ? 250 : 0;
      const settleDelay = Math.max(currentState.readyDelayMs, gallerySettleDelay);

      const finishReady = () => {
        const liveState = states.get(frame);
        if (!liveState || liveState.token !== token) return;

        liveState.readyTimer = null;
        let finished = false;

        const commitReady = () => {
          if (finished) return;
          finished = true;

          const latestState = states.get(frame);
          if (!latestState || latestState.token !== token) return;

          if (latestState.readyFallbackTimer) {
            clearTimeout(latestState.readyFallbackTimer);
            latestState.readyFallbackTimer = null;
          }

          if (latestState.readyRaf) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(latestState.readyRaf);
            latestState.readyRaf = 0;
          }

          markReady(frame, token);
        };

        // Hidden/throttled tabs may not run requestAnimationFrame promptly.
        // The timer fallback guarantees that Gallery can still become ready.
        liveState.readyFallbackTimer = setTimeout(commitReady, 160);
        liveState.readyRaf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          const nextState = states.get(frame);
          if (!nextState || nextState.token !== token) return;

          nextState.readyRaf = requestAnimationFrame(commitReady);
        });
      };

      clearReadyTimer(currentState);
      currentState.readyTimer = setTimeout(finishReady, settleDelay);
    });
  };

  const isLoaderDisabledFor = element =>
    !!element?.closest?.('[data-aifg-no-loader]');

  const clearLoaderStateFor = frame => {
    const state = states.get(frame);
    clearReadyTimer(state);
    if (state?.hideTimer) clearTimeout(state.hideTimer);
    if (state?.mask?.isConnected) state.mask.remove();
    states.delete(frame);
    frame.classList.remove('aifg-managed-iframe', 'aifg-iframe-loading', 'aifg-iframe-ready');
    delete frame.dataset.aifgReadySrc;
    delete frame.dataset.aifgIframeReady;
  };

  window.aifgPrepareIframe = function(frameOrId, options = {}) {
    const frame = resolveElement(frameOrId);
    if (!frame || frame.tagName !== 'IFRAME') return null;
    if (isLoaderDisabledFor(frame)) {
      clearLoaderStateFor(frame);
      return frame;
    }

    bindLoadEvent(frame);
    const state = getState(frame);
    const currentSrc = normalizeUrl(frame.getAttribute('src'));
    const readySrc = frame.dataset.aifgReadySrc || state.readySrc;

    clearReadyTimer(state);
    state.token += 1;
    state.expectedSrc = currentSrc;
    state.reloading = false;
    state.readyDelayMs = resolveReadyDelayMs(options, currentSrc);
    state.host = resolveElement(options.host) || frame.parentElement;
    state.fullHost = options.fullHost === true;
    state.label = String(options.label || state.label || 'Loading…');

    if (isBlankFrame(frame)) {
      frame.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loading', 'aifg-iframe-ready');
      hideMask(frame, true);
      return frame;
    }

    if (frame.dataset.aifgIframeReady === '1' && readySrc === currentSrc) {
      frame.classList.add('aifg-managed-iframe', 'aifg-iframe-ready');
      frame.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loading');
      hideMask(frame, true);
      return frame;
    }

    markLoading(frame, options);
    return frame;
  };

  window.aifgLoadIframe = function(frameOrId, src, options = {}) {
    const frame = resolveElement(frameOrId);
    if (!frame || frame.tagName !== 'IFRAME' || !src) return null;
    if (isLoaderDisabledFor(frame)) {
      clearLoaderStateFor(frame);
      if (options.forceReload || normalizeUrl(frame.getAttribute('src')) !== normalizeUrl(src)) {
        frame.setAttribute('src', src);
      }
      return frame;
    }

    bindLoadEvent(frame);
    const state = getState(frame);
    const expectedSrc = normalizeUrl(src);
    const currentSrc = normalizeUrl(frame.getAttribute('src'));
    const readySrc = frame.dataset.aifgReadySrc || state.readySrc;

    clearReadyTimer(state);
    state.host = resolveElement(options.host) || frame.parentElement;
    state.fullHost = options.fullHost === true;
    state.readyDelayMs = resolveReadyDelayMs(options, expectedSrc);
    state.label = String(options.label || state.label || 'Loading…');

    if (!options.forceReload && currentSrc === expectedSrc && readySrc === expectedSrc) {
      frame.classList.add('aifg-managed-iframe', 'aifg-iframe-ready');
      frame.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loading');
      frame.dataset.aifgIframeReady = '1';
      hideMask(frame, true);
      return frame;
    }

    state.token += 1;
    state.expectedSrc = expectedSrc;
    state.readySrc = '';
    state.reloading = false;
    delete frame.dataset.aifgReadySrc;
    markLoading(frame, options);
    frame.setAttribute('src', src);
    return frame;
  };

  window.aifgReloadIframe = function(frameOrId, src, options = {}) {
    return window.aifgLoadIframe(frameOrId, src, {
      ...options,
      forceReload: true
    });
  };

  window.aifgUnloadIframe = function(frameOrId, options = {}) {
    const frame = resolveElement(frameOrId);
    if (!frame || frame.tagName !== 'IFRAME') return null;

    const state = getState(frame);
    clearReadyTimer(state);
    state.token += 1;
    state.expectedSrc = '';
    state.readySrc = '';
    state.readyDelayMs = 0;
    state.reloading = false;
    delete frame.dataset.aifgReadySrc;
    delete frame.dataset.aifgIframeReady;
    frame.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loading', 'aifg-iframe-ready');
    hideMask(frame, true);

    if (options.replace === true && frame.parentNode) {
      const replacement = frame.cloneNode(false);
      replacement.classList.remove('aifg-managed-iframe', 'aifg-iframe-loading', 'aifg-iframe-ready');
      replacement.style.removeProperty('opacity');
      replacement.style.removeProperty('transition');
      replacement.style.removeProperty('pointer-events');
      replacement.removeAttribute('data-aifg-ready-src');
      replacement.removeAttribute('data-aifg-iframe-ready');
      replacement.setAttribute('src', 'about:blank');
      frame.parentNode.replaceChild(replacement, frame);
      states.delete(frame);
      return replacement;
    }

    frame.setAttribute('src', 'about:blank');
    return frame;
  };

  window.aifgRemoveIframe = function(frameOrId) {
    const frame = resolveElement(frameOrId);
    if (!frame || frame.tagName !== 'IFRAME') return;
    const state = states.get(frame);
    if (state?.hideTimer) clearTimeout(state.hideTimer);
    if (state?.mask?.isConnected) state.mask.remove();
    states.delete(frame);
    frame.remove();
  };

  window.aifgHideIframeMasks = function(root = document) {
    root.querySelectorAll?.('.aifg-iframe-loader-mask').forEach(mask => {
      mask.hidden = true;
      mask.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
      mask.style.removeProperty('opacity');
      mask.style.removeProperty('pointer-events');
    });
  };

  window.aifgAttachFirstIframeLoader = function(containerOrId, label = 'Loading…') {
    const container = resolveElement(containerOrId);
    if (!container) return null;

    if (isLoaderDisabledFor(container)) {
      container.querySelectorAll?.('.aifg-iframe-loader-mask').forEach(mask => mask.remove());
      return null;
    }

    const previousObserver = containerObservers.get(container);
    if (previousObserver) {
      previousObserver.disconnect();
      containerObservers.delete(container);
    }

    const placeholder = ensureContainerLoaderMask(container, label);
    if (!placeholder) return null;

    const attach = iframe => {
      if (!iframe) return false;
      const state = getState(iframe);
      state.mask = placeholder;
      state.host = container;
      state.fullHost = true;
      window.aifgPrepareIframe(iframe, { label, host: container, fullHost: true });
      return true;
    };

    if (attach(container.querySelector('iframe'))) return null;

    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (!attach(container.querySelector('iframe'))) return;
      observer.disconnect();
      if (containerObservers.get(container) === observer) {
        containerObservers.delete(container);
      }
    });
    containerObservers.set(container, observer);
    observer.observe(container, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    return observer;
  };

  window.openAifgIframePopup = function(popupOrId, src, label = 'Loading…') {
    const popup = resolveElement(popupOrId);
    if (!popup) return null;
    const frame = popup.querySelector('iframe');
    if (!frame) return null;
    popup.style.display = 'flex';
    window.aifgLoadIframe(frame, src, { label });
    return frame;
  };

  window.closeAifgIframePopup = function(popupOrId) {
    const popup = resolveElement(popupOrId);
    if (!popup) return;
    const frame = popup.querySelector('iframe');
    popup.style.display = 'none';
    if (frame) window.aifgUnloadIframe(frame);
  };

  window.openAifgDynamicIframePopup = function(popupOrId, slotOrId, src, label = 'Loading…') {
    const popup = resolveElement(popupOrId);
    const slot = resolveElement(slotOrId);
    if (!popup || !slot) return null;

    let frame = slot.querySelector('iframe');
    if (!frame) {
      frame = document.createElement('iframe');
      frame.src = 'about:blank';
      frame.allow = 'clipboard-read *; clipboard-write *; fullscreen';
      frame.allowFullscreen = true;
      frame.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:100%;border:none;display:block;';
      slot.appendChild(frame);
    }

    popup.style.display = 'flex';
    window.aifgLoadIframe(frame, src, { label, host: slot, fullHost: true });
    return frame;
  };

  window.closeAifgDynamicIframePopup = function(popupOrId, slotOrId) {
    const popup = resolveElement(popupOrId);
    const slot = resolveElement(slotOrId);
    if (popup) popup.style.display = 'none';
    if (!slot) return;
    const frame = slot.querySelector('iframe');
    if (frame) window.aifgRemoveIframe(frame);
    slot.querySelectorAll('.aifg-iframe-loader-mask').forEach(mask => mask.remove());
  };
})();
</script>

<script>
(function initAifgGalleryTransitionMask() {
  if (window.aifgShowGalleryTransitionMask) return;

  const state = {
    token: 0,
    showTimer: null,
    hideTimer: null,
    readyWatchTimer: null,
    readyWatchCleanup: null,
    shownAt: 0,
    mask: null,
    iframeObserver: null
  };

  const clearReadyWatch = () => {
    if (state.readyWatchTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.readyWatchTimer);
      state.readyWatchTimer = null;
    }

    if (state.readyWatchCleanup) {
      state.readyWatchCleanup();
      state.readyWatchCleanup = null;
    }
  };

  const clearTimers = () => {
    if (state.showTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.showTimer);
      state.showTimer = null;
    }
    if (state.hideTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.hideTimer);
      state.hideTimer = null;
    }
    clearReadyWatch();
  };

  const completeHide = (mask, token) => {
    if (!mask || state.token !== token) return;
    mask.hidden = true;
    mask.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
    mask.style.removeProperty('opacity');
    mask.style.removeProperty('pointer-events');
    mask.ontransitionend = null;
    state.hideTimer = null;
  };

  const ensureMask = () => {
    const host = document.getElementById('embedCtn');
    if (!host) return null;

    let mask = host.querySelector(':scope > .aifg-gallery-transition-mask');
    if (!mask) {
      mask = document.createElement('div');
      mask.className = 'aifg-iframe-loader-mask aifg-gallery-transition-mask';
      mask.dataset.aifgGalleryTransition = 'true';
      mask.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      mask.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      mask.innerHTML = `
        <div class="aifg-gallery-transition-content">
          <span class="aifg-iframe-loader-spinner" aria-hidden="true"></span>
          <span class="aifg-iframe-loader-label"></span>
        </div>
      `;
      host.appendChild(mask);
    }

    /* Preserve hot-reload compatibility with an already-created mask from the older layout. */
    if (!mask.querySelector(':scope > .aifg-gallery-transition-content')) {
      const content = document.createElement('div');
      content.className = 'aifg-gallery-transition-content';

      const spinner = mask.querySelector(':scope > .aifg-iframe-loader-spinner');
      const label = mask.querySelector(':scope > .aifg-iframe-loader-label');

      if (spinner) content.appendChild(spinner);
      if (label) content.appendChild(label);
      mask.appendChild(content);
    }

    state.mask = mask;
    return mask;
  };

  window.aifgPrepareGalleryIframeLoader = function(label = 'Loading Gallery…') {
    const gallery = document.getElementById('chatIframe4');
    const host = document.getElementById('embedCtn');
    if (!gallery || !host) return null;

    const attach = () => {
      const frame = gallery.querySelector('iframe');
      if (!frame) return null;

      window.aifgPrepareIframe?.(frame, {
        label,
        host,
        fullHost: true
      });
      return frame;
    };

    const frame = attach();
    if (frame) {
      state.iframeObserver?.disconnect();
      state.iframeObserver = null;
      return frame;
    }

    state.iframeObserver?.disconnect();
    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (!attach()) return;
      observer.disconnect();
      if (state.iframeObserver === observer) state.iframeObserver = null;
    });
    state.iframeObserver = observer;
    observer.observe(gallery, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    return null;
  };

  window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMask = function(immediate = true) {
    clearTimers();
    const token = ++state.token;
    const mask = state.mask || document.querySelector('#embedCtn > .aifg-gallery-transition-mask');
    if (!mask) return;

    mask.ontransitionend = null;

    if (immediate) {
      completeHide(mask, token);
      return;
    }

    mask.classList.add('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
    mask.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    mask.ontransitionend = event => {
      if (event.target === mask && event.propertyName === 'opacity') {
        completeHide(mask, token);
      }
    };
    state.hideTimer = setTimeout(() => completeHide(mask, token), 220);
  };

  window.aifgShowGalleryTransitionMask = function(label = 'Loading Gallery…', options = 420) {
    const mask = ensureMask();
    if (!mask) return null;

    const config = typeof options === 'object' && options !== null
      ? options
      : { minVisibleMs: options };

    const persistent = config.persistent === true;
    const minVisibleMs = Math.max(
      180,
      Math.min(Number(config.minVisibleMs) || 420, 2000)
    );

    clearTimers();
    const token = ++state.token;
    const labelEl = mask.querySelector('.aifg-iframe-loader-label');

    state.shownAt = performance.now();

    if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = String(label || 'Loading Gallery…');
    mask.setAttribute('aria-label', String(label || 'Loading Gallery…'));
    mask.ontransitionend = null;
    mask.hidden = false;
    mask.classList.remove('aifg-iframe-loader-leaving');
    mask.style.opacity = '1';
    mask.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';

    if (persistent) return mask;

    state.showTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (state.token !== token) return;
      state.showTimer = null;
      window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMask(false);
    }, minVisibleMs);

    return mask;
  };

  window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMaskWhenReady = function(frameOrId, expectedSrc, options = {}) {
    const frame = typeof frameOrId === 'string'
      ? document.getElementById(frameOrId)
      : frameOrId;

    if (!frame || frame.tagName !== 'IFRAME') {
      window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMask(false);
      return;
    }

    clearReadyWatch();

    const token = state.token;
    const normalizeUrl = value => {
      const raw = String(value || '').trim();
      if (!raw) return '';
      try {
        return new URL(raw, window.location.href).href;
      } catch (error) {
        return raw;
      }
    };

    const wantedSrc = normalizeUrl(expectedSrc);
    const minVisibleMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.minVisibleMs) || 320);
    const timeoutMs = Math.max(1000, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 20000);
    const startedAt = performance.now();
    let stopped = false;

    const stop = () => {
      if (stopped) return;
      stopped = true;
      frame.removeEventListener('aifg:iframe-ready', checkReady);
      if (state.readyWatchTimer) {
        clearTimeout(state.readyWatchTimer);
        state.readyWatchTimer = null;
      }
      if (state.readyWatchCleanup === stop) state.readyWatchCleanup = null;
    };

    const finish = () => {
      if (state.token !== token) {
        stop();
        return;
      }

      const elapsedVisible = performance.now() - state.shownAt;
      const remaining = Math.max(0, minVisibleMs - elapsedVisible);

      stop();
      state.readyWatchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        state.readyWatchTimer = null;
        if (state.token === token) {
          window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMask(false);
        }
      }, remaining);
    };

    function checkReady() {
      if (stopped || state.token !== token) {
        stop();
        return;
      }

      const readySrc = normalizeUrl(frame.dataset.aifgReadySrc);
      const isReady =
        frame.dataset.aifgIframeReady === '1' &&
        (!wantedSrc || readySrc === wantedSrc);

      if (isReady) {
        finish();
        return;
      }

      if (performance.now() - startedAt >= timeoutMs) {
        stop();
        window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMask(false);
        return;
      }

      if (state.readyWatchTimer) clearTimeout(state.readyWatchTimer);
      state.readyWatchTimer = setTimeout(checkReady, 100);
    }

    frame.addEventListener('aifg:iframe-ready', checkReady);
    state.readyWatchCleanup = stop;
    checkReady();
  };
})();
</script>

  <div id="appealPopup" style="background:#131516;padding:20px 30px;border-radius:8px;max-width:650px;box-shadow:0 0 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);font-family:'Open Sans',sans-serif;text-align:left; max-height: 90vh; overflow-y: auto;">

      <li>This channel is STRICTLY for personal appeals. This is NOT a channel for user reports, website feedback, rule suggestions, FAQs, or petitions.</li>
      <li>Understand that an appeal is a request for leniency or review, not a negotiation or an automatic right. The original decision remains valid unless the moderators explicitly change it.</li>
      <li>Identify yourself and the issue: Provide exact identifiers (username, full ID, incident, date) and the specific decision you are appealing. Appeals missing identifying information will only slow down processing.</li>
      <li>State your case clearly: Explain your side, provide relevant evidence, acknowledge any mistakes, and state exactly what outcome you want (correction, leniency, or re-evaluation).</li>
      <li>Skip the "@mod" or "hello." Submit your full appeal as one brief, respectful message right away, then wait for a response.</li>
      <li>Submit the appeal yourself — proxy appeals are not accepted to protect privacy and ensure accountability.</li>
      <li>Appeal again if necessary, but understand that repeated requests without new evidence or spamming may be refused.</li>
    </ul>

    </div>
  </div>
