<script>
  window.closePrivateWarning = function() {
      const checkbox = document.getElementById("privateChannelDontShow");
      if (checkbox && checkbox.checked) {
          localStorage.setItem("hidePrivateChannelWarningV1", "true");
      }
      document.getElementById("privateChannelWarningOverlay").style.display = "none";
  };
  
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    
    // ===== CONFIGURATION =====
    const GEN_KEY = 'eighteenConsentDataV3';       // Storage for Normal 18+
    const LOUNGE_KEY = 'loungeConsentDataV1';      // Storage for Adult Lounge
    const ADULT_ENTRY_PASS_KEY = '_adultEntryPassV1'; // Permanent browser membership card
    const ADULT_CORRECTION_KEY = '_adultNicknameCorrectionRequiredV1';

    // Snapshot existing adult-room consent before this page can create new consent.
    // This lets older already-checked browsers migrate to the newer membership-card key
    // without allowing genuinely new users to skip the Waiting Room.
    const hadAdultConsentBeforeThisLoad = [GEN_KEY, LOUNGE_KEY].some(key => {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || 'null')?.stop === true;
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    });

    if (
      hadAdultConsentBeforeThisLoad &&
      localStorage.getItem(ADULT_ENTRY_PASS_KEY) !== '1' &&
      localStorage.getItem(ADULT_CORRECTION_KEY) !== '1' &&
      typeof classifyAdultNickname === 'function'
    ) {
      const cachedNickname = String(
        window._myNickname || localStorage.getItem('_myNickname') || ''
      ).normalize('NFC').trim();

      if (classifyAdultNickname(cachedNickname).status === 'adult') {
        localStorage.setItem(ADULT_ENTRY_PASS_KEY, '1');
      }
    }

    window.hasAdultEntryPass = () => localStorage.getItem(ADULT_ENTRY_PASS_KEY) === '1';
    window.needsAdultNicknameCheck = () =>
      !window.hasAdultEntryPass() || localStorage.getItem(ADULT_CORRECTION_KEY) === '1';
    
    // State Tracking
    window.isLoungeTarget = false; 

    // Standard Button Style for adult-entry notices
    const BTN_STYLE = "background:#5b5b5b; color:#d8d4cf; padding:8px 16px; border:none; border-radius:5px; font-size:0.85em; cursor:pointer; font-family:'Open Sans',sans-serif;";

    // ===== UTILITIES & PRE-ACTIONS =====
    function removeEl(id) {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.remove();
    }

    // RESTORED: Uses CSS classes instead of inline style
    function createPreActionsPopup(id, title, confirmText, onConfirm) {
      removeEl(id);
      const overlay = document.createElement('div');
      overlay.id = id;
      overlay.className = 'pre-actions-overlay';
      overlay.onclick = (e) => { if (e.target === overlay) removeEl(id); };
      overlay.innerHTML = `
        <div class="pre-actions-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <h4>${title}</h4>
          <div class="popup-actions">
            <button class="popup-btn" id="${id}-confirm">${confirmText}</button>
            <button class="popup-btn" id="${id}-cancel">Cancel</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(overlay);
      document.getElementById(`${id}-cancel`).onclick = () => removeEl(id);
      document.getElementById(`${id}-confirm`).onclick = () => { removeEl(id); onConfirm(); };
    }

    // RESTORED: Uses CSS classes instead of inline style
    function createMultiChoicePopup(id, title, buttons) {
      removeEl(id);
      const overlay = document.createElement('div');
      overlay.id = id;
      overlay.className = 'pre-actions-overlay';
      overlay.onclick = (e) => { if (e.target === overlay) removeEl(id); };
      const buttonHTML = buttons.map((btnData, index) => {
        return `<button class="popup-btn" id="${id}-btn-${index}">${btnData.text}</button>`;
      }).join('');
      overlay.innerHTML = `
        <div class="pre-actions-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <h4>${title}</h4>
          <div class="popup-actions" style="flex-direction: column; gap: 8px;"> ${buttonHTML}
            <button class="popup-btn" id="${id}-cancel">Cancel</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(overlay);
      buttons.forEach((btnData, index) => {
        document.getElementById(`${id}-btn-${index}`).onclick = () => {
          removeEl(id);
          btnData.action();
        };
      });
      document.getElementById(`${id}-cancel`).onclick = () => removeEl(id);
    }

    // ===== STORAGE UTILITIES (STATE-BASED) =====
    window.getConsentData = () => {
        const key = window.isLoungeTarget ? LOUNGE_KEY : GEN_KEY;
        try {
            return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)) || { stop: false };
        } catch {
            return { stop: false };
        }
    };
    
    window.saveConsentData = (newData) => { 
        const key = window.isLoungeTarget ? LOUNGE_KEY : GEN_KEY;
        let current = { stop: false };

        try {
            current = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)) || current;
        } catch {}

        localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify({
            ...current,
            ...newData,
            time: Date.now()
        }));
    };

    function openRequiredAdultNicknameCheck() {
      const channel = window.isLoungeTarget ? "adult-lounge" : "chat2";
      if (typeof showAdultNicknameCheck === "function") {
        showAdultNicknameCheck(channel, "missing");
      } else if (typeof showAgeVerificationModal === "function") {
        showAgeVerificationModal();
      }
    }

    function resumePendingAdultDestination() {
      const tab = window.pending18Tab;
      const resumeSidebarSwitch = window.pendingAdultSidebarSwitch === true;

      window.pending18Tab = null;
      window.pendingAdultSidebarSwitch = false;

      if (tab) {
        tab.click();
      } else if (resumeSidebarSwitch) {
        document.getElementById("switchChannelBtn")?.click();
      }
    }

    // ===== SUB-NOTICE SYSTEM (Standard Gray Buttons) =====
    window.showSubNotice = (titleText, messageLines, onCloseCallback = null, showRefreshButton = false, showOkButton = true) => {
      const subOverlay = document.getElementById("subNoticeOverlay");
      const subTitle = document.getElementById("subNoticeTitle");
      const subText = document.getElementById("subNoticeText");
      const subButtonArea = document.getElementById("subNoticeButtonArea");
      if (!subOverlay) return;

      subButtonArea.innerHTML = '';

      subTitle.textContent = titleText;
      subText.innerHTML = Array.isArray(messageLines) ? messageLines.join("<br>") : messageLines;
      subOverlay.style.display = "flex";

      if (showOkButton) {
          const okBtn = document.createElement("button");
          okBtn.style.cssText = BTN_STYLE;
          okBtn.textContent = "OK";
          okBtn.onclick = () => {
            subOverlay.style.display = "none";
            if (onCloseCallback) onCloseCallback();
          };
          subButtonArea.appendChild(okBtn);
      }

      if (showRefreshButton) {
        const refreshBtn = document.createElement("button");
        refreshBtn.style.cssText = BTN_STYLE;
        refreshBtn.textContent = "⟳︎ Refresh";
        refreshBtn.onclick = () => location.reload();
        subButtonArea.appendChild(refreshBtn);
      }
    };

    // ===== 1. ART STYLE CONTEST =====
    
    const contestKeywords = ["𝗦𝘁𝘆𝗹𝗲 𝗖𝗼𝗻𝘁𝗲𝘀𝘁"]; 

    const ContestOverlay = document.createElement("div");
    ContestOverlay.id = "styleContestContestOverlayEl";
    ContestOverlay.style.cssText = `inset:0;position:fixed;display:none;justify-content:center;align-items:center;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.85);backdrop-filter:blur(5px);z-index:100005;`;
    document.body.appendChild(ContestOverlay);

    // Using the "Big Modal" structure
    const guideCard = document.createElement("div");
    guideCard.style.cssText = `
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        width: 95vw;
        max-width: 800px;
        height: auto;
        max-height: 90vh;
        background: var(--box-color, #222);
        color: var(--text-color, #fff);
        border: 1px solid var(--border-color, #444);
        border-radius: 12px;
        box-shadow: 0 10px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
        font-family: 'Open Sans', sans-serif;
        overflow: hidden;
    `;
    
    // Prevent click propagation
    guideCard.onclick = (e) => e.stopPropagation();
    ContestOverlay.appendChild(guideCard);

    // Close on background click
    ContestOverlay.onclick = () => ContestOverlay.style.display = 'none';

    // Function to render content with dynamic countdowns
    window.updateContestOverlayContent = function() {
        const now = Date.now();
        
        // Dates
        // Start from 2026-04-05 23:59 UTC+8 = 2026-04-05 15:59 UTC
        // Submit: Apr 05 15:59 UTC - Apr 10 15:59 UTC
        const tSubmitStart = Date.UTC(2026, 3, 5, 15, 59);
        const tSubmitEnd   = Date.UTC(2026, 3, 10, 15, 59);
        
        // Vote: Apr 10 15:59 UTC - Apr 13 10:00 UTC
        const tVoteStart   = Date.UTC(2026, 3, 10, 15, 59);
        const tVoteEnd     = Date.UTC(2026, 3, 13, 10, 0);
        
        const getDiffStr = (target) => {
            const diff = target - now;
            if (diff < 0) return "Ended";
            const d = Math.floor(diff / 86400000);
            const h = Math.floor((diff % 86400000) / 3600000);
            return `${d}d ${h}h remaining`;
        };
        
        const strSubmit = (now < tSubmitStart) ? "Starts soon" : (now < tSubmitEnd ? getDiffStr(tSubmitEnd) : "Ended");
        const strVote   = (now < tVoteStart)   ? "Starts soon" : (now < tVoteEnd   ? getDiffStr(tVoteEnd)   : "Ended");

        guideCard.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:15px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color);background:var(--box-color);flex-shrink:0;">
                <div style="font-weight:bold;font-size:1.1rem;color:var(--text-color);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.5rem;">
                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined">sports_score</span> 
                    Style Contest Guide
                </div>
                <button onclick="document.getElementById('styleContestContestOverlayEl').style.display='none'" 
                    style="font-size:1.4rem;background:transparent;border:none;cursor:pointer;opacity:0.7;color:var(--text-color);padding:0 5px;line-height:1;">✕</button>
            </div>

            <div class="custom-thin-scroll" style="flex:1; overflow-y:auto; padding:25px; line-height:1.6; font-size:0.95em; text-align:left;">
                
                <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); border:1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:8px; padding:15px; margin-bottom:20px;">
                    <h4 style="margin:0 0 10px 0; color:var(--text-color); opacity:0.9; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
                        <i class="fa-regular fa-calendar-days"></i> Timeline (UTC+0)
                    </h4>
                      <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: 100px 1fr auto; row-gap:8px; align-items:center;">
                          <span style="font-weight:bold; color:var(--accent-color);">Submit</span>
                          <span>Apr 05 – Apr 10</span>
                          <span style="font-family:monospace; font-size:0.85em; background:#333; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px; text-align:right;">${strSubmit}</span>
                      
                          <span style="font-weight:bold; color:var(--accent-color);">Vote</span>
                          <span>Apr 11 – Apr 13</span>
                          <span style="font-family:monospace; font-size:0.85em; background:#333; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px; text-align:right;">${strVote}</span>
                      </div>
                </div>

                <h4 style="margin:0 0 10px 0; opacity:0.9; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-scroll"></i> Contest Rules
                </h4>
                <p style="margin-top:0;">
                    Complete rules are available in our official 
                    <b style="color:var(--special-color,#6bf); cursor:pointer; text-decoration:underline;" 
                        onclick="window.openAifgIframePopup('guidePopup', window.getAifgGuideUrl(), 'Loading Guide…')">Guide</b>.
                </p>
                
                <ul style="padding-left: 20px; margin: 10px 0;">
                    <li style="margin-bottom:6px;"><b>Update:</b> Prompt weighting is allowed, such as <code>(tag:1.3)</code> or <code>(tag:0.7)</code>.</li>
                    <li style="margin-bottom:6px;"><b>Update:</b> Originality is judged against both existing default styles and your own other entries in the same contest.</li>
                    <li style="margin-bottom:6px;"><b>Update:</b> There is <b>no final round</b> this time.</li>
                    <li style="margin-bottom:6px;"><b>Update:</b> Public voting now counts as a separate <b>User Vote (0–5)</b> axis based on <b>average star rating</b>, alongside moderator scoring.</li>
                    <li style="margin-bottom:6px;"><b>Update:</b> The voting phase now lasts <b>2 days</b>.</li>
                    <li style="margin-bottom:6px;"><b>Update:</b> Suspicious or manipulated votes may be removed without prior notice.</li>
                </ul>

    };

    // ===== 2. 18+ CONSENT SETUP (CORRECTED) =====
    const overlay = document.getElementById("eighteenConsentOverlay");
    const agreeBtn = document.getElementById("agree18plus");
    const leaveBtn = document.getElementById("leave18plus");
    
    // 1. When they click "I am 18" in the first popup
    if (agreeBtn && overlay) {
      agreeBtn.onclick = () => {
        if (agreeBtn.disabled) return;
        
        overlay.style.display = "none";

        if (window.isLoungeTarget) {
            const loungeRules = document.getElementById("rulesLoungeOverlay");
            if (loungeRules) loungeRules.style.display = "flex";
        } else {
            const rules = document.getElementById("rules18PlusOverlay");
            if (rules) rules.style.display = "flex";
        }
      };
    }

    // 2. Accept this room's rules once. First-ever adult entry then goes through
    // the mandatory Waiting Room before adult-room access is allowed. Passing the

          stop: true,
          entryMethod: "rules"
        });

        if (window.needsAdultNicknameCheck()) {
          openRequiredAdultNicknameCheck();
          return;
        }

        resumePendingAdultDestination();
    };

    window.proceedFromRPRules = function() {
        const neverShowCb = document.getElementById("rpNeverShowCb");
        const tab = window.pendingRPTab;
        window.pendingRPTab = null;
    
        if (neverShowCb && neverShowCb.checked) {
            localStorage.setItem("rpRuleDismissedV1", "true");
        }
    
        window.bypassRoleplayGateOnce = true;
        document.getElementById("rulesRoleplayOverlay").style.display = "none";
    
        if (tab) tab.click();
    };
  
    // 3. Leave Button Logic
    if (leaveBtn && overlay) {
      leaveBtn.onclick = () => {
        window.pendingAdultSidebarSwitch = false
        const generalTab = [...document.querySelectorAll('.tabbed-comments-plugin-tab')].find(el => el.innerText.trim().toLowerCase().includes('general'));
        if (generalTab) generalTab.click();
        
        overlay.style.display = "none";
        
        showSubNotice("Notice", [
          "18+ rooms are for adults only.",
          "If you are under 18, please stay in safe areas.",
          "Unauthorized entry may lead to bans.",
          "⟳︎ Refresh <b>NOW</b> to prevent NSFW notifications!"

    // This intercepts the click BEFORE the plugin sees it.
    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const clickedTab = e.target.closest('.tabbed-comments-plugin-tab');
      if (!clickedTab) return;

      // FIX: Clone the tab and strip the unread badge before reading the text.
      // We use textContent instead of innerText to bypass the CSS '#' injection.
      const tempClone = clickedTab.cloneNode(true);
      const unreadBadge = tempClone.querySelector('.tabbed-comments-plugin-unseen-count');
      if (unreadBadge) unreadBadge.remove();

      const originalTabText = tempClone.textContent.trim();
      const tabText = originalTabText.toLowerCase();

      // --- 1. SET THE ACTIVE ADULT ROOM IMMEDIATELY ---
      if (tabText.includes("adult lounge")) {
          window.isLoungeTarget = true;
      } else {
          window.isLoungeTarget = false;
      }

      // --- 2. HANDLE NON-18+ CHANNELS ---
      if (tabText.includes("furdex")) {

          "general", "18+ general", "adult lounge", "prompt", 
          "announcement", "furdex", "emoji", "feedback", "submit",
          "appeal", "test", "roleplay", "𝘀𝘁𝘆𝗹𝗲 𝗰𝗼𝗻𝘁𝗲𝘀𝘁", "𝘀𝘁𝘆𝗹𝗲 𝗰𝗼𝗻𝘁𝗲𝘀𝘁 ⛿"
      ];

      const isKnownChannel = knownChannels.includes(tabText);

      // If it's an unrecognized channel, show the privacy warning (if not opted out)

    // ===== ONE-TIME REFRESH CHECK (Zero RAM Drain) =====
    // Runs exactly once when the plugin finishes loading, then destroys itself.
    const initObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations, obs) => {
      const activeTab = document.querySelector('.tabbed-comments-plugin-active-tab');
      if (activeTab) {
        obs.disconnect(); // Kill observer immediately to free up memory.

        const tabText = activeTab.innerText.trim().toLowerCase();

              if (overlay) overlay.style.display = "flex";
            } else {
              openRequiredAdultNicknameCheck();
            }
          }
        }
      }
    });
    
    initObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

  });
</script>

(function() {
  // 1. Start the 'Search' loop  This is for intromessage iframe theme stuck
  let injectionTimer = setInterval(injectGuide, 1000);

  // 2. THE FAIL-SAFE (100% Leak-Proof)
  // Stops the CPU loop after 10 seconds no matter what.
  setTimeout(() => clearInterval(injectionTimer), 10000);

  function injectGuide() {
    const container = document.getElementById('delayedGuideBtn');
    
    // Check if the container is ready and empty
    if (container && container.innerHTML.trim() === "") {
      // Small delay so the Theme Engine finishes first
      setTimeout(() => {
        container.innerHTML = `
          <div onclick="openAndLoad()" style="cursor:pointer;display:inline-block;padding:7px 14px;background:var(--box-color);border:1px solid var(--border-color);border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-family:'Open Sans',sans-serif;font-size:92%;">
            <span class='material-symbols-outlined' style='vertical-align:-0.15em;font-size:1.1em;margin-right:4px;'>info</span>
            Guide, Style Preview, Rule
          </div>
        `;
        // SUCCESS: Stop the loop immediately
        clearInterval(injectionTimer);
      }, 500);
    }
  }

  window.openAndLoad = function() {
    window.openAifgIframePopup('guidePopup', window.getAifgGuideUrl(), 'Loading Guide…');
  };

  window.killAndClose = function() {
    window.closeAifgIframePopup('guidePopup');
  };
})();
</script>

  function flagImg(name){
    const code = FLAG_ISO[name];
    return code === "_unknown"
      ? `<img src="https://user.uploads.dev/file/a3d3b04381d0773d5801a30b95973d57.webp" alt="" style="width:16px;border-radius:2px;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:-2px;">`
      : code
        ? `<img src="https://flagcdn.com/w20/${code}.webp" alt="" style="width:16px;border-radius:2px;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:-2px;">`
        : "";
  }
  
  /* Fetch total users; keep pill; expand view grows down/right (same container) */
  /* === REPLACEMENT FOR fetchActiveUsers LOGIC === */
   
  // 1. Global timer variable (outside the function so it persists)
  window._ucTimer = null;
  window._shrinkTimer = null; // <-- ADDED: Track the shrink timer
  
  // 2. NEW: Track last count to prevent layout thrashing (The Stutter Fix)
  let lastFetchedCount = -1;

  async function fetchActiveUsers() {
    if (showingCountries) return;
    
    try {
      const res = await fetch("https://furrybackend.onrender.com/active-users", { headers: { "Accept": "application/json" }});
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
      const data = await res.json();
 
      const myPath = location.pathname.replace(/\/$/, "");
      const mainPath = "/ai-furry-generator";
      const mainUrl = "https://perchance.org/ai-furry-generator";
 
      const userCount = parseInt((data && (data[mainUrl] ?? data.activeUsers)) ?? 0);
      const lastUpdated = parseInt(data.lastUpdated) || Date.now();
      const timeSinceUpdate = Math.floor((Date.now() - lastUpdated) / 1000);
 
      const usrCountEl = document.getElementById("usrCount");
      const statusText = document.getElementById("statusText");
      const statusDot  = document.getElementById("statusDot");
      const lastUpdatedText = document.getElementById("lastUpdatedText");
      const authenticInfo = document.getElementById("authenticInfo");
 
      if (myPath !== mainPath) {
        authenticInfo.innerHTML = `<a href="${mainUrl}" target="_blank" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;opacity:.8;">Data from the authentic site <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-up-right-from-square fa-sm"></i></a>`;
        authenticInfo.style.display = "inline";
      } else {
        authenticInfo.style.display = "none";
      }
 
      // === OPTIMIZED LOGIC ===
      if (userCount === lastFetchedCount) return;
      lastFetchedCount = userCount;
 
      usrCountEl.textContent = userCount;
 
      if (userCount > 0) {
        statusDot.style.background = "lime";
        lastUpdatedText.style.display = "none";
        statusText.textContent = "Online";
      } else {
        statusText.textContent = "Offline";
        statusDot.style.background = "orange";
        lastUpdatedText.style.display = "inline";
        lastUpdatedText.textContent = `(Last updated: ${timeSinceUpdate}s ago)`;
      }

      // Ensure it is visible ONLY on first load or manual toggle
      if (!alreadyShrunk) {
        statusText.style.opacity = "1";
        statusText.style.maxWidth = "120px";
        statusText.style.marginLeft = "4px";

        // --- RESTORED SHRINK TIMER ---
        if (window._shrinkTimer) clearTimeout(window._shrinkTimer);
        window._shrinkTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            if (!showingCountries) {
                statusText.style.maxWidth = "0px";
                statusText.style.opacity = "0";
                statusText.style.marginLeft = "0px";
                alreadyShrunk = true;
            }
        }, 4000); // <-- Shrinks after 4 seconds
      }
      
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Error fetching data:", e);
      // Fallback display
      const el = document.getElementById("usrCount");
      if(el) el.textContent = "Loading...";
      el.style.fontSize = "0.75rem";
    }
  }
 
  // Initial Fetch
  fetchActiveUsers();
  
  // === SAFER INTERVAL HANDLER ===
  // Clears previous instances to prevent "decimal time" accumulation
  if (window._ucTimer) clearInterval(window._ucTimer);
  window._ucTimer = setInterval(fetchActiveUsers, 30000);
 
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    // 1. Always clear first. This makes the logic idempotent.
    if (window._ucTimer) clearInterval(window._ucTimer);
    
    // 2. Only restart if page is visible
    if (!document.hidden) {
      fetchActiveUsers(); // Fetch immediately on tab focus
      window._ucTimer = setInterval(fetchActiveUsers, 30000);
    }
  });
  
  /* Morph to country list (same pill; expands down/right). Click outside to close. */
  document.getElementById("usrCountWrapper").addEventListener("click", async (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (showingCountries) return; // Prevent running if already open

    const tipIcon = document.getElementById('userCountTipIcon');
    if (tipIcon && localStorage.getItem(userCountTipKey) !== 'true') {
      localStorage.setItem(userCountTipKey, 'true');
      tipIcon.style.opacity = '0';
      tipIcon.style.maxWidth = '0px';
      tipIcon.style.marginLeft = '0px';
    }
        // 1. Set state and cursor immediately
    showingCountries = true;
    document.getElementById("usrCountWrapper").style.cursor = "default";
  
    // 2. Get elements
    const mainLine = document.getElementById("mainCountLine");
    const countryBox = document.getElementById("countryList");
    const countryContent = document.getElementById("countryListContent");
  
    // 3. --- Trigger Hide/Show Animations ---
    // Instantly hide main line (this solves the "double" issue)
    mainLine.style.display = "none";
    mainLine.style.maxHeight = "0px";
    mainLine.style.opacity = "0";
    
    // Show and animate-in the country box with the *only* loading state
    countryBox.style.display = "flex"; // Set display before animating
    countryBox.style.maxHeight = "200px";
    countryBox.style.opacity = "1";
    countryContent.innerHTML = '<span style="opacity:.8;"><i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin" style="margin-right: 5px;"></i>Loading...</span>';
  
    // 4. Define and attach the 'close' listener
    const close = (ev) => {
      if (!document.getElementById("usrCountWrapper").contains(ev.target)) {
        // --- Trigger Close Animations ---
        countryBox.style.maxHeight = "0px";
        countryBox.style.opacity = "0";
        
        mainLine.style.display = "flex";
        mainLine.style.maxHeight = "30px";
        mainLine.style.opacity = "1";
    
        showingCountries = false;
        document.getElementById("usrCountWrapper").style.cursor = "pointer";
        document.removeEventListener("click", close, true);
    
        // Hide countryBox display *after* animation finishes
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (!showingCountries) countryBox.style.display = "none";
          alreadyShrunk = false;  
          
          // FORCE UI REFRESH so it triggers the shrink timer again
          lastFetchedCount = -1;
          fetchActiveUsers();
        }, 300); // 300ms matches animation
      }
    };
    
    // attach the listener slightly after opening to enable outside-click closing
    setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener("click", close, true), 0);
    
    // 5. --- Now, fetch the data in the background ---
    try {
      const res = await fetch("https://furrybackend.onrender.com/country-stats", { headers: { "Accept": "application/json" }});
      const data = await res.json();
      const entries = Object.entries(data.countries || {}).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);

      // 6. Populate the panel (only if it's still open)
          if (showingCountries) {
            countryContent.innerHTML = entries.length
              ? entries.map(([c,v]) =>
                  `<span style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;line-height:1.8;">
                     <span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;">
                       ${FLAG_ISO[c] ? `<a href="https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(c)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" title="Google: ${c}">${flagImg(c)}</a>` : flagImg(c)}
                       ${c}
                     </span>
                     <span>&nbsp;${v}</span>
                   </span>`
                ).join("")
              : `<span style="opacity:.8;">No active users by country.</span>`;
          }
    } catch {
      // 7. Show error (only if it's still open)
      if (showingCountries) {
        countryContent.innerHTML = '<span style="opacity:.8;"><i class="fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation" style="margin-right: 5px;"></i>Failed to fetch data.</span>';
      }
    }
  });
  </script>

<!-- First-visit calm mode: reuse the existing consent marker; no extra storage key. -->
<script>
window.aifgShouldSpareFirstVisit = (() => {
  let firstVisit;
  return () => {
    if (firstVisit !== undefined) return firstVisit;
    try {
      firstVisit = !localStorage.getItem("userConsent");
    } catch {
      firstVisit = true;
    }
    return firstVisit;
  };
})();
</script>

    { html: `<i class="fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles" style="margin-right:4px;"></i><b>Image-to-Image (I2i):</b> Fix, refine, or completely transform your generations! Open the right-side menu and select <b>AI Image Editor</b> to modify your furry art.` },
    { html: `<i class="fa-solid fa-folder-tree" style="margin-right:4px;"></i><b>Concept Board & Workspace Update:</b> You can now route saved media with Smart Folders, toggle sidebar buttons, and add up to 10 Custom Apps! Configure these in the <i class="fa-solid fa-user-gear"></i> <b>Account & Settings</b> menu.` },
  ];

  const version = "broadcast_v14"; // update = renew
  const baseKey = "tickerBanner";
  const countKey= `${baseKey}_dismissCount_${version}`;
  const rotKey  = `${baseKey}_startIndex_${version}`;
  const maxDismisses = 2;

  const dismissCount = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(countKey) || "0", 10);
  if (dismissCount >= maxDismisses) return;

  const stack = document.getElementById("bannerStack") || (() => {
    const s = document.createElement("div");
    s.id = "bannerStack";
    s.style.position = "sticky";
    s.style.top = "0";
    s.style.zIndex = "9999";
    s.style.display = "flex";
    s.style.flexDirection = "column";
    s.style.gap = "4px";               /* match spacing */
    s.style.marginBottom = "8px";      /* match spacing */
    document.body.prepend(s);
    return s;
  })();

  const banner = document.createElement("div");
  banner.style.cssText = `
    background: var(--special-color);
    color: #fff;
    padding: 8px 14px 8px 42px;       /* keep as banner1 spec */
    box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    font-family: 'Open Sans', sans-serif;
    border-radius: 4px;
  `;

  const wrap = document.createElement("div");
  wrap.style.cssText = "overflow:hidden; position:relative; height:1.4em; flex:1; user-select:none;";

  const closeBtn = document.createElement("button");
  closeBtn.textContent = "✕";
  closeBtn.style.cssText = "background:none;border:none;color:#fff;font-size:1rem;cursor:pointer;margin-left:20px;";
  closeBtn.onclick = () => {
    stopTicker();
    banner.remove();
    localStorage.setItem(countKey, String(Math.min(maxDismisses, dismissCount + 1)));
  };

  banner.appendChild(wrap);
  banner.appendChild(closeBtn);
  stack.appendChild(banner);

  let idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * items.length);
  const tickerGapPx = 48; // compact visual separation; about 0.6s at 80px/s
  const activeTickerItems = [];
  let tickerFrame = 0;
  let lastTickerTime = 0;
  let tickerPaused = false;
  let tickerStopped = false;

  function addTickerItem(startX) {
    const item = items[idx];
    idx = (idx + 1) % items.length;

    const itemEl = document.createElement("span");
    itemEl.style.cssText = "position:absolute; white-space:nowrap; left:0; top:0; will-change:transform;";
    itemEl.innerHTML = item.html ?? "";
    if (item.text && !item.html) itemEl.textContent = item.text;
    itemEl.style.textDecoration = item.onClick ? "underline" : "none";
    itemEl.style.cursor = item.onClick ? "pointer" : "default";
    itemEl.onclick = item.onClick || null;
    wrap.appendChild(itemEl);

    const tickerItem = {
      el: itemEl,
      x: startX,
      width: itemEl.scrollWidth || 300
    };
    activeTickerItems.push(tickerItem);
    return tickerItem;
  }

  function stopTicker() {
    tickerStopped = true;
    if (tickerFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(tickerFrame);
    tickerFrame = 0;
  }

  function runTicker(now) {
    if (tickerStopped) return;

    if (!lastTickerTime) lastTickerTime = now;
    const elapsedMs = Math.min(64, now - lastTickerTime);
    lastTickerTime = now;

    if (!tickerPaused) {
      const wrapW = wrap.clientWidth || 300;
      const travelPx = pxPerSec * elapsedMs / 1000;

      for (let i = activeTickerItems.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const tickerItem = activeTickerItems[i];
        tickerItem.x -= travelPx;

        if (tickerItem.x + tickerItem.width <= 0) {
          tickerItem.el.remove();
          activeTickerItems.splice(i, 1);
          continue;
        }

        tickerItem.el.style.transform = `translate3d(${tickerItem.x}px, 0, 0)`;
      }

      if (!activeTickerItems.length) {
        addTickerItem(wrapW);
      }

      let tail = activeTickerItems[activeTickerItems.length - 1];
      while (tail.x + tail.width <= wrapW - tickerGapPx) {
        tail = addTickerItem(tail.x + tail.width + tickerGapPx);
        tail.el.style.transform = `translate3d(${tail.x}px, 0, 0)`;
      }
    }

    tickerFrame = requestAnimationFrame(runTicker);
  }

  banner.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => { tickerPaused = true; });
  banner.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {
    tickerPaused = false;
    lastTickerTime = performance.now();
  });

  function openLightbox(src, alt) {
    if (document.getElementById("fudexbannerOverlay")) return;
    const overlay = document.createElement("div");
    overlay.id = "fudexbannerOverlay";
    overlay.style.cssText = `inset:0;position:fixed;background:rgba(0,0,0,.6);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:10000;`;
    const wrap = document.createElement("div");
    wrap.id = "fudexbannerImgWrap";
    wrap.style.cssText = `position:relative;max-width:90vw;max-height:90vh;width:90vw;height:90vh;display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;z-index:10001;`;
    const img = document.createElement("img");
    img.src = src; img.alt = alt || "";
    img.style.cssText = "max-width:100%;max-height:100%;border-radius:8px;display:block;";
    const close = document.createElement("button");
    close.id = "fudexbannerClose"; close.textContent = "✕";
    close.style.cssText = `position:absolute;top:8px;right:8px;font-size:20px;line-height:1;background:rgba(0,0,0,.55);color:#fff;border:none;cursor:pointer;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:6px;`;
    const closeAll = () => overlay.remove();
    overlay.addEventListener("click", closeAll);
    close.addEventListener("click", closeAll);
    wrap.addEventListener("click", e => e.stopPropagation());
    wrap.appendChild(img); wrap.appendChild(close); overlay.appendChild(wrap); document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  }

  window.openLightbox = openLightbox;

  addTickerItem(wrap.clientWidth || 300);
  tickerFrame = requestAnimationFrame(runTicker);
});
</script>

On desktop, Studio can also use an optional resizable configuration/results split with independent scrolling. Change both options in <a href="#" onclick="event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();window.openAccountModal?.('general');" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;font-weight:600;">Account &amp; Settings → General Preferences</a>.</div>` },
  ];  // <=  Do not delete this ending mark 

  const sessionDismissedKeys = new Set();
  const showNextBanner = () => {
    const container = getUpdateContainer();
    container.innerHTML = "";
    
    for (const bannerData of allBanners) {
      const { key, maxShows, html, withCountdown, endMs } = bannerData;
      const count = Number(localStorage.getItem(key) ?? 0);
      if (count >= maxShows || sessionDismissedKeys.has(key)) {
        continue;
      }
      makeBanner(key, html, withCountdown, endMs);
      return;
    }
  };

  const getUpdateContainer = () =>
    document.getElementById("updateBannerContainer") ||
    (() => {
      const s = document.createElement("div");
      s.id = "updateBannerContainer";
      s.style.zIndex = "9999";
      s.style.position = "relative";
      s.style.pointerEvents = "none";  // <-- prevents invisible layer from blocking user count clicks
      const tickerStack = document.getElementById("bannerStack");
      if (tickerStack) tickerStack.after(s);
      else document.body.prepend(s);
      return s;
    })();

  const makeBanner = (key, html, withCountdown = false, endMs = null) => {
    let timer = null; 
    
    const banner = document.createElement("div");
    banner.style.cssText =
      "position:relative;background:var(--special-color);color:#fff;padding:8px 14px;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.2);font-size:.9rem;font-family:'Open Sans',sans-serif;border-radius:4px;margin-bottom:8px;margin-top:4px;";
    banner.style.pointerEvents = "auto";
    banner.innerHTML = `<div style="min-width:0;padding-right:2rem;">${html}</div><button type="button" data-banner-dismiss style="position:absolute;top:0.42rem;right:0.42rem;width:1.7rem;height:1.7rem;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:0;background:none;border:1px solid transparent;border-radius:0.4rem;color:#fff;font-size:1rem;cursor:pointer;opacity:0.72;" aria-label="Dismiss">✕</button>`;

    banner.querySelector("[data-banner-dismiss]").addEventListener(
      "click",
      () => {
        if (timer) clearInterval(timer); 

        try {
          localStorage.setItem(
            key,
            String(Number(localStorage.getItem(key) ?? 0) + 1)
          );
          sessionDismissedKeys.add(key); 
        } catch {}
        banner.remove();
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("bannerDismissed"));
      },
      { once: true }
    );

    const container = getUpdateContainer();
    container.appendChild(banner);

    // --- Countdown Logic ---
    if (withCountdown && Number.isFinite(endMs)) {
      const span = banner.querySelector("[data-countdown='true']");

      if (!span) return;

      const tick = () => {
        const diffMs = endMs - Date.now();

        // 1. Check if time has run out
        if (diffMs <= 0) {
          span.textContent = "Ended";
          if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
          return;
        }

        // 2. Calculate time remaining
        const totalHours = Math.floor(diffMs / 3600000);
        const days = Math.floor(totalHours / 24);
        const hours = totalHours % 24;

        // 3. Update the text
        span.textContent = `Ends in ${days}d ${hours}h`;
      };

      // Run immediately once, then set interval
      tick();
      timer = setInterval(tick, 60000);
    }
  };

  document.addEventListener("bannerDismissed", () => {
    setTimeout(showNextBanner, 0);
  });
  showNextBanner();
});
</script>
<!-- ====== update banner bar ====== -->

<script>
window.galleryCommentNotice = () => {
  const key = "galleryCommentRule";
  const shown = localStorage.getItem(key);

  if (!shown) {
    localStorage.setItem(key, "true");
    showSubNotice(
      "Gallery comment rule",
      [
        "All community rules apply equally to gallery discussions.",
        "Off-topic or disruptive behavior may result in warnings or bans."
      ],
      null,    // This is for onCloseCallback
      false,   // This is for showRefreshButton
      5        // This is the new 5-second duration
    );
  }
};
</script>

  html[data-workspace-layout="dock"][data-workspace-split="on"][data-workspace-panel="studio"] #aifgStudioConfigColumn,
  html[data-workspace-layout="dock"][data-workspace-split="on"][data-workspace-panel="studio"] #aifgStudioBatchColumn{

    overflow:hidden auto;
    overscroll-behavior:contain;
    scrollbar-gutter:auto;
  }
  html[data-workspace-layout="dock"][data-workspace-split="on"][data-workspace-panel="studio"] #aifgStudioConfigColumn{
    padding-right:0;
  }
  html[data-workspace-layout="dock"][data-workspace-split="on"][data-workspace-panel="studio"] #aifgStudioBatchColumn{
    padding-left:clamp(1rem,2vw,1.5rem);
  }
  html[data-workspace-layout="dock"][data-workspace-split="on"][data-workspace-panel="studio"] #aifgStudioSplitter{
    width:.9rem;
    height:100%;
    display:block;
    position:relative;

  }

    content:"";

  html[data-workspace-layout="dock"][data-workspace-split="on"][data-workspace-panel="studio"] #aifgStudioSplitter::after{
    content:"";
    position:absolute;

      radial-gradient(circle,var(--text-color) 1.2px,transparent 1.4px) center/5px 7px repeat-y,

    width:3px;

  }
  html[data-workspace-layout="dock"][data-workspace-split="on"][data-workspace-panel="studio"] #aifgStudioSplitter:hover::after,
