<script>
(function(){
  const cfg={
    percentMin:20,
    percentMax:200,
    percentStep:5,
    baseHeightPx:450,
    storageKey:"t2iResult_Lite_v5"
  };

  let currentZoom=parseInt(localStorage.getItem(cfg.storageKey+"_val")||"100",10);
  let isBatchOpen=localStorage.getItem(cfg.storageKey+"_open")!=="false";
  let isPanelCreated=false, rafId=null, hideTimer=null;
  let isStudioOpen=true;

  function getBatchImages(){
    const output=document.getElementById("outputAreaEl");
    return output ? Array.from(output.children).filter(el=>el.classList?.contains("t2i-image-ctn")) : [];
  }

  function getGenerateWrap(){
    const button=document.getElementById("generateButtonEl");
    return button?.parentElement?.parentElement || null;
  }

  function syncBatchActions(){
    const images=getBatchImages();
    const hasImages=images.length>0;
    const hasPinnedImages=images.some(el=>el.classList.contains("t2i-pinned"));
    const hasClearableImages=images.some(el=>!el.classList.contains("t2i-pinned"));
    const toolbar=document.getElementById("t2iBatchToolbar");
    const zoomControl=document.getElementById("t2iZoomControl");
    const toggleBtn=document.getElementById("t2iBatchToggleBtn");
    const toggleIcon=document.getElementById("t2iBatchToggleIcon");
    const toggleLabel=document.getElementById("t2iBatchToggleLabel");
    const unpinAllBtn=document.getElementById("t2iUnpinAllBtn");
    const clearAllBtn=document.getElementById("t2iClearAllBtn");

    if(toolbar) toolbar.hidden=!hasImages;
    if(zoomControl) zoomControl.hidden=!hasImages;
    if(toggleBtn) toggleBtn.hidden=!hasImages;
    if(unpinAllBtn) unpinAllBtn.hidden=!hasPinnedImages;
    if(clearAllBtn) clearAllBtn.hidden=!hasClearableImages;

    if(toggleIcon) toggleIcon.className=isBatchOpen ? "fa-solid fa-eye-slash" : "fa-solid fa-eye";
    if(toggleLabel) toggleLabel.textContent=isBatchOpen ? "Hide" : "Show";
    if(toggleBtn){
      const label=isBatchOpen ? "Hide generated results" : "Show generated results";
      toggleBtn.title=label;
      toggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-label",label);
      toggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-pressed",isBatchOpen ? "true" : "false");
    }
    window.syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage?.();
  }

  window.__syncT2iBatchActions=syncBatchActions;

  window.__clearT2iGeneratedBatch=function(){
    const output=document.getElementById("outputAreaEl");
    if(!output)return;

    window.closeImageMenu?.();
    window._currentImageCtn=null;

    Array.from(output.children).forEach(child=>{
      const isPinnedImage=
        child.classList?.contains("t2i-image-ctn") &&
        child.classList.contains("t2i-pinned");

      if(!isPinnedImage) child.remove();
    });

    applyStudioVisibility();
  };

  function updateVisuals(p){
    p=Math.max(cfg.percentMin,Math.min(cfg.percentMax,p));
    const height=Math.round((p/100)*cfg.baseHeightPx);
    const lvl=document.getElementById("t2iZoomLevelEl");
    const lbl=document.getElementById("t2iZoomSliderLabel");
    const slider=document.getElementById("t2iZoomSlider");
    const output=document.getElementById("outputAreaEl");

    if(lvl)lvl.innerHTML=`<i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass"></i><span>${p}%</span>`;
    if(lbl)lbl.textContent=`${p}%`;

    if(slider){
      slider.value=p;
      const percentage=((p-cfg.percentMin)/(cfg.percentMax-cfg.percentMin))*100;
      slider.style.background=`linear-gradient(to right, var(--text-color) ${percentage}%, var(--border-color) ${percentage}%)`;
    }

    if(output)output.style.setProperty("--t2i-h",`${height}px`);
    currentZoom=p;
  }

  function saveZoom(val){
    localStorage.setItem(cfg.storageKey+"_val",val);
  }

  function syncStudioToggleUi(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById("t2iStudioToggleWrap");
    const btn=document.getElementById("t2iStudioToggleBtn");
    const arrow=document.getElementById("t2iStudioArrow");

    if(arrow) arrow.classList.toggle("rotate-closed",!isStudioOpen);
    if(btn){
      btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded",isStudioOpen ? "true" : "false");
      btn.title=isStudioOpen ? "Collapse Studio" : "Expand Studio";
      btn.setAttribute("aria-label",isStudioOpen ? "Collapse Studio" : "Expand Studio");
    }
  }

  function applyStudioVisibility(){
    const userInputs=document.getElementById("userInputsCtn8462746262");
    const share=document.getElementById("shareLinkOuterCtn2893827");
    const generateWrap=getGenerateWrap();
    const toolbarWrap=document.getElementById("t2iBatchToolbarWrap");
    const output=document.getElementById("outputAreaEl");

    if(userInputs) userInputs.style.display=isStudioOpen ? "flex" : "none";
    if(share) share.style.display=isStudioOpen ? "" : "none";
    if(generateWrap){
      if(!generateWrap.id) generateWrap.id="t2iGenerateWrap";
      generateWrap.style.display=isStudioOpen ? "" : "none";
    }
    if(toolbarWrap) toolbarWrap.style.display=isStudioOpen ? "flex" : "none";
    if(output){
      const hasOutputContent=output.children.length>0;
      output.style.display=isStudioOpen && isBatchOpen && hasOutputContent ? "flex" : "none";
    }

    syncStudioToggleUi();
    syncBatchActions();
  }

  function toggleStudio(){
    isStudioOpen=!isStudioOpen;
    applyStudioVisibility();
  }

  function toggleBatch(){
    isBatchOpen=!isBatchOpen;
    localStorage.setItem(cfg.storageKey+"_open",String(isBatchOpen));
    applyStudioVisibility();
  }

  function ensureStudioToggle(){
    let wrap=document.getElementById("t2iStudioToggleWrap");
    if(!wrap){
      const title=document.getElementById("pageTitleEl48759084793");
      if(!title || !title.parentNode)return;

      wrap=document.createElement("div");
      wrap.id="t2iStudioToggleWrap";
      wrap.className="t2i-studio-toggle-wrap";
      wrap.innerHTML=`
        <button type="button" id="t2iStudioToggleBtn" class="t2i-toggle-btn">
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined">dashboard_2</span>
          <span>Studio</span>
          <span id="t2iStudioArrow" class="t2i-toggle-arrow">▼</span>
        </button>`;
      title.insertAdjacentElement("afterend",wrap);
      document.getElementById("t2iStudioToggleBtn").onclick=e=>{
        e.stopPropagation();
        toggleStudio();
      };
    }

    applyStudioVisibility();
  }

  function swapMode(showSlider){
    const btn=document.getElementById("t2iBtnGroup");
    const sldr=document.getElementById("t2iSliderWrap");
    if(!btn||!sldr)return;

    if(showSlider){
      btn.classList.add("t2i-hidden");
      sldr.classList.remove("t2i-hidden");
      clearTimeout(hideTimer);
      hideTimer=setTimeout(()=>swapMode(false),2000);
    }else{
      sldr.classList.add("t2i-hidden");
      btn.classList.remove("t2i-hidden");
    }
  }

  function createZoomPanel(){
    if(isPanelCreated)return;

    const outputEl=document.getElementById("outputAreaEl");
    if(!outputEl)return;

    const root=document.createElement("div");
    root.id="t2iBatchToolbarWrap";
    root.className="t2i-batch-toolbar-wrap";
    root.innerHTML=`
      <div id="t2iBatchToolbar" class="t2i-batch-toolbar" hidden>
        <button type="button" id="t2iBatchToggleBtn" class="t2i-batch-control-btn" title="Hide generated results" aria-label="Hide generated results" aria-pressed="true">
          <i id="t2iBatchToggleIcon" class="fa-solid fa-eye-slash"></i>
          <span id="t2iBatchToggleLabel" class="t2i-batch-action-label">Hide</span>
        </button>

        <div id="t2iZoomControl" class="t2i-zoom-control" title="Result size" aria-label="Result size">
          <div id="t2iBtnGroup" style="display:flex;align-items:center;height:100%;">
            <button type="button" id="t2iZoomOutBtn" class="t2i-zoom-segment" title="Zoom out" aria-label="Zoom out"><i class="fa-solid fa-minus"></i></button>
            <button type="button" id="t2iZoomLevelEl" class="t2i-zoom-segment" title="Adjust result size" aria-label="Adjust result size"><i class="fa-solid fa-magnifying-glass"></i><span>100%</span></button>
            <button type="button" id="t2iZoomInBtn" class="t2i-zoom-segment" title="Zoom in" aria-label="Zoom in"><i class="fa-solid fa-plus"></i></button>
          </div>
          <div id="t2iSliderWrap" class="t2i-slider-mode-wrapper t2i-hidden">
            <input type="range" id="t2iZoomSlider" min="${cfg.percentMin}" max="${cfg.percentMax}" step="${cfg.percentStep}" value="${currentZoom}" aria-label="Result size">
            <span id="t2iZoomSliderLabel">100%</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button type="button" id="t2iUnpinAllBtn" class="t2i-batch-control-btn" hidden title="Unpin all generated images" aria-label="Unpin all generated images">
          <i class="fa-solid fa-thumbtack-slash"></i>
          <span class="t2i-batch-action-label">Unpin all</span>
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="t2iClearAllBtn" class="t2i-batch-control-btn" hidden title="Clear all generated content" aria-label="Clear all generated content">
          <i class="fa-solid fa-trash-can"></i>
          <span class="t2i-batch-action-label">Clear all</span>
        </button>
      </div>`;

    outputEl.parentNode.insertBefore(root,outputEl);
    isPanelCreated=true;

    document.getElementById("t2iBatchToggleBtn").onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();toggleBatch()};
    document.getElementById("t2iZoomLevelEl").onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();swapMode(true)};
    document.getElementById("t2iZoomInBtn").onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();updateVisuals(currentZoom+cfg.percentStep);saveZoom(currentZoom)};
    document.getElementById("t2iZoomOutBtn").onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();updateVisuals(currentZoom-cfg.percentStep);saveZoom(currentZoom)};
    document.getElementById("t2iUnpinAllBtn").onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();window.menuActionUnpinAll?.();syncBatchActions()};
    document.getElementById("t2iClearAllBtn").onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();window.__clearT2iGeneratedBatch()};

    const slider=document.getElementById("t2iZoomSlider");
    slider.oninput=e=>{
      clearTimeout(hideTimer);
      hideTimer=setTimeout(()=>swapMode(false),3000);
      const val=parseInt(e.target.value,10);
      if(rafId)cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      rafId=requestAnimationFrame(()=>updateVisuals(val));
    };
    slider.onchange=e=>saveZoom(e.target.value);

    window.t2iApplyResultZoom?.();
    updateVisuals(currentZoom);
    syncBatchActions();
    applyStudioVisibility();
  }

  const bootLoop=setInterval(()=>{
    const title=document.getElementById("pageTitleEl48759084793");
    const genBtn=document.getElementById("generateButtonEl");
    if(!title || !genBtn)return;
    clearInterval(bootLoop);

    // Studio always exists. Before the first generation it sits under the
    // normal title/banner; when the framework swaps that same title element
    // to nextTitle, Studio naturally remains directly underneath it.
    ensureStudioToggle();

    genBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>{
      createZoomPanel();

      setTimeout(()=>{
        updateVisuals(currentZoom);
        syncBatchActions();
      },200);
    });
  },100);
})();
</script>
<!-- === t2i outputAreaEl zoom slider === --->

<!-- === Classic / Focused Workflow workspace layout === -->
<script>
(() => {
  'use strict';

  const LAYOUT_KEY = 'pl_workspaceLayout';
  const PANEL_KEY = 'pl_workspaceDockPanel';
  const SPLIT_KEY = 'pl_workspaceStudioSplit';
  const SPLIT_RATIO_KEY = 'pl_workspaceStudioSplitRatio';
  const PREVIEW_KEY = 'pl_workspaceLayoutPreviewV1';
  const INTRO_DONE_KEY = 'pl_focusedModeIntroDoneV1';
  const INTRO_VISIT_KEY = 'pl_focusedModeVisitCountV1';
  const INTRO_VISIT_SESSION_KEY = 'pl_focusedModeVisitCountedV1';
  const FOCUSED_EMPTY_MESSAGE = 'Generated images will appear here.';
  const PAGE_PANELS = ['studio', 'comments', 'gallery'];
  const desktopStudioMedia = window.matchMedia?.('(min-width:900px)') || null;
  let activationToken = 0;
  let studioWorkflowState = null;
  let floatingSurfaceSyncBound = false;
  let pageTitleDismissButton = null;
  let pageTitleObserver = null;
  let pageTitleResizeObserver = null;
  let pageTitleMountObserver = null;
  let batchOutputObserver = null;
  let focusedOriginalIntro = null;
  let pageTitleRepairing = false;
  let studioHeightFrame = 0;
  const panelScrollState = new Map(PAGE_PANELS.map(panel => [panel, {
    pageY: 0,
    hostY: 0
  }]));
  let panelScrollRestoreFrame = 0;
  let panelScrollRestoreObserver = null;
  let panelScrollRestoreTimer = null;
  let panelScrollRestoreToken = 0;

  const clampStudioSplitRatio = value => Math.min(55, Math.max(30, Number(value) || 38));

  const readStudioSplitRatio = () => {
    try {
      return clampStudioSplitRatio(localStorage.getItem(SPLIT_RATIO_KEY));
    } catch {
      return 38;
    }
  };

  const setStudioSplitRatio = (value, persist = false) => {
    const ratio = clampStudioSplitRatio(value);
    const wrapper = studioWorkflowState?.wrapper;
    const splitter = studioWorkflowState?.splitter;
    wrapper?.style.setProperty('--aifg-studio-left', `${ratio}%`);
    splitter?.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', String(Math.round(ratio)));
    if (persist) {
      try { localStorage.setItem(SPLIT_RATIO_KEY, String(Math.round(ratio))); } catch {}
    }
    return ratio;
  };

  const syncStudioWorkspaceHeight = () => {
    const wrapper = studioWorkflowState?.wrapper;
    if (!wrapper) return;

    const isActiveSplit =
      document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceLayout === 'dock' &&
      document.documentElement.dataset.workspacePanel === 'studio' &&
      document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceSplit === 'on' &&
      desktopStudioMedia?.matches === true;

    if (!isActiveSplit) {
      wrapper.style.removeProperty('--aifg-studio-height');
      return;
    }

    const top = Math.max(0, wrapper.getBoundingClientRect().top);
    const available = Math.max(320, Math.floor(window.innerHeight - top));
    wrapper.style.setProperty('--aifg-studio-height', `${available}px`);
  };

  const scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight = () => {
    if (studioHeightFrame) return;
    studioHeightFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      studioHeightFrame = 0;
      syncStudioWorkspaceHeight();
    });
  };

  const bindStudioSplitter = splitter => {
    let activePointerId = null;
    let currentRatio = readStudioSplitRatio();

    const updateFromPointer = event => {
      if (activePointerId !== event.pointerId) return;
      const wrapper = studioWorkflowState?.wrapper;
      const rect = wrapper?.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect?.width) return;
      const styles = getComputedStyle(wrapper);
      const paddingLeft = parseFloat(styles.paddingLeft) || 0;
      const paddingRight = parseFloat(styles.paddingRight) || 0;
      const contentWidth = Math.max(1, rect.width - paddingLeft - paddingRight);
      currentRatio = setStudioSplitRatio(((event.clientX - rect.left - paddingLeft) / contentWidth) * 100);
    };

    const finishDrag = event => {
      if (activePointerId !== event.pointerId) return;
      updateFromPointer(event);
      splitter.releasePointerCapture?.(activePointerId);
      activePointerId = null;
      splitter.removeAttribute('data-dragging');
      setStudioSplitRatio(currentRatio, true);
    };

    splitter.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
      if (document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceSplit !== 'on' || desktopStudioMedia?.matches !== true) return;
      activePointerId = event.pointerId;
      splitter.setPointerCapture?.(activePointerId);
      splitter.setAttribute('data-dragging', '1');
      updateFromPointer(event);
      event.preventDefault();
    });
    splitter.addEventListener('pointermove', updateFromPointer);
    splitter.addEventListener('pointerup', finishDrag);
    splitter.addEventListener('pointercancel', finishDrag);
    splitter.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
      if (event.key !== 'ArrowLeft' && event.key !== 'ArrowRight') return;
      currentRatio = setStudioSplitRatio(currentRatio + (event.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -2 : 2), true);
      event.preventDefault();
    });

    setStudioSplitRatio(currentRatio);
  };

  const ensurePageTitleDismiss = () => {
    const title = document.getElementById('pageTitleEl48759084793');
    if (!title || typeof window.___showNextTitle746291937 !== 'function') return false;

    if (!pageTitleDismissButton) {
      pageTitleDismissButton = document.createElement('button');
      pageTitleDismissButton.id = 'aifgPageTitleDismiss';
      pageTitleDismissButton.type = 'button';
      pageTitleDismissButton.title = 'Show compact title';
      pageTitleDismissButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Show compact title');
      pageTitleDismissButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
      pageTitleDismissButton.addEventListener('click', event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        window.___showNextTitle746291937();
        scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
      });
    }

    const attachInsideTitleVisual = () => {
      const visual = title.querySelector('#pageTitleBanner');
      if (!visual) {
        pageTitleDismissButton.remove();
        return;
      }
      if (pageTitleRepairing || pageTitleDismissButton.isConnected && title.contains(pageTitleDismissButton)) return;
      pageTitleRepairing = true;
      try {
        visual.classList.add('aifg-page-title-dismiss-host');
        visual.appendChild(pageTitleDismissButton);
        scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
      } finally {
        pageTitleRepairing = false;
      }
    };

    attachInsideTitleVisual();

    if (!pageTitleObserver && typeof MutationObserver === 'function') {
      pageTitleObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {
        attachInsideTitleVisual();
      });
      pageTitleObserver.observe(title, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    if (!pageTitleResizeObserver && typeof ResizeObserver === 'function') {
      pageTitleResizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight);
      pageTitleResizeObserver.observe(title);
    }
    return true;
  };

  const ensureGlobalTitleDismiss = () => {
    if (ensurePageTitleDismiss()) {
      pageTitleMountObserver?.disconnect();
      pageTitleMountObserver = null;
      return true;
    }
    if (!pageTitleMountObserver && typeof MutationObserver === 'function') {
      pageTitleMountObserver = new MutationObserver(() => ensureGlobalTitleDismiss());
      pageTitleMountObserver.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    return false;
  };

  const ensureSharedIntroHost = () => {
    let host = document.getElementById('aifgSharedIntroHost');
    if (host) return host;

    const title = document.getElementById('pageTitleEl48759084793');
    if (!title?.parentNode) return null;

    host = document.createElement('div');
    host.id = 'aifgSharedIntroHost';
    title.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', host);
    return host;
  };

  const syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage = () => {
    const output = document.getElementById('outputAreaEl');
    const batchColumn = studioWorkflowState?.batchColumn;
    const liveIntro = document.getElementById('t2iIntroMessageEl');
    if (liveIntro && !focusedOriginalIntro) focusedOriginalIntro = liveIntro;

    const root = document.documentElement;
    const isIntroPhase = !!(liveIntro || focusedOriginalIntro?.isConnected);
    const activePanel = root.dataset.workspacePanel;
    const useSharedIntro =
      isIntroPhase &&
      root.dataset.workspaceLayout === 'dock' &&
      (activePanel === 'comments' || activePanel === 'gallery');
    const sharedIntroHost = useSharedIntro
      ? ensureSharedIntroHost()
      : document.getElementById('aifgSharedIntroHost');

    if (useSharedIntro && focusedOriginalIntro && sharedIntroHost) {
      if (focusedOriginalIntro.parentNode !== sharedIntroHost) sharedIntroHost.append(focusedOriginalIntro);
    } else if (isIntroPhase && focusedOriginalIntro && output) {
      if (focusedOriginalIntro.parentNode !== output) output.append(focusedOriginalIntro);
    } else if (!isIntroPhase) {
      focusedOriginalIntro?.remove();
      sharedIntroHost?.remove();
    }

    const hasImages = !!output?.querySelector(':scope > .t2i-image-ctn');
    const shouldShowFocusedEmpty =
      !!output &&
      !!batchColumn?.isConnected &&
      root.dataset.workspaceLayout === 'dock' &&
      activePanel === 'studio' &&
      root.dataset.workspaceSplit === 'on' &&
      desktopStudioMedia?.matches === true &&
      !hasImages;

    if (shouldShowFocusedEmpty) {
      let focusedEmpty = document.getElementById('aifgFocusedBatchEmpty');
      if (!focusedEmpty) {
        focusedEmpty = document.createElement('div');
        focusedEmpty.id = 'aifgFocusedBatchEmpty';
        focusedEmpty.setAttribute('role', 'status');
        focusedEmpty.textContent = FOCUSED_EMPTY_MESSAGE;
      }
      if (focusedEmpty.parentNode !== output) output.prepend(focusedEmpty);
      focusedEmpty.hidden = false;
      output.classList.remove('aifg-output-compact-empty');
      output.style.display = 'flex';
      return;
    }

    document.getElementById('aifgFocusedBatchEmpty')?.remove();
    const hasVisibleContent = Array.from(output?.children || []).some(child => !child.hidden);
    output?.classList.toggle('aifg-output-compact-empty', !isIntroPhase && !hasVisibleContent);
  };
  window.syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage = syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage;

  const mountStudioWorkflow = () => {
    if (studioWorkflowState?.wrapper?.isConnected) return true;

    const main = document.getElementById('mainColumnEl85394739');
    const userInputs = document.getElementById('userInputsCtn8462746262');
    const share = document.getElementById('shareLinkOuterCtn2893827');
    const generateButton = document.getElementById('generateButtonEl');
    const generateWrap = generateButton?.parentElement?.parentElement || null;
    const output = document.getElementById('outputAreaEl');
    const toolbar = document.getElementById('t2iBatchToolbarWrap');

    const requiredNodes = [userInputs, share, generateWrap, output];
    if (!main || requiredNodes.some(node => !node || node.parentNode !== main)) return false;
    if (toolbar && toolbar.parentNode !== main) return false;

    if (!generateWrap.id) generateWrap.id = 't2iGenerateWrap';

    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    const configColumn = document.createElement('div');
    const splitter = document.createElement('div');
    const batchColumn = document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.id = 'aifgStudioWorkflow';
    configColumn.id = 'aifgStudioConfigColumn';
    configColumn.classList.add('custom-thin-scroll');
    splitter.id = 'aifgStudioSplitter';
    splitter.tabIndex = 0;
    splitter.setAttribute('role', 'separator');
    splitter.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Resize Studio configuration and generated results');
    splitter.setAttribute('aria-orientation', 'vertical');
    splitter.setAttribute('aria-valuemin', '30');
    splitter.setAttribute('aria-valuemax', '55');
    batchColumn.id = 'aifgStudioBatchColumn';
    batchColumn.classList.add('custom-thin-scroll');

    const markers = {
      userInputs: document.createComment('aifg-studio-user-inputs'),
      share: document.createComment('aifg-studio-share'),
      generate: document.createComment('aifg-studio-generate'),
      batch: document.createComment('aifg-studio-batch')
    };

    main.insertBefore(markers.userInputs, userInputs);
    main.insertBefore(markers.share, share);
    main.insertBefore(markers.generate, generateWrap);
    main.insertBefore(markers.batch, toolbar || output);
    main.insertBefore(wrapper, markers.userInputs);

    wrapper.append(configColumn, splitter, batchColumn);
    configColumn.append(userInputs, share, generateWrap);
    if (toolbar) batchColumn.append(toolbar);
    batchColumn.append(output);

    studioWorkflowState = {
      wrapper,
      configColumn,
      splitter,
      batchColumn,
      markers,
      nodes: { userInputs, share, generateWrap, output }
    };
    bindStudioSplitter(splitter);
    if (typeof MutationObserver === 'function') {
      batchOutputObserver?.disconnect();
      batchOutputObserver = new MutationObserver(syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage);
      batchOutputObserver.observe(output, { childList: true });
    }
    scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
    syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage();
    return true;
  };

  const unmountStudioWorkflow = () => {
    const state = studioWorkflowState;
    if (!state) return;

    const restoreBefore = (node, marker) => {
      if (node && marker?.parentNode) marker.parentNode.insertBefore(node, marker);
    };

    restoreBefore(state.nodes.userInputs, state.markers.userInputs);
    restoreBefore(state.nodes.share, state.markers.share);
    restoreBefore(state.nodes.generateWrap, state.markers.generate);
    restoreBefore(document.getElementById('t2iBatchToolbarWrap'), state.markers.batch);
    restoreBefore(state.nodes.output, state.markers.batch);

    state.wrapper.remove();
    Object.values(state.markers).forEach(marker => marker.remove());
    batchOutputObserver?.disconnect();
    batchOutputObserver = null;
    studioWorkflowState = null;
  };

  const getControl = panel => ({
    studio: document.getElementById('t2iStudioToggleBtn'),
    comments: document.getElementById('commentToggleBtn'),
    gallery: document.getElementById('galleryToggleHeader')
  })[panel] || null;

  const isPanelOpen = panel => {
    if (panel === 'studio') {
      return getControl(panel)?.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
    }
    if (panel === 'comments') {
      return getControl(panel)?.dataset.hidden !== '1';
    }
    if (panel === 'gallery') {
      return document.getElementById('galleryBodyContainer')?.style.display !== 'none';
    }
    return false;
  };

  const setPanelOpen = (panel, shouldOpen) => {
    const control = getControl(panel);
    if (control && isPanelOpen(panel) !== shouldOpen) control.click();
  };

  const isMenuVisible = () => {
    const menu = document.getElementById('menuCtn');
    return !!menu && getComputedStyle(menu).display !== 'none';
  };

  const syncMenuButtonState = () => {
    const button = document.querySelector('#aifgActionDock [data-dock-panel="menu"]');
    if (!button) return;
    const embed = document.getElementById('embedCtn');
    const embedOpen = !!(embed && !embed.hidden);
    const menuOpen = !embedOpen && isMenuVisible();
    const state = embedOpen ? 'panel' : menuOpen ? 'menu' : 'closed';
    const isActive = state !== 'closed';

    if (button.dataset.surfaceState !== state) {
      button.dataset.surfaceState = state;
      button.innerHTML = state === 'panel'
        ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-angle-down" aria-hidden="true"></i>'
        : state === 'menu'
          ? '<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark" aria-hidden="true"></i>'
          : '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" aria-hidden="true">chrome_reader_mode</span>';
    }

    const label = state === 'panel' ? 'Return to menu' : state === 'menu' ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu';
    button.title = label;
    button.setAttribute('aria-label', label);
    button.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isActive ? 'true' : 'false');
  };

  const setDockActive = active => {
    if (active) {
      document.documentElement.dataset.workspacePanel = active;
    } else {
      delete document.documentElement.dataset.workspacePanel;
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('#aifgActionDock [data-dock-panel]').forEach(button => {
      if (!PAGE_PANELS.includes(button.dataset.dockPanel)) return;
      const isActive = button.dataset.dockPanel === active;
      button.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', isActive ? 'true' : 'false');
    });
  };

  const getPanelScrollRoot = panel => ({
    studio: document.getElementById('aifgStudioWorkflow'),
    comments: document.getElementById('commentsPluginCtn654732'),
    gallery: document.getElementById('galleryBodyContainer')
  })[panel] || null;

  const cancelPanelScrollRestore = () => {
    panelScrollRestoreToken += 1;
    if (panelScrollRestoreFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(panelScrollRestoreFrame);
    if (panelScrollRestoreTimer) clearTimeout(panelScrollRestoreTimer);
    panelScrollRestoreObserver?.disconnect();
    panelScrollRestoreFrame = 0;
    panelScrollRestoreTimer = null;
    panelScrollRestoreObserver = null;
  };

  const capturePanelScroll = panel => {
    const state = panelScrollState.get(panel);
    if (!state) return;

    state.pageY = Math.max(0, window.scrollY || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0);
    state.hostY = Math.max(0, getPanelScrollRoot(panel)?.scrollTop || 0);
  };

  const restorePanelScroll = panel => {
    cancelPanelScrollRestore();
    const state = panelScrollState.get(panel);
    if (!state) return;

    const token = panelScrollRestoreToken;
    const root = getPanelScrollRoot(panel);

    const apply = () => {
      panelScrollRestoreFrame = 0;
      if (
        token !== panelScrollRestoreToken ||
        document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceLayout !== 'dock' ||
        document.documentElement.dataset.workspacePanel !== panel
      ) return;

      const page = document.scrollingElement || document.documentElement;
      const pageMax = Math.max(0, page.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight);
      window.scrollTo(0, Math.min(state.pageY, pageMax));

      if (root) root.scrollTop = Math.min(state.hostY, Math.max(0, root.scrollHeight - root.clientHeight));

      const pageReady = state.pageY <= pageMax + 1;
      const rootReady = !root || state.hostY <= Math.max(0, root.scrollHeight - root.clientHeight) + 1;
      if (pageReady && rootReady) cancelPanelScrollRestore();
    };

    const queueApply = () => {
      if (token !== panelScrollRestoreToken) return;
      if (panelScrollRestoreFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(panelScrollRestoreFrame);
      panelScrollRestoreFrame = requestAnimationFrame(apply);
    };

    const needsGrowthWatch = state.pageY > 0 || state.hostY > 0;
    if (needsGrowthWatch && typeof ResizeObserver === 'function') {
      panelScrollRestoreObserver = new ResizeObserver(queueApply);
      if (root) panelScrollRestoreObserver.observe(root);
      panelScrollRestoreTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (token === panelScrollRestoreToken) cancelPanelScrollRestore();
      }, 4000);
    }
    queueApply();
  };

  const selectPagePanel = panel => {
    if (!PAGE_PANELS.includes(panel)) return;

    const previousPanel = document.documentElement.dataset.workspacePanel;
    if (PAGE_PANELS.includes(previousPanel)) capturePanelScroll(previousPanel);
    PAGE_PANELS.forEach(candidate => setPanelOpen(candidate, candidate === panel));
    localStorage.setItem(PANEL_KEY, panel);
    setDockActive(panel);
    scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
    syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage();
    restorePanelScroll(panel);
  };

  const showMenu = () => {
    const sourceButton = document.getElementById('floatingBtn');
    sourceButton?.click();
    requestAnimationFrame(syncMenuButtonState);
  };

  const bindFloatingSurfaceSync = () => {
    if (floatingSurfaceSyncBound) return true;
    const sourceButton = document.getElementById('floatingBtn');
    const menu = document.getElementById('menuCtn');
    if (!sourceButton || !menu) return false;

    const syncAfterAction = () => requestAnimationFrame(syncMenuButtonState);
    sourceButton.addEventListener('click', syncAfterAction);
    menu.addEventListener('click', syncAfterAction);
    floatingSurfaceSyncBound = true;
    syncMenuButtonState();
    return true;
  };

  const ensureDock = () => {
    let dock = document.getElementById('aifgActionDock');
    if (dock) return dock;

    dock = document.createElement('nav');
    dock.id = 'aifgActionDock';
    dock.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Workspace');
    dock.innerHTML = `
      <button id="aifgActionDockBack" class="aifg-action-dock-btn" type="button" title="Back to top" aria-label="Back to top" hidden>
        <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-up" aria-hidden="true"></i>
      </button>
      <button class="aifg-action-dock-btn" type="button" data-dock-panel="studio" title="Studio" aria-label="Show Studio" aria-pressed="false">
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined" aria-hidden="true">dashboard_2</span>
      </button>
      <button class="aifg-action-dock-btn" type="button" data-dock-panel="comments" title="Comment" aria-label="Show Comment" aria-pressed="false">
        <i class="fa-solid fa-comments" aria-hidden="true"></i>
      </button>
      <button class="aifg-action-dock-btn" type="button" data-dock-panel="gallery" title="Gallery" aria-label="Show Gallery" aria-pressed="false">
        <i class="fa-regular fa-images" aria-hidden="true"></i>
      </button>
      <button class="aifg-action-dock-btn" type="button" data-dock-panel="menu" title="Menu" aria-label="Show Menu" aria-pressed="false">
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined" aria-hidden="true">chrome_reader_mode</span>
      </button>`;

    dock.querySelector('#aifgActionDockBack').addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.getElementById('backToTopBtn')?.click();
    });
    dock.querySelector('[data-dock-panel="studio"]').addEventListener('click', () => selectPagePanel('studio'));
    dock.querySelector('[data-dock-panel="comments"]').addEventListener('click', () => selectPagePanel('comments'));
    dock.querySelector('[data-dock-panel="gallery"]').addEventListener('click', () => selectPagePanel('gallery'));
    dock.querySelector('[data-dock-panel="menu"]').addEventListener('click', showMenu);

    document.body.appendChild(dock);
    return dock;
  };

  const isFocusedModePreview = () => {
    try {
      return sessionStorage.getItem(PREVIEW_KEY) === 'dock';
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const prepareFocusedModeIntro = () => {
    try {
      const alreadyChosen = localStorage.getItem(LAYOUT_KEY) !== null;
      const alreadyFinished = localStorage.getItem(INTRO_DONE_KEY) === '1';
      if (alreadyChosen || alreadyFinished) return false;

      let visits = Math.max(0, Number(localStorage.getItem(INTRO_VISIT_KEY)) || 0);
      if (sessionStorage.getItem(INTRO_VISIT_SESSION_KEY) !== '1') {
        visits = Math.min(3, visits + 1);
        localStorage.setItem(INTRO_VISIT_KEY, String(visits));
        sessionStorage.setItem(INTRO_VISIT_SESSION_KEY, '1');
      }

      if (visits < 3) return false;
      sessionStorage.setItem(PREVIEW_KEY, 'dock');
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const getBannerStack = () => {
    const current = document.getElementById('bannerStack');
    if (current) return current;

    const stack = document.createElement('div');
    stack.id = 'bannerStack';
    stack.style.cssText = 'position:sticky;top:0;z-index:9999;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;margin-bottom:8px;';
    document.body.prepend(stack);
    return stack;
  };

  window.dismissFocusedModeIntro = () => {
    document.getElementById('aifgFocusedModeIntro')?.remove();
  };

  const finishFocusedModeIntro = mode => {
    localStorage.setItem(LAYOUT_KEY, mode === 'dock' ? 'dock' : 'classic');
    localStorage.setItem(INTRO_DONE_KEY, '1');
    try { sessionStorage.removeItem(PREVIEW_KEY); } catch {}
    window.dismissFocusedModeIntro();
    window.applyWorkspaceLayout();
  };

  const showFocusedModeIntro = () => {
    if (!isFocusedModePreview() || localStorage.getItem(INTRO_DONE_KEY) === '1') return;
    if (document.getElementById('aifgFocusedModeIntro')) return;

    const banner = document.createElement('section');
    banner.id = 'aifgFocusedModeIntro';
    banner.setAttribute('role', 'region');
    banner.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Focused Workflow preview');
    banner.innerHTML = `
      <div class="aifg-focused-mode-intro-copy">
        <b>Trying Focused Workflow.</b>
        Switch between Studio, Comment, Gallery, and Menu from one compact control. On desktop, Studio can use a resizable configuration and generated-results split.
        Keep it, switch back, or change it anytime in
        <button type="button" class="aifg-focused-mode-intro-link" data-focused-settings>Account &amp; Settings → General Preferences</button>.
      </div>
      <div class="aifg-focused-mode-intro-actions">
        <button type="button" class="aifg-focused-mode-intro-btn" data-focused-back>Switch Back</button>
        <button type="button" class="aifg-focused-mode-intro-btn is-primary" data-focused-keep>Keep Focused Workflow</button>
      </div>`;

    banner.querySelector('[data-focused-settings]').addEventListener('click', () => {
      window.openAccountModal?.('general');
    });
    banner.querySelector('[data-focused-back]').addEventListener('click', () => {
      finishFocusedModeIntro('classic');
    }, { once: true });
    banner.querySelector('[data-focused-keep]').addEventListener('click', () => {
      finishFocusedModeIntro('dock');
    }, { once: true });

    getBannerStack().appendChild(banner);
    scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
  };

  const scheduleFocusedModeIntro = () => {
    const schedule = () => setTimeout(showFocusedModeIntro, 5000);
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', schedule, { once: true });
    } else {
      schedule();
    }
  };

  window.syncActionDockBackButton = () => {
    const proxy = document.getElementById('aifgActionDockBack');
    const source = document.getElementById('backToTopBtn');
    if (!proxy) return;

    const shouldShow = document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceLayout === 'dock' &&
      source?.style.display === 'flex' &&
      source.style.opacity !== '0';
    proxy.hidden = !shouldShow;
  };

  const activateSavedPanel = (token, attempt = 0) => {
    if (token !== activationToken || document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceLayout !== 'dock') return;

    const workflowReady = mountStudioWorkflow();
    const controlsReady = PAGE_PANELS.every(panel => !!getControl(panel));
    const floatingReady = bindFloatingSurfaceSync();
    if ((!workflowReady || !controlsReady || !floatingReady) && attempt < 40) {
      setTimeout(() => activateSavedPanel(token, attempt + 1), 100);
      return;
    }
    if (!workflowReady || !controlsReady) return;

    const storedPanel = localStorage.getItem(PANEL_KEY);
    selectPagePanel(PAGE_PANELS.includes(storedPanel) ? storedPanel : 'studio');
    syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage();
    scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
    syncMenuButtonState();
  };

  window.applyWorkspaceLayout = () => {
    const previousPanel = document.documentElement.dataset.workspacePanel;
    if (
      document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceLayout === 'dock' &&
      PAGE_PANELS.includes(previousPanel)
    ) capturePanelScroll(previousPanel);
    cancelPanelScrollRestore();

    const mode = isFocusedModePreview() || localStorage.getItem(LAYOUT_KEY) === 'dock'
      ? 'dock'
      : 'classic';
    const dock = ensureDock();
    document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceLayout = mode;
    document.documentElement.dataset.workspaceSplit = localStorage.getItem(SPLIT_KEY) === 'off' ? 'off' : 'on';
    dock.setAttribute('aria-hidden', mode === 'dock' ? 'false' : 'true');

    activationToken += 1;
    if (mode === 'dock') {
      activateSavedPanel(activationToken);
    } else {
      setDockActive('');
      unmountStudioWorkflow();
    }

    syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage();
    scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
    syncMenuButtonState();
    window.syncActionDockBackButton();
    return mode;
  };

  const syncResponsiveWorkspace = () => {
    syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage();
    scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
  };

  if (desktopStudioMedia) {
    if (typeof desktopStudioMedia.addEventListener === 'function') {
      desktopStudioMedia.addEventListener('change', syncResponsiveWorkspace);
    } else {
      desktopStudioMedia.addListener?.(syncResponsiveWorkspace);
    }
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize', scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('load', scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight, { once: true });
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', cancelPanelScrollRestore, { once: true });
  document.addEventListener('bannerDismissed', scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight);
  document.addEventListener('t2iNextTitleReady', ensureGlobalTitleDismiss, { once: true });
  document.addEventListener('t2iNextTitleShown', () => {
    syncFocusedBatchEmptyMessage();
    scheduleStudioWorkspaceHeight();
  });

  const shouldIntroduceFocusedMode = prepareFocusedModeIntro();
  ensureGlobalTitleDismiss();
  window.applyWorkspaceLayout();
  if (shouldIntroduceFocusedMode) scheduleFocusedModeIntro();
})();
</script>
<!-- === Classic / Focused Workflow workspace layout === -->
  
<!-- === Main Gallery Panel === --->
<script>
(function installAifgGalleryChangeAll() {
  const escapeRegex = value =>
    String(value).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');

  const formatSearchPattern = query =>
    String(query || '')
      .replace(/"([^"]+)"/g, (_, quotedText) => {
        const safeText = escapeRegex(quotedText.trim());
        return safeText ? `(^|[^a-z0-9])${safeText}($|[^a-z0-9])` : '';
      })
      .replace(/\bAND\b/gi, '.*')
      .replace(/\bOR\b/gi, '|')
      .replace(/\s*\|\s*/g, '|')
      .replace(/\s*\.\*\s*/g, '.*')
      .trim();

  const readGalleryData = url => {
    const rawData = decodeURIComponent(url.hash || '')
      .replace(/^#data=/, '')
      .replace(/^"/, '')
      .replace(/"$/, '');

    if (!rawData) return {};

    try {
      return JSON.parse(rawData);
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Could not read Gallery iframe data.', error);
      return {};
    }
  };

  const readBasePromptBans = () => {
    const settings = window.___userSettings746291937;
    const bans = settings?.galleryOptions?.bannedPromptPhrases;

    if (Array.isArray(bans)) return [...bans];

    if (bans?.selectAll) {
      return bans.selectAll.map(item => item.evaluateItem || item.getName);
    }

    return [];
  };

  const isVisibleFloatingGalleryFrame = frame => {
    const gallery = frame.closest('#chatIframe4');
    return !!gallery && !gallery.hidden && gallery.offsetParent !== null;
  };

  // Shared adaptive-height navigation guard for the floating Gallery.
  // Desktop and mobile use the same lifecycle: keep the previous iframe height
  // while a channel/search navigation is loading, then release it only after
  // the new adaptive height has stopped changing briefly.
  const galleryReloadState = new WeakMap();

  const beginGalleryReload = (frame, useUnifiedLoader) => {
    const gallery = frame.closest('#chatIframe4');

    if (!gallery || gallery.hidden || gallery.offsetParent === null) {
      return null;
    }

    const previousState = galleryReloadState.get(frame);
    previousState?.finish?.();

    const originalMinHeight = frame.style.minHeight;
    const stableHeight = Math.max(
      frame.offsetHeight || 0,
      Number.parseFloat(getComputedStyle(frame).height) || 0
    );

    // A different channel/search result starts at the top on every device.
    // This also prevents an old deep scrollTop from becoming invalid if the
    // adaptive iframe temporarily reports a much smaller loading document.
    gallery.scrollTop = 0;

    if (stableHeight > 0) {
      frame.style.minHeight = `${Math.ceil(stableHeight)}px`;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    let ready = false;
    let settleTimer = null;
    let timeoutTimer = null;
    let finished = false;
    let lastAdaptiveHeight = frame.style.height;

    const finish = () => {
      if (finished) return;
      finished = true;

      controller.abort();
      styleObserver.disconnect();
      if (settleTimer) clearTimeout(settleTimer);
      if (timeoutTimer) clearTimeout(timeoutTimer);

      if (galleryReloadState.get(frame)?.finish === finish) {
        galleryReloadState.delete(frame);
      }

      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        frame.style.minHeight = originalMinHeight;
        gallery.scrollTop = 0;
      });
    };

    const scheduleFinish = () => {
      if (!ready || finished) return;
      if (settleTimer) clearTimeout(settleTimer);

      // Adaptive-height can update several times as the new Gallery lays out.
      // Release only after those updates have been quiet for a short window.
      settleTimer = setTimeout(finish, 350);
    };

    const styleObserver = new MutationObserver(() => {
      const nextHeight = frame.style.height;
      if (nextHeight === lastAdaptiveHeight) return;
      lastAdaptiveHeight = nextHeight;
      scheduleFinish();
    });
    styleObserver.observe(frame, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['style'] });

    const readyEvent = useUnifiedLoader ? 'aifg:iframe-ready' : 'load';
    frame.addEventListener(readyEvent, () => {
      ready = true;
      scheduleFinish();
    }, { once: true, signal: controller.signal });

    // Failsafe only: never leave a temporary minimum height behind forever.
    timeoutTimer = setTimeout(finish, 20000);

    galleryReloadState.set(frame, { finish });
    return finish;
  };

  const changeAll = function(tagName, badTags, useUnifiedLoader = true) {
    if (tagName === undefined && badTags === undefined) return;

    const rawTagName = String(tagName || '');
    const rawBadTags = String(badTags || '');
    const formatTagName = formatSearchPattern(rawTagName);
    const formatBadTags = rawBadTags
      .split(',')
      .map(tag => tag.trim())
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map(formatSearchPattern)
      .filter(Boolean);

    const iframes = [...document.querySelectorAll('iframe.text-to-image-plugin-gallery')];
    const basePromptBans = readBasePromptBans();
    const contentFilter = typeof window.__getGalleryContentFilter === 'function'
      ? window.__getGalleryContentFilter()
      : 'pg13';

    localStorage.setItem('lookForThis', rawTagName);
    localStorage.setItem('dontLookForThis', rawBadTags);

    let transitionFrame = null;
    let transitionSrc = '';

    if (useUnifiedLoader && iframes.some(isVisibleFloatingGalleryFrame)) {
      window.aifgShowGalleryTransitionMask?.('Loading Gallery…', {
        persistent: true,
        minVisibleMs: 320
      });
    }

    for (const iframe of iframes) {
      let url;

      try {
        url = new URL(iframe.getAttribute('src') || iframe.src, window.location.href);
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn('Could not update Gallery iframe URL.', error);
        continue;
      }

      const dataObj = readGalleryData(url);
      const bannedTags = formatBadTags.map(tag => `/${tag}/i`);

      if (formatTagName) {
        bannedTags.unshift(`/^(?!.*(${formatTagName})).*$/i`);
      }

      dataObj.bannedPromptPhrases = [
        ...basePromptBans,
        ...bannedTags
      ];

      url.searchParams.set('subChannel', window.__t2iCurrentGallery || 'public');
      url.searchParams.set('contentFilter', contentFilter);
      url.hash = '#data=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(dataObj));

      const targetSrc = url.toString();
      const floatingFrame = isVisibleFloatingGalleryFrame(iframe);
      const finishGalleryReload = floatingFrame
        ? beginGalleryReload(iframe, useUnifiedLoader && typeof window.aifgLoadIframe === 'function')
        : null;

      if (useUnifiedLoader && typeof window.aifgLoadIframe === 'function') {
        const galleryLoaderHost = typeof window.__getGalleryLoaderHostForIframe === 'function'
          ? (window.__getGalleryLoaderHostForIframe(iframe) || iframe.parentElement)
          : iframe.parentElement;
        window.aifgLoadIframe(iframe, targetSrc, {
          label: 'Loading Gallery…',
          host: floatingFrame
            ? document.getElementById('embedCtn')
            : galleryLoaderHost,
          fullHost: true,
          readyDelayMs: 250
        });
      } else {
        iframe.setAttribute('src', targetSrc);
      }

      if (finishGalleryReload && iframe.dataset.aifgIframeReady === '1') {
        const readySrc = iframe.dataset.aifgReadySrc || '';
        try {
          if (new URL(readySrc, window.location.href).href === new URL(targetSrc, window.location.href).href) {
            setTimeout(() => finishGalleryReload(), 0);
          }
        } catch (error) {
          if (readySrc === targetSrc) setTimeout(() => finishGalleryReload(), 0);
        }
      }

      if (floatingFrame) {
        transitionFrame = iframe;
        transitionSrc = targetSrc;
      }
    }

    if (useUnifiedLoader && transitionFrame) {
      window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMaskWhenReady?.(
        transitionFrame,
        transitionSrc,
        {
          minVisibleMs: 320,
          timeoutMs: 20000
        }
      );
    } else if (useUnifiedLoader) {
      window.aifgHideGalleryTransitionMask?.(false);
    }
  };

  const applyOverride = () => {
    window.changeAll = changeAll;
    window.__aifgGalleryChangeAll = changeAll;
  };

  // Install now, then re-install exactly once after the imported framework has
  // had a chance to assign its own window.changeAll during startup.
  applyOverride();

  let didReassert = false;
  const reassertOnce = () => {
    if (didReassert) return;
    didReassert = true;
    applyOverride();
  };

  const startupFallback = setTimeout(reassertOnce, 750);

  const reassertAfterLoad = () => {
    setTimeout(() => {
      clearTimeout(startupFallback);
      reassertOnce();
    }, 500);
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
    reassertAfterLoad();
  } else {
    window.addEventListener('load', reassertAfterLoad, { once: true });
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   GLOBAL HELPERS (No Closures, Int Flags, Flat Scope)
   ============================================================ */
var YEET_cache = {}; // JIT Cache

function YEET_rst(r){ if(r.global||r.sticky) r.lastIndex=0; return r; }

// Compiler (JIT) - Returns {a:RegExp, b:Int}
// Keep this close to the original Perchance-compatible design.
function YEET_jit(k){
  if(YEET_cache[k]) return YEET_cache[k];

  const raw = jail.galleryYeet[k];

  // Do not cache empty if jail/galleryYeet is not ready.
  if(!raw) return [];

  const src =
    (raw.selectAll && raw.selectAll.map(a=>a.getName??a)) ||
    (Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : (raw.list ? raw.list : [raw]));

  YEET_cache[k] = src.map(x => {
    let re=null, t=0;
    const f=(fl)=>[...new Set((fl||'')+'is')].join('');

    if(x && typeof x==='object' && ('re' in x)){
      const s = x.re instanceof RegExp ? x.re.source : (x.re?.source||'');
      const fl = x.re instanceof RegExp ? x.re.flags  : (x.re?.flags||'');
      re = new RegExp(s, f(fl));
      t = (x.type==='S'?1:x.type==='P'?2:x.type==='R'?3:x.type==='B'?4:0);
    }
    else if(x instanceof RegExp){
      re = new RegExp(x.source, f(x.flags));
      t = 0;
    }
    else if(typeof x==='string'){
      const m = x.match(/^\{\s*re\s*:\s*(\/.+\/[a-z]*)\s*,\s*type\s*:?\s*'?([SNPRB])'?/i);
      if(m){
        const r = m[1].match(/^\/(.+)\/([a-z]*)$/i);
        re = new RegExp(r[1], f(r[2]));
        t = (m[2].toUpperCase()==='S'?1:m[2].toUpperCase()==='P'?2:m[2].toUpperCase()==='R'?3:m[2].toUpperCase()==='B'?4:0);
      } else {
        const n = x.match(/^\/(.+)\/([a-z]*)$/i);
        if(n){ re = new RegExp(n[1], f(n[2])); t=0; }
      }
    }

    return re ? {a:re, b:t} : {a:/$^/, b:0};
  });

  return YEET_cache[k];
}

// Cleaner (On-the-fly calculation)
function YEET_clnRe(r){
  const s=r.source;
  if((s.includes(".*")||s.includes(".+")) && !s.includes("(")){
    try{return new RegExp(s.split(/\.\*|\.\+/).filter(x=>x&&x!=='\\b').join("|"),"gi")}catch(e){return r}
  } return r;
}

function YEET_runCln(val, arr, type){
  if(!val) return val;
  let out = val;

  for(let i=0, l=arr.length; i<l; i++){
    const o = arr[i];
    if(o.b !== type) continue;

    YEET_rst(o.a);
    if(o.a.test(out)) out = out.replace(YEET_clnRe(o.a), " ");
  }

  return out.replace(/\s{2,}/g," ").trim();
}

function YEET_disp(r){
  const s=r.source;
  if((s.includes(".*")||s.includes(".+")) && !s.includes("(")){
    return s.split(/\.\*|\.\+/).filter(x=>x&&x!=='\\b').map(x=>x.replace(/\\b|\\/g,""));
  } return null;
}

function YEET_det(val, arr, type){
  if(!val) return [];
  const f = [];
  for(let i=0, l=arr.length; i<l; i++){
    const o = arr[i];
    if(o.b!==type) continue;
    YEET_rst(o.a);
    const m = val.match(o.a);
    if(m){
      const p = YEET_disp(o.a);
      if(p) f.push(p.join("..."));
      else {
        let t = m[0].replace(/[\r\n]+/g," ");
        if(t.length>20) t = t.slice(0,8)+"..."+t.slice(-8);
        f.push(t);
      }
    }
  }
  return [...new Set(f)];
}

function YEET_has(val, arr, type){
  if(!val) return false;
  for(let i=0, l=arr.length; i<l; i++){
    const o = arr[i];
    if(o.b===type){ YEET_rst(o.a); if(o.a.test(val)) return true; }
  }
  return false;
}

}

var YEET_cnt=0, YEET_sup=false, YEET_supR=false;
var YEET_nCurseKey="_gallery_n_curse_v1";
var YEET_nClearCodesCache=null;

function YEET_nCursed(){
  try{return localStorage.getItem(YEET_nCurseKey)==="1"}catch(_){return false}
}

function YEET_setNCursed(on){
  try{
    if(on) localStorage.setItem(YEET_nCurseKey,"1");
    else localStorage.removeItem(YEET_nCurseKey);
  }catch(_){}
}

function YEET_getNClearCodes(){
  if(YEET_nClearCodesCache!==null) return YEET_nClearCodesCache;

  const raw=jail.galleryYeet.clearCodes;

  // Do not cache before the imported banlist is available.
  if(!raw) return [];

  const src=
    (raw.selectAll&&raw.selectAll.map(a=>a.getName??a))||
    (Array.isArray(raw)?raw:(raw.list?raw.list:[raw]));

  YEET_nClearCodesCache=[...new Set(
    src
      .map(value=>String(value??"").trim())
      .filter(Boolean)
  )];

  return YEET_nClearCodesCache;
}

function YEET_isNClearCode(val){
  const code=String(val??"").trim();
  if(!code) return false;

  const clearCodes=YEET_getNClearCodes();
  return clearCodes.length>0&&clearCodes.includes(code);
}

/* ============================================================
   DIRECT T2I GENERATE GATE
   ============================================================ */
function YEET_setInput(el, val){
  if(!el) return;

  el.value = val || "";

  if(el.oninput) {
    el.oninput({ target: el, currentTarget: el, _isInitCall: false });
  } else {
    el.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
  }
}

window.YEET_beforeGenerate746291937 = function(event, runGenerate){
  const q = (s) => document.querySelector(s);

  const taD = q('textarea[data-name="description"]');
  const taN = q('textarea[data-name="negative"]');
  const ti  = q('input[data-name="usertitle"]');
  const taU = q('textarea[data-name="userdescription"]');

  const v = {
    d: taD ? taD.value || "" : "",
    n: taN ? taN.value || "" : "",
    t: ti  ? ti.value  || "" : "",
    u: taU ? taU.value || "" : ""
  };

  const clearD = YEET_isNClearCode(v.d);
  const clearN = YEET_isNClearCode(v.n);
  const clearT = YEET_isNClearCode(v.t);
  const clearU = YEET_isNClearCode(v.u);

  // A Gallery N curse blocks every generation attempt until the exact clear code is submitted.
  if(YEET_nCursed()){
    if(clearD || clearN || clearT || clearU){
      YEET_setNCursed(false);
      if(clearD) YEET_setInput(taD, "");
      if(clearN) YEET_setInput(taN, "");
      if(clearT) YEET_setInput(ti,  "");
      if(clearU) YEET_setInput(taU, "");
      YEET_pClear();
      return;
    }

    YEET_p2();
    return;
  }

  // Blank generate should not run Yeet.
  if(!(v.d.trim() || v.n.trim() || v.t.trim() || v.u.trim())) {
    return runGenerate();
  }

  const R = {
    d: v.d ? YEET_jit("desc")  : [],
    n: v.n ? YEET_jit("neg")   : [],
    t: v.t ? YEET_jit("title") : [],
    u: v.u ? YEET_jit("userd") : []
  };

  // Type N: hard block and persist the Gallery N curse.
  const N = [
    ...YEET_det(v.d, R.d, 0),
    ...YEET_det(v.n, R.n, 0),
    ...YEET_det(v.t, R.t, 0),
    ...YEET_det(v.u, R.u, 0)
  ];

  if(N.length){
    YEET_setNCursed(true);
    YEET_p2(N);
    return;
  }

  // Type B: restricted / legacy only.
  const B = [
    ...YEET_det(v.d, R.d, 4),
    ...YEET_det(v.n, R.n, 4),
    ...YEET_det(v.t, R.t, 4),
    ...YEET_det(v.u, R.u, 4)
  ];

  if(B.length){
    YEET_pB(B);
    return;
  }

  // Type S: clean first, then generate directly.
  const S = [
    ...YEET_det(v.d, R.d, 1),
    ...YEET_det(v.n, R.n, 1),
    ...YEET_det(v.t, R.t, 1),
    ...YEET_det(v.u, R.u, 1)
  ];

  if(S.length){
    YEET_p1(S, () => {
      if(v.d) YEET_setInput(taD, YEET_runCln(v.d, R.d, 1));
      if(v.n) YEET_setInput(taN, YEET_runCln(v.n, R.n, 1));
      if(v.t) YEET_setInput(ti,  YEET_runCln(v.t, R.t, 1));
      if(v.u) YEET_setInput(taU, YEET_runCln(v.u, R.u, 1));
      runGenerate();
    });
    return;
  }

  // Type P: gallery warning.
  const P = [
    ...YEET_det(v.d, R.d, 2),
    ...YEET_det(v.n, R.n, 2),
    ...YEET_det(v.t, R.t, 2),
    ...YEET_det(v.u, R.u, 2)
  ];

  if(P.length){
    if(YEET_sup) return runGenerate();

    YEET_p3(P, () => {
      if(v.d) YEET_setInput(taD, YEET_runCln(v.d, R.d, 2));
      if(v.n) YEET_setInput(taN, YEET_runCln(v.n, R.n, 2));
      if(v.t) YEET_setInput(ti,  YEET_runCln(v.t, R.t, 2));
      if(v.u) YEET_setInput(taU, YEET_runCln(v.u, R.u, 2));
      runGenerate();
    }, () => {
      runGenerate();
    });

    return;
  }

  // Type R: gentle reminder.
  const R_tags = [
    ...YEET_det(v.d, R.d, 3),
    ...YEET_det(v.n, R.n, 3),
    ...YEET_det(v.t, R.t, 3),
    ...YEET_det(v.u, R.u, 3)
  ];

  if(R_tags.length){
    if(YEET_supR) return runGenerate();
    YEET_p4(R_tags, () => runGenerate());
    return;
  }

  return runGenerate();
};

</script>
<!-- ===== The Yeet YeExile System ===== -->

<!-- ==== Clipboards Modal (normal container, no iframe) ==== -->
<style>
  .clip-tab, .clip-add {
    display:flex; 
    align-items:center; 
    gap: 0px;
    height:1.5rem;
    margin: 0px 0.5px;
    padding: 0px 8px;
    border-radius:0.3rem;
    background: var(--background-color);
    min-width: max-content;    
    color: var(--text-color);
    opacity: 0.8;
    font:70% "Open Sans", system-ui, sans-serif;
    font-optical-sizing: auto;
    font-weight:500;
    font-style: normal;
    font-variation-settings: "wdth" 100;
    cursor:pointer; user-select:none;
  }
  .clip-add {
    padding: 0px 8.8px;
    border: 0 !important;
    border-style: none !important;
  }
  .clip-tab.active {
    border: 1.5px solid rgba(0,134,201,0.8) !important;
    font-weight:500 !important;
    opacity: 1.0;
  }
  #clipTabs::-webkit-scrollbar { height:4px; }
  #clipTabs::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border-color); border-radius:4px; }
  /* In Edit Mode: Make auto-detected links look like normal text */
  #clipEditor[contenteditable="true"] a {
    color: inherit;           /* Use the 80% opacity text color */
    text-decoration: none;    /* Remove the underline */
    cursor: text;             /* Use the text cursor, not the hand */
    pointer-events: none;     /* Stop it from being clickable */
  }
  
  /* In Preview Mode: Make all links look like actual, clickable links */
  #clipEditor[contenteditable="false"] a {
    color: inherit;           /* Use the 80% opacity text color */
    text-decoration: underline;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
</style>

<script>
(function(){
  const STORE_KEY='clipboards.v3';
  let state=null,saveTimer=null;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const editor = $('clipEditor');

  function loadState(){
    try{
      const raw=localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY);
      if(raw){const o=JSON.parse(raw);if(o&&Array.isArray(o.tabs))return o;}
    }catch{}
    return{tabs:[{name:'page1',html:''}],active:0};
  }
  const saveState=()=>{clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=setTimeout(()=>localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state)),400);};

  function renderTabs(){
    const box=$('clipTabs'); box.innerHTML='';
    state.tabs.forEach((t,i)=>{
      const tab=document.createElement('div');
      tab.className='clip-tab'+(i===state.active?' active':'');
      tab.onclick=()=>setActive(i);
      const name=document.createElement('span'); name.textContent=t.name; tab.appendChild(name);
      if (i === state.active) {
        const dot = document.createElement('div');
        dot.style.display = "inline-block";
        dot.style.paddingLeft = "6px";
        dot.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-ellipsis-vertical fa-xs clip-ellipsis"></i>';
        dot.onclick = e => { e.stopPropagation(); showMenu(dot,i); };
        tab.appendChild(dot);
      }  
      box.appendChild(tab);
    });
    const add=document.createElement('button');
    add.className='clip-add'; add.textContent='+';
    add.onclick=addTab; box.appendChild(add);
    $('clipEditor').innerHTML=state.tabs[state.active].html;
  }

  const nextName=()=>{let n=1;const s=new Set(state.tabs.map(t=>t.name));while(s.has('page'+n))n++;return'page'+n;};
  const addTab=()=>{state.tabs.push({name:nextName(),html:''});state.active=state.tabs.length-1;saveState();renderTabs();focusEnd();};
  const setActive=i=>{state.tabs[state.active].html=$('clipEditor').innerHTML;state.active=i;saveState();renderTabs();focusEnd();};
  const focusEnd=()=>{const ed=$('clipEditor');ed.focus();const r=document.createRange();r.selectNodeContents(ed);r.collapse(false);const s=window.getSelection();s.removeAllRanges();s.addRange(r);};

  const showMenu=(anchor,i)=>{
    let m=$('clipMenu'); if(m) m.remove();
    m=document.createElement('div'); m.id='clipMenu';
    Object.assign(m.style,{position:'fixed',zIndex:'1000000',background:'var(--embedctn-bg-color)',color:'var(--text-color)',
      border:'1px solid var(--floatingpanel-border-color)',borderRadius:'8px',padding:'6px',
      font:'500 12px Open Sans, sans-serif',boxShadow:'0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.25)'});
    const r=anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
    m.style.left=(r.right+6)+'px'; m.style.top=(r.top-6)+'px';
    m.innerHTML=`<button style="display:block;padding:6px 10px;width:100%;background:transparent;border:none;text-align:left;cursor:pointer;">Rename</button>
                 <button style="display:block;padding:6px 10px;width:100%;background:transparent;border:none;text-align:left;cursor:pointer;">Delete</button>`;
    document.body.appendChild(m);
    const [re,de]=m.querySelectorAll('button');
    re.onclick=()=>{const v=prompt('Rename page:',state.tabs[i].name);if(!v){m.remove();return;}
      if(state.tabs.some((t,x)=>t.name===v&&x!==i))alert('Name already exists.');
      else{state.tabs[i].name=v;saveState();renderTabs();}m.remove();};
    de.onclick=()=>{if(!confirm('Delete this page? Cannot undo.')){m.remove();return;}
      if(state.tabs.length===1){alert('At least one page required.');m.remove();return;}
      state.tabs.splice(i,1);if(state.active>=state.tabs.length)state.active=state.tabs.length-1;saveState();renderTabs();m.remove();};
    const closer=e=>{if(!m.contains(e.target)){m.remove();document.removeEventListener('mousedown',closer,true);} };
    setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener('mousedown',closer,true),0);
  };

  const linkifyHTML = (html) => {
    // Regex to find all links (http, https, www)
    const urlRegex = /((?:https?:\/\/|www\.)[^\s<]+)/g;
    // Regex to check if a URL is an image
    const imgRegex = /\.(jpe?g|png|gif|webp)(?=[?#]|$)/i;
    return html.replace(urlRegex, (match, url) => {
      // Create the proper href
      const href = url.startsWith('www.') ? 'http://' + url : url;
      // 1. Create the link.
      // (This answers your Goal 2: The styling is applied by your CSS rule at [source 511])
      const link = `<a href="${href}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${url}</a>`;
      // 2. Check if the URL is an image
      if (imgRegex.test(url)) {
        // If yes, append the image preview
        return link + ` <img src="${href}" style="max-width: 48px; max-height: 48px; vertical-align: bottom;">`;
      }
      // If not, just return the link
      return link;
    });
  };
  
  window.clip_onEdit = () => {
    if (editor.contentEditable === 'false') return; // ⛔ block preview mode autosave
    state.tabs[state.active].html = editor.innerHTML;
    saveState();
  };
  window.clip_linkify=()=>{const ed=$('clipEditor');const a=linkifyHTML(ed.innerHTML);if(a!==ed.innerHTML){ed.innerHTML=a;state.tabs[state.active].html=a;saveState();focusEnd();}};
  window.clip_copy=async()=>{try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText($('clipEditor').innerText);}catch{}const i=$('clipCopyIcon');i.className='fa-solid fa-check';setTimeout(()=>i.className='fa-regular fa-copy',2000);};
  // --- THIS IS THE CORRECTED CLICK LISTENER ---
  // This forces links to be clickable in "preview" mode
  editor.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    // Check if the editor is in preview mode AND the user clicked a link
    if (editor.contentEditable === 'false' && e.target.tagName === 'A') {
      e.preventDefault(); // Stop the browser from doing anything else
      const href = e.target.getAttribute('href');
      if (href) {
        // Opens the link in a new tab
        window.open(href, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
      }
    }
  });
  
  // --- BLOCK IMAGE PASTE ---
  editor.addEventListener('paste', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const text = e.clipboardData.getData('text/plain') || '';
    document.execCommand('insertText', false, text);
  });

  // --- preview tap x2 size ---
  let zoomedImg = null;
  
  editor.addEventListener('click', e => {
    if (editor.contentEditable === 'false' && e.target.tagName === 'IMG') {
  
      // already zoomed? ignore, global click handler will close it
      if (!zoomedImg) {
        zoomedImg = e.target;
        zoomedImg.classList.add('zoomed');
        zoomedImg.style.transform = 'scale(4)';
        zoomedImg.style.position = 'relative';
        zoomedImg.style.zIndex = '9999';
        zoomedImg.style.transition = 'transform 0.05s ease';
      }
  
      e.stopPropagation(); // prevent immediate close
    }
  });
  
  // click ANYWHERE → restore
  document.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (zoomedImg) {
      zoomedImg.style.transform = '';
      zoomedImg.style.position = '';
      zoomedImg.style.zIndex = '';
      zoomedImg.classList.remove('zoomed');
      zoomedImg = null;
    }
  });
  
  // This function controls the new Edit/View mode toggle (FIXED)
  window.toggleLinkMode = () => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('clipLinkToggleBtn');
    const icon = btn.querySelector('span');  // your existing <span> icon container
    const isEditMode = editor.contentEditable === 'true';
  
    if (isEditMode) {
      // --- SWITCH TO PREVIEW MODE ---
      
      // 1. Save original rich HTML
      editor.dataset.editHtml = editor.innerHTML;
  
      // 2. Clean HTML (remove <a> tags)
      const cleanHtml = editor.innerHTML.replace(/<a\b[^>]*>(.*?)<\/a>/gi, '$1');
  
      // 3. Linkify clean HTML
      const viewHtml = linkifyHTML(cleanHtml);
  
      // 4. Replace editor contents & lock editing
      editor.innerHTML = viewHtml;
      editor.contentEditable = 'false';
  
      // 5. SWITCH ICON → pen
      btn.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-pencil" style="font-size:0.75rem;opacity:0.7;"></i>`;
      btn.title = "Switch to Edit Mode";
  
    } else {
      // --- SWITCH BACK TO EDIT MODE ---
  
      // 1. Restore original HTML
      const editHtml = editor.dataset.editHtml || editor.innerText;
      editor.innerHTML = editHtml;
      editor.dataset.editHtml = '';
  
      // 2. Enable editing
      editor.contentEditable = 'true';
  
      // 3. SWITCH ICON 
      btn.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-regular fa-eye" style="font-size:0.75rem;opacity:0.7;"></i>`;
      btn.title = "Switch to Preview Mode";
  
      // 4. Put cursor back
      editor.focus();
    }
  };

  /* === integrated with shared floating panel === */
  // PASTE THIS NEW FUNCTION
  window.openClipboards=()=>{
    if(!state)state=loadState();
    renderTabs();
    
    if (editor.contentEditable === 'false') {
      const cleanHtml = editor.dataset.editHtml || editor.innerText;
      const viewHtml = linkifyHTML(cleanHtml);
      editor.innerHTML = viewHtml;
    }

    document.getElementById('menuCtn').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('embedCtn').hidden = false;
  
    // Hide all other panels inside embedCtn
    document.getElementById('chatContainer3').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('chatIframe4').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('uploaderIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('tldrawIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('expandTldrawBtn').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('furaiCtn').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('expandAIAssistantBtn').hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('expandFurPostBtn')) document.getElementById('expandFurPostBtn').hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('furPostIframe')) document.getElementById('furPostIframe').hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('expandFurdexSidebarBtn')) document.getElementById('expandFurdexSidebarBtn').hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('furdexSidebarIframe')) document.getElementById('furdexSidebarIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('infopanel').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('imageEditIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('expandImageEditBtn').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('imageToVideoIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('expandImageToVideoBtn').hidden = true;
    
    // --- HIDE CUSTOM APPS ---
    CUSTOM_APP_HIDE_IDS.forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.hidden = true;
    });
    // ------------------------
    
    document.getElementById('clipboardsPanel').style.display = 'flex';
  
    updateFloatingBtnIcon();
    toggleBackToTopBtn();
  };
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => {
    if (!state) return;
    if (editor.contentEditable === 'false') return; // ⛔ block preview save
    state.tabs[state.active].html = editor.innerHTML;
    localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
  });
})();
</script>

  .icon-select-item:hover { background: var(--pagebtn-color-hover, #444); }
