  window.showStatusTooltip = window.showStatusTooltip || (() => {
    let activeTip = null;

    return msg => {
      if (activeTip) activeTip.remove();

      const tip = document.createElement("div");
      activeTip = tip;
      tip.innerText = msg;
      tip.style = `
        bottom:20px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);position:fixed;
        background:#222;color:#fff;padding:8px 14px;
        border:1px solid var(--floatingpanel-border-color);
        border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;z-index:99999;
        opacity:0;transition:opacity 0.3s ease;
      `;

      document.body.appendChild(tip);
      requestAnimationFrame(() => tip.style.opacity = "1");

      setTimeout(() => {
        if (tip === activeTip) tip.style.opacity = "0";
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (tip === activeTip) {
            tip.remove();
            activeTip = null;
          }
        }, 300);
      }, 3000);
    };
  })();

  function updateColorSchemeButton() {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const ds = (root.dataset.scheme || '').toLowerCase();
    const colorScheme = root.style.colorScheme || 'light';
    const followsSystem = typeof window.isFollowingSystemColorScheme === 'function'
      && window.isFollowingSystemColorScheme();
    const mode = ['light', 'grey', 'dark'].includes(root.dataset.schemeMode)
      ? root.dataset.schemeMode
      : (['light', 'grey', 'dark'].includes(localStorage.getItem('forceColorScheme'))
        ? localStorage.getItem('forceColorScheme')
        : 'light');

    const modeLabel = followsSystem
      ? 'Auto (follow browser setting)'
      : (typeof window.getColorSchemeModeLabel === 'function'
        ? window.getColorSchemeModeLabel(mode)
        : (mode === 'light' ? 'Light theme' : mode === 'grey' ? 'Read mode' : 'Dark theme'));

    let iconHTML;
    if (followsSystem) {
      iconHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-size:1.05rem;">hdr_auto</span>';
    } else if (mode === 'dark') {
      iconHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-size:1rem;display:inline-block;transform:rotate(-30deg) translateY(1px) translateX(0);">nightlight</span>';
    } else if (mode === 'grey') {
      iconHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-size:1rem;">scene</span>';
    } else {
      iconHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-size:1rem;">light_mode</span>';
    }

    colorSchemeBtn.innerHTML = iconHTML;
    colorSchemeBtn.title = modeLabel;

    const schemeForGallery = (ds === 'dark' || colorScheme === 'dark') ? 'dark' : 'light';
  
    infopanel.style.backgroundColor = schemeForGallery === 'light' ? 'white' : '#131516';
    settings.galleryOptions.forceColorScheme = schemeForGallery;

    const tIn = document.getElementById('galleryTagInputEl');
    const bIn = document.getElementById('galleryBadTagInputEl');
    const hasSearch = (tIn && tIn.value.trim() !== "") || (bIn && bIn.value.trim() !== "");

    if (hasSearch && typeof window.changeAll === 'function') {
        // Re-trigger search to rebuild gallery with BOTH the preserved search filters and the new theme
        window.changeAll(tIn.value, bIn.value, false);
    } else {
        // No search active: Update the gallery theme dynamically via URL manipulation
        const galleryCtn = document.getElementById('imageGalleryCtn8569284');
        const iframes = galleryCtn ? galleryCtn.querySelectorAll("iframe") : [];
        iframes.forEach(iframe => {
            const url = new URL(iframe.src);
            const hash = url.hash;
            if (hash) {
                const rawData = decodeURIComponent(hash).replace(/^#data=/, "").replace(/^"/, "").replace(/"$/, "");
                try {
                    const dataObj = JSON.parse(rawData);
                    dataObj.forceColorScheme = schemeForGallery;
                    url.hash = "#data=" + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(dataObj));
                } catch(e) {}
            }
            // Keep theme switching on the gallery's original direct navigation path.
            iframe.src = url.toString();
        });
    }
    
    const chatIframe4 = document.getElementById('chatIframe4');
    if (chatIframe4) {
      update(chatIframe4);
    }
  }

  document.getElementById('colorSchemeBtn').addEventListener('click', function () {
    const btn = this;

    observer.disconnect();

    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.style.opacity = '0.5';

    
    document.getElementById('darkModeBtn_875979f948')?.click();

    const selectedMode = document.documentElement.dataset.schemeMode || 'light';
    const selectedModeLabel = typeof window.getColorSchemePickerLabel === 'function'
      ? window.getColorSchemePickerLabel()
      : (selectedMode === 'light' ? 'Light theme' : selectedMode === 'grey' ? 'Read mode' : 'Dark theme');
    window.showStatusTooltip(selectedModeLabel);

    setTimeout(() => {
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.style.opacity = '';
      
      updateColorSchemeButton(); 

      observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
        attributes: true,
        attributeFilter: ['style', 'data-scheme', 'data-scheme-mode']
      });
    }, 500);
  });

  const observer = new MutationObserver(updateColorSchemeButton);
  
  observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
    attributes: true,
    attributeFilter: ['style', 'data-scheme', 'data-scheme-mode']
  });

  function toggleFullscreen() {
    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
      document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
    } else {
      if (document.exitFullscreen) {
        document.exitFullscreen();
      }
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', function() {
    fullscreenBtn.innerHTML = document.fullscreenElement
      ? '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-size: 1.2rem">collapse_content</span>'
      : '<span class="material-symbols-outlined" style="font-size: 1rem">pan_zoom</span>';
  });

  function toggleEmbedCtn() {
    const guidePanel = document.getElementById('infopanel');
    const ctn = document.getElementById('embedCtn');

    if (!ctn.hidden && !guidePanel.hidden) {
        toggleEmbedCtnState(false); 
        return;
    }

    toggleEmbedCtnState(true);
  }

  function toggleEmbedCtnState(shouldOpen) {
    const ctn = document.getElementById('embedCtn');
    
    if (!shouldOpen) {
      const gf = document.getElementById('guideIframe');
      if (gf) window.aifgUnloadIframe(gf); 
      
      ctn.hidden = true;
      menuCtn.style.display = 'flex'; 
      document.getElementById('expandGuideBtn').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('clipboardsPanel').style.display = 'none';
      
    } else {
      hidePerchanceMakerView();

      ctn.hidden = false;
      menuCtn.style.display = 'none';

      ctn.style.width = '25rem';
      ctn.style.height = '75dvh';
      ctn.style.maxWidth = '';  
      ctn.style.maxHeight = ''; 
      ctn.style.top = 'auto';
      ctn.style.left = 'auto';
      ctn.style.right = '0.75rem';
      ctn.style.bottom = '3.8rem';

      isGuideExpanded = false;
      document.getElementById('expandGuideBtn').innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-expand fa-xs fa-fw" style="margin-right:0.5rem; position: relative; top: 0.5px;"></i><span style="font-size:90%">Expand</span>';

      document.getElementById('chatContainer3').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('chatIframe4').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('uploaderIframe').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('tldrawIframe').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('expandTldrawBtn').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('furaiCtn').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('expandAIAssistantBtn').hidden = true;
      if(document.getElementById('expandFurPostBtn')) document.getElementById('expandFurPostBtn').hidden = true;
      if(document.getElementById('furPostIframe')) document.getElementById('furPostIframe').hidden = true;
      if(document.getElementById('expandFurdexSidebarBtn')) document.getElementById('expandFurdexSidebarBtn').hidden = true;
      if(document.getElementById('furdexSidebarIframe')) document.getElementById('furdexSidebarIframe').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('imageEditIframe').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('expandImageEditBtn').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('expandUploaderBtn').hidden = true; 
      document.getElementById('imageToVideoIframe').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('expandImageToVideoBtn').hidden = true;

      // --- HIDE CUSTOM APPS ---
      CUSTOM_APP_HIDE_IDS.forEach(id => {
          const el = document.getElementById(id);
          if (el) el.hidden = true;
      });
      // ------------------------

      document.getElementById('infopanel').hidden = false;
      document.getElementById('expandGuideBtn').hidden = false;

      const gf = document.getElementById('guideIframe');
      if (gf) {
        window.aifgLoadIframe(gf, window.getAifgGuideUrl(), {
          label: 'Loading Guide…',
          host: document.getElementById('infopanel'),
          fullHost: true
        });
      }
    }
    updateFloatingBtnIcon();
    toggleBackToTopBtn();
  }

  function scrollToTop() {
    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: 'smooth'
    });
  }

  function toggleBackToTopBtn() {
    if (window.scrollY > 800 && embedCtn.hidden && menuCtn.style.display === 'none' && isScrollingUp) {
      backToTopBtn.style.display = 'flex';
      backToTopBtn.style.opacity = '1';
      window.syncActionDockBackButton?.();
    } else {
      backToTopBtn.style.opacity = '0';
      window.syncActionDockBackButton?.();
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (backToTopBtn.style.opacity === '0') {
          backToTopBtn.style.display = 'none';
        }
        window.syncActionDockBackButton?.();
      }, 100);
    }
  }

  let scrollTimeout = null; 

  window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
    if (scrollTimeout) return;

    scrollTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      let st = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
      
      isScrollingUp = st < lastScrollTop;
      lastScrollTop = st <= 0 ? 0 : st;
      
      toggleBackToTopBtn();
      
      scrollTimeout = null;
    }, 100); 
  });

  toggleBackToTopBtn();
</script>

<!-- loadUploader floating panel -->    
<script>
function loadUploader() {
    hidePerchanceMakerView();

    menuCtn.style.display = 'none';
    embedCtn.hidden = false;

    // Hide other tools
    document.getElementById('chatContainer3').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('chatIframe4').hidden = true;
    tldrawIframe.hidden = true;
    expandTldrawBtn.hidden = true;
    furaiCtn.hidden = true;
    infopanel.hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('expandGuideBtn').hidden = true;
    expandAIAssistantBtn.hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('expandFurPostBtn')) document.getElementById('expandFurPostBtn').hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('furPostIframe')) document.getElementById('furPostIframe').hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('expandFurdexSidebarBtn')) document.getElementById('expandFurdexSidebarBtn').hidden = true;
    if(document.getElementById('furdexSidebarIframe')) document.getElementById('furdexSidebarIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('imageEditIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('expandImageEditBtn').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('imageToVideoIframe').hidden = true;
    document.getElementById('expandImageToVideoBtn').hidden = true;
    
    // --- HIDE CUSTOM APPS ---
    CUSTOM_APP_HIDE_IDS.forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.hidden = true;
    });
    // ------------------------

    document.getElementById('expandUploaderBtn').hidden = false;

    handleAppOpen('uploader');

    const uploaderIframe = document.getElementById('uploaderIframe');

    uploaderIframe.hidden = false;
    window.aifgLoadIframe(
      uploaderIframe,
      "https://perchance.org/upload",
      { label: "Loading File Uploader…" }
    );

    updateFloatingBtnIcon();
    toggleBackToTopBtn();
  }
</script>
    
<script>
// Purpose: generate shortcut button with spinner effect and restore SVG after delay
document.getElementById('generateBtn').onclick = function () {
  const wrapper = document.getElementById('generateIconWrapper');
  const svg = wrapper.querySelector('svg');

  if (!svg) return;

  svg.style.display = 'none';

  const spinner = document.createElement('i');
  spinner.className = 'fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin';
  spinner.id = 'generateSpinner';
  wrapper.appendChild(spinner);

  this.disabled = true;
  this.style.opacity = '0.5';
  document.getElementById('generateButtonEl')?.click();

  setTimeout(() => {
    spinner.remove();
    svg.style.display = 'block';
    this.disabled = false;
    this.style.opacity = '';
  }, 3000);
};
</script>

<script>
document.getElementById('generateBtn').onclick = function () {
  const wrapper = document.getElementById('generateIconWrapper');
  const originalIcon = wrapper.innerHTML;

  wrapper.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-spinner fa-spin"></i>`;
  this.disabled = true;
  this.style.opacity = '0.5';

  document.getElementById('generateButtonEl')?.click();

  setTimeout(() => {
    wrapper.innerHTML = originalIcon;
    this.disabled = false;
    this.style.opacity = '';
  }, 3000);
};
</script>

}

<script>
// Purpose: toggle notification/do not disturb
const toggleBtn = document.getElementById("notifyToggleBtn");
const icon = toggleBtn.querySelector("span");
const STORAGE_KEY = "perch_notify_mode";

// Load saved mode or fallback
let mode = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
if (!["off", "ad_off", "norefresh", "all"].includes(mode)) mode = "all";

// Apply mode
const applyMode = () => {
  // Master switch (kills EVERYTHING if false, used for DND)
  window.perchNotifyToggle = mode !== "off";

  // Text alerts (Mentions, Awoo, Announcements)
  window.perchTextAlertsToggle = mode === "all" || mode === "ad_off";

  // Link Previews
  window.perchLinkPreviewToggle = mode === "all" || mode === "norefresh";

  // Auto-refresh & Heavy Mod popups
  window.perchForceRefreshToggle = mode === "all";
};

// Reuse the same status hint used by the theme toggle.
const showStatusTooltip = window.showStatusTooltip;
// On click: switch to next mode
toggleBtn.onclick = () => {
  mode =
    mode === "all" ? "norefresh" :
    mode === "norefresh" ? "ad_off" :
    mode === "ad_off" ? "off" :
    "all";

  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, mode);
  applyMode();

  showStatusTooltip(
    mode === "all" ? "All notifications enabled" :
    mode === "norefresh" ? "Link previews only (no announcement)" :
    mode === "ad_off" ? "Text notifications only (no link pop-ups)" :
    "Do Not Disturb mode (all disabled)"
  );

  updateIcon();
};

// Update icon and title for current mode
const updateIcon = () => {
  icon.innerText =
    mode === "all" ? "notifications_active" :
    mode === "norefresh" ? "voice_over_off" :
    mode === "ad_off" ? "ad_off" :
    "do_not_disturb_on";

  toggleBtn.title =
    mode === "all" ? "Switch to Link previews only mode" :
    mode === "norefresh" ? "Switch to Text notifications only mode" :
    mode === "ad_off" ? "Switch to Do Not Disturb mode" :
    "Enable all notifications";
};

// Init
applyMode();
updateIcon();
</script>
    
<script>
