// after page load, to keep the URL clean for users.
// This does NOT affect Google Analytics tracking — GA4 already captures it.

if (window.location.search.includes('utm_')) {
  window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
}
</script>

<script>
// --- MASTER PRIVACY & DATA MANAGEMENT SYSTEM ---
// Logic Source: Aggressive DB/Cache Clearing (Code A)
// Design Source: Friendly/Standard UI (Code B)

(function() {
    const KEYS = { USER: 'pl_user', PASS: 'pl_pass', LOCK: 'pl_lock', AVATAR: 'pl_avatar', TITLE: 'pl_gen_name' };
    const PRIVACY_GATE_NOTICE_ACK_KEY = 'pl_privacy_gate_notice_ack';

    window.updatePrivacyGateNoticeVisibility = function() {
        const notice = document.getElementById('privacyGateNotice');
        if (!notice) return;

        let dismissed = false;
        try {
            dismissed = localStorage.getItem(PRIVACY_GATE_NOTICE_ACK_KEY) === 'true';
        } catch (e) {}

        notice.style.display = dismissed ? 'none' : 'block';
    };

    window.dismissPrivacyGateNotice = function() {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(PRIVACY_GATE_NOTICE_ACK_KEY, 'true');
        } catch (e) {}

        const notice = document.getElementById('privacyGateNotice');
        if (notice) notice.style.display = 'none';
    };

    const normalizeEntryGateCredentialValue = value => String(value || '').trim();

    const getEntryGateCredentialParts = kind => {
        if (kind === 'uid') {
            return {
                main: document.getElementById('accUidField'),
                confirm: document.getElementById('accUidConfirmField'),
                wrap: document.getElementById('accUidConfirmWrap')
            };
        }

        return {
            main: document.getElementById('accSafeCodeField'),
            confirm: document.getElementById('accSafeCodeConfirmField'),
            wrap: document.getElementById('accSafeCodeConfirmWrap')
        };
    };

    const aifgEntryGateCredentialState = {
        savedUid: '',
        confirmedUid: '',
        confirmedDeleteCode: ''
    };

    const syncEntryGateCredentialConfirmation = kind => {
        const state = aifgEntryGateCredentialState;
        const { main, confirm, wrap } = getEntryGateCredentialParts(kind);
        if (!main || !confirm || !wrap) return false;

        const value = normalizeEntryGateCredentialValue(main.value);
        const confirmedValue = kind === 'uid'
            ? state.confirmedUid
            : state.confirmedDeleteCode;
        const isPreviouslyConfirmed = kind === 'uid'
            ? (value === state.savedUid || value === confirmedValue)
            : value === confirmedValue;

        if (!value || isPreviouslyConfirmed) {
            wrap.hidden = true;
            confirm.value = '';
            confirm.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
            return false;
        }

        const confirmation = normalizeEntryGateCredentialValue(confirm.value);

        if (confirmation && confirmation === value) {
            if (kind === 'uid') state.confirmedUid = value;
            else state.confirmedDeleteCode = value;

            wrap.hidden = true;
            confirm.value = '';
            confirm.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
            return false;
        }

        wrap.hidden = false;
        confirm.setAttribute('aria-invalid', confirmation ? 'true' : 'false');
        return true;
    };

    window.getEntryGateCredentialValidation = function() {
        const uidPending = syncEntryGateCredentialConfirmation('uid');
        const deleteCodePending = syncEntryGateCredentialConfirmation('deleteCode');
        const uid = normalizeEntryGateCredentialValue(
            document.getElementById('accUidField')?.value
        );
        const isLocked = !!document.getElementById('lockModeToggle')?.checked;

        let message = '';
        let warning = false;

        if (uidPending && deleteCodePending) {
            message = 'Confirm the changed Account Name and Delete Code to save.';
        } else if (uidPending) {
            message = 'Confirm the changed Account Name to save.';
        } else if (deleteCodePending) {
            message = 'Confirm the changed Delete Code to save.';
        } else if (isLocked && !uid) {
            warning = true;
            message = 'Account Name is empty. The entry gate will be a welcome screen anyone can enter. Are you sure?';
        }

        return {
            valid: !uidPending && !deleteCodePending,
            warning,
            message,
            uidPending,
            deleteCodePending
        };
    };

    window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation = function() {
        const result = window.getEntryGateCredentialValidation();
        const status = document.getElementById('entryGateCredentialStatus');
        const saveButton = document.getElementById('accountSaveSettingsBtn');

        if (saveButton) saveButton.disabled = !result.valid;

        if (status) {
            status.classList.toggle('is-warning', result.valid && result.warning);
            status.classList.toggle('is-error', !result.valid);
            status.textContent = result.message;
            status.hidden = !result.message;
        }

        return result.valid;
    };

    const AIFG_ACCOUNT_SECTION_STATE_KEY = 'aifg_account_section_state_v1';
    const AIFG_ACCOUNT_SCROLL_STATE_KEY = 'aifg_account_scroll_top_v1';

    let aifgAccountScrollSaveTimer = null;
    let aifgAccountViewportRestoreFrame = 0;

    function getAifgAccountScrollBody() {
        return document.getElementById('accountSettingsScrollBody');
    }

    function readAifgAccountSectionState() {
        try {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(AIFG_ACCOUNT_SECTION_STATE_KEY) || '{}');
            return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : {};
        } catch (error) {
            return {};
        }
    }

    function writeAifgAccountSectionState(state) {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(AIFG_ACCOUNT_SECTION_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state || {}));
        } catch (error) {}
    }

    function normalizeAifgAccountSectionTarget(targetSection) {
        const raw = String(targetSection || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        const aliases = {
            preferences: 'general',
            general: 'general',
            apps: 'sidebar',
            sidebar: 'sidebar',
            access: 'privacy',
            gate: 'privacy',
            privacy: 'privacy',
            storage: 'data',
            data: 'data'
        };

        return aliases[raw] || raw;
    }

    function normalizeAifgAccountOptionTarget(targetOption) {
        const raw = String(targetOption || '').trim();
        if (!raw) return '';

        const aliases = {
            language: 'aifgLanguageSection',
            'interface-language': 'aifgLanguageSection'
        };

        const normalizedAlias = raw.toLowerCase();
        return aliases[normalizedAlias] || raw.replace(/^#/, '');
    }

    function revealAifgAccountOption(targetOption, focusSelector = '') {
        const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();
        const targetId = normalizeAifgAccountOptionTarget(targetOption);
        const target = targetId ? document.getElementById(targetId) : null;

        if (!body || !target || !body.contains(target)) return false;

        const reveal = () => {
            const bodyRect = body.getBoundingClientRect();
            const targetRect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
            const nextScrollTop = body.scrollTop + targetRect.top - bodyRect.top - 12;
            const maxScroll = Math.max(0, body.scrollHeight - body.clientHeight);

            body.scrollTop = Math.min(Math.max(0, nextScrollTop), maxScroll);
            saveAifgAccountScrollTop(body.scrollTop);
        };

        reveal();
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            reveal();

            const focusTarget = focusSelector
                ? target.querySelector(focusSelector)
                : target.matches('button, input, select, textarea, [tabindex]')
                    ? target
                    : target.querySelector('button, input, select, textarea, [tabindex]');

            focusTarget?.focus?.({ preventScroll: true });
        });

        return true;
    }

    function getAifgAccountSections() {
        const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();
        return body
            ? Array.from(body.querySelectorAll(':scope > .aifg-account-section[data-account-section]'))
            : [];
    }

    function hasRememberedAifgAccountSectionState(savedState = readAifgAccountSectionState()) {
        return getAifgAccountSections().some(section => {
            const key = String(section.dataset.accountSection || '').trim();
            return key && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(savedState, key);
        });
    }

    function writeCurrentAifgAccountSectionState() {
        const state = {};

        getAifgAccountSections().forEach(section => {
            const key = String(section.dataset.accountSection || '').trim();
            if (key) state[key] = !section.classList.contains('is-collapsed');
        });

        writeAifgAccountSectionState(state);
    }

    function getAifgAccountSavedScrollTop() {
        const value = Number(localStorage.getItem(AIFG_ACCOUNT_SCROLL_STATE_KEY));
        return Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0 ? value : 0;
    }

    function saveAifgAccountScrollTop(scrollTop) {
        const value = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(scrollTop) || 0));

        try {
            localStorage.setItem(AIFG_ACCOUNT_SCROLL_STATE_KEY, String(value));
        } catch (error) {}
    }

    function queueAifgAccountScrollSave(body = getAifgAccountScrollBody()) {
        if (!body) return;

        clearTimeout(aifgAccountScrollSaveTimer);
        aifgAccountScrollSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            saveAifgAccountScrollTop(body.scrollTop);
        }, 120);
    }

    function restoreAifgAccountScrollTop(scrollTop = getAifgAccountSavedScrollTop()) {
        const modal = document.getElementById('accountSettingsOverlay');
        const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();

        if (!modal || !body || modal.style.display !== 'flex') return;

        const apply = () => {
            const maxScroll = Math.max(0, body.scrollHeight - body.clientHeight);
            body.scrollTop = Math.min(Math.max(0, Number(scrollTop) || 0), maxScroll);
        };

        apply();
        requestAnimationFrame(apply);
    }

    function queueAifgAccountViewportRestore(scrollTop, anchor = null, anchorTop = null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(aifgAccountViewportRestoreFrame);

        const apply = () => {
            const modal = document.getElementById('accountSettingsOverlay');
            const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();

            if (!modal || !body || modal.style.display !== 'flex') return;

            if (
                anchor instanceof Element &&
                anchor.isConnected &&
                body.contains(anchor) &&
                Number.isFinite(anchorTop)
            ) {
                body.scrollTop += anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top - anchorTop;
            } else {
                const maxScroll = Math.max(0, body.scrollHeight - body.clientHeight);
                body.scrollTop = Math.min(Math.max(0, Number(scrollTop) || 0), maxScroll);
            }
        };

        aifgAccountViewportRestoreFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            apply();
            aifgAccountViewportRestoreFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                apply();
                saveAifgAccountScrollTop(getAifgAccountScrollBody()?.scrollTop || 0);
                aifgAccountViewportRestoreFrame = 0;
            });
        });
    }

    function setAifgAccountSectionCollapsed(section, collapsed, { persist = true } = {}) {
        if (!section) return;

        const key = String(section.dataset.accountSection || '').trim();
        const title = Array.from(section.children).find(child =>
            child.classList?.contains('aifg-account-section-title')
        );
        const nextCollapsed = !!collapsed;

        section.classList.toggle('is-collapsed', nextCollapsed);

        if (title) {
            title.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!nextCollapsed));
            title.title = `${nextCollapsed ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse'} ${title.dataset.accountSectionLabel || 'section'}`;
        }

        if (persist && key) {
            writeCurrentAifgAccountSectionState();
        }
    }

    function toggleAifgAccountSection(section) {
        if (!section) return;

        const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();
        const title = Array.from(section.children).find(child =>
            child.classList?.contains('aifg-account-section-title')
        );

        if (!body || !title) return;

        const titleTopBefore = title.getBoundingClientRect().top;
        const shouldOpen = section.classList.contains('is-collapsed');

        if (shouldOpen) {
            getAifgAccountSections().forEach(candidate => {
                setAifgAccountSectionCollapsed(candidate, candidate !== section, { persist: false });
            });
        } else {
            setAifgAccountSectionCollapsed(section, true, { persist: false });
        }

        writeCurrentAifgAccountSectionState();

        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            const titleTopAfter = title.getBoundingClientRect().top;
            body.scrollTop += titleTopAfter - titleTopBefore;
            saveAifgAccountScrollTop(body.scrollTop);
        });
    }

    function initAifgAccountSections(targetSection = '') {
        const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();
        if (!body) return;

        const savedState = readAifgAccountSectionState();
        const sections = getAifgAccountSections();
        const targetKey = normalizeAifgAccountSectionTarget(targetSection);
        const hasValidTarget = !!targetKey && sections.some(section =>
            String(section.dataset.accountSection || '').trim() === targetKey
        );
        const hasRememberedState = sections.some(section => {
            const key = String(section.dataset.accountSection || '').trim();
            return key && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(savedState, key);
        });
        const rememberedOpenSection = hasRememberedState
            ? sections.find(section => {
                const key = String(section.dataset.accountSection || '').trim();
                return key && savedState[key] === true;
            })
            : null;
        const initialOpenKey = hasValidTarget
            ? targetKey
            : rememberedOpenSection
                ? String(rememberedOpenSection.dataset.accountSection || '').trim()
                : hasRememberedState
                    ? ''
                    : String(sections[0]?.dataset.accountSection || '').trim();

        sections.forEach(section => {
            const title = Array.from(section.children).find(child =>
                child.classList?.contains('aifg-account-section-title')
            );

            if (!title) return;

            const labelClone = title.cloneNode(true);
            labelClone.querySelectorAll('i, .material-symbols-outlined, .aifg-account-section-chevron').forEach(node => node.remove());

            const label = labelClone.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
            const key = String(section.dataset.accountSection || '').trim();
            const isOpen = !!initialOpenKey && key === initialOpenKey;

            title.dataset.accountSectionLabel = label;
            title.setAttribute('role', 'button');
            title.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');

            if (!title.querySelector('.aifg-account-section-chevron')) {
                const chevron = document.createElement('i');
                chevron.className = 'fa-solid fa-chevron-down aifg-account-section-chevron';
                chevron.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                title.appendChild(chevron);
            }

            setAifgAccountSectionCollapsed(section, !isOpen, { persist: false });
        });

        // Persist the normalized accordion state so legacy multi-open layouts
        // cannot reopen more than one section on the next visit.
        writeCurrentAifgAccountSectionState();

        if (body.dataset.accountSectionsReady === '1') return;
        body.dataset.accountSectionsReady = '1';

        body.addEventListener('click', event => {
            const title = event.target.closest('.aifg-account-section > .aifg-account-section-title');
            if (!title || !body.contains(title)) return;

            event.preventDefault();
            toggleAifgAccountSection(title.parentElement);
        });

        body.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
            const title = event.target.closest('.aifg-account-section > .aifg-account-section-title');
            if (
                !title ||
                !body.contains(title) ||
                (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ')
            ) return;

            event.preventDefault();
            toggleAifgAccountSection(title.parentElement);
        });

        body.addEventListener('scroll', () => queueAifgAccountScrollSave(body), { passive: true });

        // Keep the same visible section when settings controls expand, collapse,
        // or refresh nearby UI. One delegated capture listener also covers
        // dynamically injected Custom App controls without per-control listeners.
        const preserveViewportForEvent = event => {
            const target = event.target;
            if (!(target instanceof Element)) return;
            if (target.closest('.aifg-account-section > .aifg-account-section-title')) return;

            const control = target.closest(
                'button, select, input[type="checkbox"], input[type="radio"], input[type="range"], input[type="color"], [onclick*="preview"], [onchange*="preview"], [oninput*="preview"]'
            );

            if (!control || !body.contains(control)) return;

            const anchor = control.closest('.aifg-account-section') || control;

            queueAifgAccountViewportRestore(
                body.scrollTop,
                anchor,
                anchor.getBoundingClientRect().top
            );
        };

        body.addEventListener('click', preserveViewportForEvent, true);
        body.addEventListener('change', preserveViewportForEvent, true);
        body.addEventListener('input', preserveViewportForEvent, true);
    }

    // --- 1. THE "DEEP CLEAN" ENGINE (Logic: Code A / Wording: Code B) ---
    async function performDeepClean() {
        console.log("Initiating Deep Clean Protocol...");
        
        // Visual cue that work is happening (Subtle opacity change)
        document.body.style.opacity = "0.5";

        // 1. THE HIT LIST (Logic from Code A)
        // We blindly target specific database names known to store persistent gallery data.
        const dbHitList = [
            'localforage',            // Standard plugin storage
            'keyvaluepairs',         // Common fallback
            'perchance-gallery',
            'media-gallery',
            'concept-board',
            'ai-furry-generator-concept-board',
            'perchance-concept-board',
            'gallery-store'
        ];

        // Attempt to find other databases if browser allows
        if (window.indexedDB && window.indexedDB.databases) {
            try {
                const dbs = await window.indexedDB.databases();
                dbs.forEach(db => dbHitList.push(db.name));
            } catch(e) { console.log("⚠️ Standard DB list unavailable, using target list."); }
        }

        // 2. EXECUTE DATABASE WIPE (Logic from Code A)
        const deletePromises = dbHitList.map(dbName => {
            return new Promise((resolve) => {
                const req = window.indexedDB.deleteDatabase(dbName);
                req.onsuccess = () => { console.log(`✓ Cleaned DB: ${dbName}`); resolve(); };
                req.onerror = () => { resolve(); }; // Continue even if failed
                req.onblocked = () => { console.log(`⚠️ Database busy: ${dbName}`); resolve(); };
            });
        });

        await Promise.all(deletePromises);

        // 3. CLEAR LOCALFORAGE LIBRARY (Logic from Code A)
        if (window.localforage) {
            try {
                await window.localforage.clear();
                console.log("✓ LocalForage cleared.");
            } catch(e) { /* Ignore */ }
        }

        // 4. STANDARD STORAGE WIPE
        localStorage.clear();
        sessionStorage.clear();
        
        // 5. CACHE WIPE (Logic from Code A)
        if ('caches' in window) {
            try {
                const keys = await caches.keys();
                keys.forEach(k => caches.delete(k));
            } catch(e) {}
        }

        // 6. COMPLETION REPORT (Wording: Code B)
        alert("Deep Clean complete.\n\nOpened channels, Concept Board prompts, Scratchpad entries, and Clipboard data have been cleared.\n\nPlease close other tabs of this page to ensure a fresh start.");
        location.reload();
    }

    // --- 2. CONFIRMATION DIALOG (Wording: Code B) ---
    window.triggerReset = function() {
        if (confirm( 
            "⚠️ Are you sure you want to reset the generator?\n\n" + 
            "This will perform a 'Deep Clean' to remove all opened channels, Concept Board, prompts, scratchpad entries, and clipboard data.\n\n" + 
            "This action cannot be undone." 
        )) {
            performDeepClean();
        }
    };

    // --- 3. UI INITIALIZATION & LOCK CHECK ---
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        const isLocked = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.LOCK) === 'true';
        const hasUser = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.USER) !== null;
        
        // Restore Custom Lock Screen Appearance
        const customAv = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.AVATAR);
        const customTitle = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.TITLE);

        if (customAv) {
            const loginImg = document.querySelector('#privacyLockOverlay img');
            if (loginImg) loginImg.src = customAv;
        }
        if (customTitle) {
            const titleEl = document.getElementById('lockScreenTitle');
            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = customTitle;
        }

        // Activate Lock Screen if needed
        if (isLocked && hasUser) {
            const overlay = document.getElementById('privacyLockOverlay');
            if (overlay) {
                overlay.style.display = 'flex';
                document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
            }
        }
    });

    const CHAT_LINK_PREFS_KEY = 'pl_chatLinkPrefs';

    function readChatLinkPrefs() {
        try {
            const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CHAT_LINK_PREFS_KEY));

            if (saved && ['off', 'selected', 'everyone'].includes(saved.mode)) {
                return {
                    mode: saved.mode,
                    save: saved.save || {},
                    hide: saved.hide || {}
                };
            }
        } catch {}

        return {
            mode: localStorage.getItem('autoSaveLinks') === 'true'
                ? 'everyone'
                : 'off',
            save: {},
            hide: {}
        };
    }

    function writeChatLinkPrefs(prefs) {
        localStorage.setItem(CHAT_LINK_PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefs));

        // Keep the old setting compatible.
        localStorage.setItem(
            'autoSaveLinks',
            prefs.mode === 'everyone' ? 'true' : 'false'
        );

        const modeInput = document.getElementById('linkAutoSaveMode');
        if (modeInput) modeInput.value = prefs.mode;

        const options = document.getElementById('autoSaveOptions');
        if (options) {
            options.style.display = prefs.mode === 'off' ? 'none' : 'block';
        }
    }

    function getChatLinkUser(user) {
        const id = String(user?.id || '').trim();
        const rawName = String(user?.name || '').trim();
        const name = rawName.toLowerCase() === 'anonymous' ? '' : rawName;
    
        if (!name && !id) return null;
    
        return {
            id,
            name,
            nickname: String(user?.nickname || user?.nick || '').trim(),
            visualId: String(user?.visualId || '').toUpperCase()
        };
    }
    
    function getChatLinkUserKey(record) {
        return record.name
            ? `name:${record.name.toLowerCase()}`
            : `id:${record.id}`;
    }
    
    function getChatLinkUserKeys(list, record) {
        if (!record) return [];
    
        const username = record.name.toLowerCase();
        const id = record.id;
    
        return Object.entries(list || {})
            .filter(([key, saved]) => {
                const savedName = String(saved?.name || '').trim().toLowerCase();
                const savedId = String(saved?.id || '').trim();
    
                if (username) {
                    return savedName === username;
                }
    
                return savedId === id || key === id || key === `id:${id}`;
            })
            .map(([key]) => key);
    }
    
    function setChatLinkUser(list, record, enabled) {
        getChatLinkUserKeys(list, record).forEach(key => {
            delete list[key];
        });
    
        if (enabled) {
            list[getChatLinkUserKey(record)] = record;
        }
    }
    
    function setChatLinkNickname(record, nickname) {
        const prefs = readChatLinkPrefs();
    
        ['save', 'hide'].forEach(listName => {
            getChatLinkUserKeys(prefs[listName], record).forEach(key => {
                const savedUser = prefs[listName][key];
    
                if (savedUser && typeof savedUser === 'object') {
                    savedUser.nickname = nickname;
                }
            });
        });
    
        writeChatLinkPrefs(prefs);
    }

    window.shouldAutoSaveChatLink = function(user) {
        const prefs = readChatLinkPrefs();
        const record = getChatLinkUser(user);
    
        if (!record || getChatLinkUserKeys(prefs.hide, record).length) {
            return false;
        }
    
        return prefs.mode === 'everyone' ||
            (
                prefs.mode === 'selected' &&
                getChatLinkUserKeys(prefs.save, record).length > 0
            );
    };
    
    window.shouldHideChatLinkPreview = function(user) {
        const prefs = readChatLinkPrefs();
        const record = getChatLinkUser(user);
    
        return Boolean(
            record &&
            getChatLinkUserKeys(prefs.hide, record).length
        );
    };

    function renderChatLinkList(containerId, listName) {
        const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
        if (!container) return;
    
        const prefs = readChatLinkPrefs();
        const seen = new Set();
    
        const users = Object.values(prefs[listName]).filter(user => {
            const rawName = String(user?.name || '').trim();
            const username = rawName === 'Anonymous' ? '' : rawName.toLowerCase();
            const id = String(user?.id || '').trim();
            const identity = username ? `name:${username}` : `id:${id}`;
    
            if (!identity || seen.has(identity)) return false;
    
            seen.add(identity);
            return true;
        });
    
        container.replaceChildren();
    
        if (!users.length) {
            const empty = document.createElement('div');
            empty.textContent = 'No users added.';
            empty.style.cssText = 'font-size:0.78rem;opacity:0.55;padding:6px 0;';
            container.appendChild(empty);
            return;
        }
    
        users.forEach(user => {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:6px 8px;margin-top:5px;background:var(--background-color,#111);border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333);border-radius:6px;';
    
            if (user.visualId) {
                const raw = String(user.id || '').slice(3, 9);
                const hex6 = /^[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(raw)
                    ? raw.toUpperCase()
                    : '888888';
    
                const r = parseInt(hex6.slice(0, 2), 16);
                const g = parseInt(hex6.slice(2, 4), 16);
                const b = parseInt(hex6.slice(4, 6), 16);
    
                const textColor =
                    0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b > 128
                        ? '#000'
                        : '#FFF';
    
                const tag = document.createElement('span');
                tag.textContent = user.visualId;
                tag.style.cssText = `font-size:65%;font-family:'Open Sans',sans-serif;padding:2px 0.1rem;border-radius:3px;font-weight:400;letter-spacing:0.1px;flex-shrink:0;user-select:text;background:#${hex6};color:${textColor};`;
    
                row.appendChild(tag);
            }
    
            const rawName = String(user.name || '').trim();
            const username = rawName === 'Anonymous' ? '' : rawName;
            const nickname = String(user.nickname || '').trim();
            
            const nameWrap = document.createElement('span');
            nameWrap.style.cssText = 'flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;font-size:0.82rem;';
            
            if (username) {
                const usernameEl = document.createElement('span');
                usernameEl.textContent = username;
                usernameEl.style.color = '#0091e2';
                usernameEl.title =
                    'Matched by username even if the nickname or ID changes';
                nameWrap.appendChild(usernameEl);
            }
            
            if (nickname) {
                const nicknameEl = document.createElement('span');
                nicknameEl.textContent = `${username ? ' ' : ''}(${nickname})`;
                nameWrap.appendChild(nicknameEl);
            }
            
            row.appendChild(nameWrap);
            
            const edit = document.createElement('button');
            edit.type = 'button';
            edit.title = 'Edit saved nickname';
            edit.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Edit saved nickname');
            edit.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-pencil"></i>';
            edit.style.cssText = 'width:26px;height:26px;padding:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;border:none;border-radius:5px;background:transparent;color:var(--text-color,#ddd);font-size:10px;cursor:pointer;opacity:0.7;';
            
            edit.onclick = () => {
                const input = document.createElement('input');
                input.type = 'text';
                input.value = nickname;
                input.placeholder = 'Nickname';
                input.maxLength = 80;
                input.title = 'Local nickname only. User matching is unchanged.';
                input.style.cssText = 'flex:1;min-width:80px;height:26px;box-sizing:border-box;padding:3px 7px;border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333);border-radius:5px;background:var(--box-color,#1e1e1e);color:var(--text-color,#ddd);font:inherit;';
            
                let finished = false;
            
                const finish = save => {
                    if (finished) return;
                    finished = true;
            
                    if (save) {
                        setChatLinkNickname(
                            user,
                            input.value.trim()
                        );
                    }
            
                    window.renderChatLinkUserLists();
                };
            
                input.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                        event.preventDefault();
                        finish(true);
                    } else if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                        event.preventDefault();
                        finish(false);
                    }
                });
            
                input.addEventListener(
                    'blur',
                    () => finish(true),
                    { once: true }
                );
            
                nameWrap.replaceWith(input);
                edit.style.display = 'none';
            
                input.focus();
                input.select();
            };
            
            row.appendChild(edit);
    
            const remove = document.createElement('button');
            remove.type = 'button';
            remove.title = 'Remove';
            remove.innerHTML = '<i class="fa-solid fa-xmark"></i>';
            remove.style.cssText = 'width:26px;height:26px;padding:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;border:none;border-radius:5px;background:transparent;color:var(--text-color,#ddd);cursor:pointer;opacity:0.7;';
    
            remove.onclick = () => {
                const current = readChatLinkPrefs();
    
                getChatLinkUserKeys(current[listName], user).forEach(key => {
                    delete current[listName][key];
                });
    
                writeChatLinkPrefs(current);
                window.renderChatLinkUserLists();
            };
    
            row.appendChild(remove);
            container.appendChild(row);
        });
    }

    window.renderChatLinkUserLists = function() {
        renderChatLinkList('linkAutoSaveUsersList', 'save');
        renderChatLinkList('linkHiddenUsersList', 'hide');
    };

    window.openChatLinkUserMenu = function(anchor, user) {
        const record = getChatLinkUser(user);
        if (!record) return;

        document.getElementById('chatLinkUserMenu')?.remove();

        const prefs = readChatLinkPrefs();
        const menu = document.createElement('div');
        menu.id = 'chatLinkUserMenu';
        menu.style.cssText = 'min-width:250px;padding:5px;background:var(--box-color,#1e1e1e);color:var(--text-color,#ddd);border:1px solid var(--border-color,#333);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);position:fixed;z-index:100000;font-family:"Open Sans",sans-serif;';

        const addAction = (icon, label, disabled, action) => {
            const button = document.createElement('button');
            button.type = 'button';
            button.disabled = disabled;
            button.innerHTML = `<i class="${icon}" style="width:18px;"></i><span>${label}</span>`;
            button.style.cssText = 'width:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:9px 10px;border:none;border-radius:6px;background:transparent;color:inherit;text-align:left;font-size:0.78rem;cursor:pointer;';

            if (disabled) {
                button.style.opacity = '0.45';
                button.style.cursor = 'default';
            } else {
                button.onmouseenter = () => {
                    button.style.background = 'rgba(127,127,127,0.15)';
                };
                button.onmouseleave = () => {
                    button.style.background = 'transparent';
                };
                button.onclick = action;
            }

            menu.appendChild(button);
        };

        const alreadySaved =
            getChatLinkUserKeys(prefs.save, record).length > 0;
        
        const alreadyHidden =
            getChatLinkUserKeys(prefs.hide, record).length > 0;

        addAction(
            alreadySaved
                ? 'fa-solid fa-star'
                : 'fa-regular fa-star',
            alreadySaved
                ? 'Remove from favorites'
                : 'Add to favorites',
            false,
            () => {
                const current = readChatLinkPrefs();
                
                const enableFavorite =
                    getChatLinkUserKeys(current.save, record).length === 0;
                
                setChatLinkUser(
                    current.save,
                    record,
                    enableFavorite
                );
                
                writeChatLinkPrefs(current);
                window.renderChatLinkUserLists();
                menu.remove();
            }
        );

        addAction(
            alreadyHidden ? 'fa-solid fa-eye' : 'fa-solid fa-eye-slash',
            alreadyHidden
                ? 'Show this user’s link previews'
                : 'Hide this user’s link previews',
            false,
            () => {
                const current = readChatLinkPrefs();
                
                const hidePreview =
                    getChatLinkUserKeys(current.hide, record).length === 0;
                
                setChatLinkUser(
                    current.hide,
                    record,
                    hidePreview
                );
                
                writeChatLinkPrefs(current);
                window.renderChatLinkUserLists();
                menu.remove();
            }
        );

        document.body.appendChild(menu);

        const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
        const left = Math.max(
            8,
            Math.min(
                rect.right - menu.offsetWidth,
                window.innerWidth - menu.offsetWidth - 8
            )
        );

        const below = rect.bottom + 5;
        const top = below + menu.offsetHeight <= window.innerHeight - 8
            ? below
            : Math.max(8, rect.top - menu.offsetHeight - 5);

        menu.style.left = `${left}px`;
        menu.style.top = `${top}px`;
    };

    if (!window.__chatLinkMenuCloseBound) {
        document.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
            const menu = document.getElementById('chatLinkUserMenu');

            if (
                menu &&
                !menu.contains(event.target) &&
                !event.target.closest?.('[data-chat-link-menu]')
            ) {
                menu.remove();
            }
        });

        window.__chatLinkMenuCloseBound = true;
    }
    
    // --- 4. ACCOUNT MODAL LOGIC (Unified 10-App Version) ---
    window.openAccountModal = function(targetSection = '', targetOption = '') {
        const normalizedTargetSection = normalizeAifgAccountSectionTarget(targetSection);
        const normalizedTargetOption = normalizeAifgAccountOptionTarget(targetOption);
        initAifgAccountSections(normalizedTargetSection);

        const storedAv = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.AVATAR);
        const storedTitle = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.TITLE);

        const chatLinkPrefs = readChatLinkPrefs();
        const linkModeInput = document.getElementById('linkAutoSaveMode');

        if (linkModeInput) {
            linkModeInput.value = chatLinkPrefs.mode;
        }

        const autoSaveOptions = document.getElementById('autoSaveOptions');
        if (autoSaveOptions) {
            autoSaveOptions.style.display =
                chatLinkPrefs.mode === 'off' ? 'none' : 'block';
        }

        window.renderChatLinkUserLists();
        
        const rememberGallerySearchInp = document.getElementById('rememberGallerySearchToggle');
        if (rememberGallerySearchInp) {
            rememberGallerySearchInp.checked = localStorage.getItem('pl_rememberGallerySearch') === 'true';
        }

        const workspaceLayoutInput = document.getElementById('workspaceLayoutSelect');
        if (workspaceLayoutInput) {
            let focusedModePreview = false;
            try {
                focusedModePreview = sessionStorage.getItem('pl_workspaceLayoutPreviewV1') === 'dock';
            } catch {}
            workspaceLayoutInput.value = focusedModePreview || localStorage.getItem('pl_workspaceLayout') === 'dock'
                ? 'dock'
                : 'classic';
        }

        const workspaceStudioSplitInput = document.getElementById('workspaceStudioSplitSelect');
        if (workspaceStudioSplitInput) {
            workspaceStudioSplitInput.value = localStorage.getItem('pl_workspaceStudioSplit') === 'off'
                ? 'off'
                : 'on';
        }

        const chatDestType = localStorage.getItem('autoSaveDestType') || 'current';
        if (document.getElementById('autoSaveDestSpecific') && chatDestType === 'specific') {
            document.getElementById('autoSaveDestSpecific').checked = true;
        } else if (document.getElementById('autoSaveDestCurrent')) {
            document.getElementById('autoSaveDestCurrent').checked = true;
        }
        document.getElementById('autoSaveDestName').value = localStorage.getItem('autoSaveDestName') || '';

        const galStorageMode = localStorage.getItem('gallerySaveStorageMode') || 'link';
        if (document.getElementById('gallerySaveStorageFile') && galStorageMode === 'file') {
            document.getElementById('gallerySaveStorageFile').checked = true;
        } else if (document.getElementById('gallerySaveStorageLink')) {
            document.getElementById('gallerySaveStorageLink').checked = true;
        }

        const galDestType = localStorage.getItem('gallerySaveDestType') || 'current';
        if (document.getElementById('gallerySaveDestSpecific') && galDestType === 'specific') {
            document.getElementById('gallerySaveDestSpecific').checked = true;
        } else if (document.getElementById('gallerySaveDestCurrent')) {
            document.getElementById('gallerySaveDestCurrent').checked = true;
        }
        document.getElementById('gallerySaveDestName').value = localStorage.getItem('gallerySaveDestName') || '';

        const genDestType = localStorage.getItem('genSaveDestType') || 'current';
        if (document.getElementById('genSaveDestSpecific') && genDestType === 'specific') {
            document.getElementById('genSaveDestSpecific').checked = true;
        } else if (document.getElementById('genSaveDestCurrent')) {
            document.getElementById('genSaveDestCurrent').checked = true;
        }
        const genDestNameInput = document.getElementById('genSaveDestName');
        if (genDestNameInput) genDestNameInput.value = localStorage.getItem('genSaveDestName') || '';

        const autoPromptHistoryToggle = document.getElementById('autoSavePromptHistoryToggle');
        if (autoPromptHistoryToggle) {
            autoPromptHistoryToggle.checked = localStorage.getItem('pl_autoSavePromptHistory') === 'true';
        }
        const autoPromptHistoryFirstImageToggle = document.getElementById('autoSavePromptHistoryFirstImageToggle');
        if (autoPromptHistoryFirstImageToggle) {
            autoPromptHistoryFirstImageToggle.checked = localStorage.getItem('pl_autoSavePromptHistoryFirstImage') === 'true';
        }

        const promptHistoryDestType = localStorage.getItem('promptHistorySaveDestType') || 'current';
        if (document.getElementById('promptHistorySaveDestSpecific') && promptHistoryDestType === 'specific') {
            document.getElementById('promptHistorySaveDestSpecific').checked = true;
        } else if (document.getElementById('promptHistorySaveDestCurrent')) {
            document.getElementById('promptHistorySaveDestCurrent').checked = true;
        }
        const promptHistoryDestNameInput = document.getElementById('promptHistorySaveDestName');
        if (promptHistoryDestNameInput) {
            promptHistoryDestNameInput.value = localStorage.getItem('promptHistorySaveDestName') || '';
        }

        const autoPromptHistoryOptions = document.getElementById('autoSavePromptHistoryOptions');
        if (autoPromptHistoryOptions) {
            autoPromptHistoryOptions.style.display = autoPromptHistoryToggle?.checked ? 'block' : 'none';
        }

        ['comments', 'gallery', 'furpost', 'tldraw', 'imageEdit', 'imageToVideo', 'bgRemove', 'finegrain', 'uploader', 'furdex', 'furai', 'emojiMaker', 'vnSpriteMaker'].forEach(app => {
            const sel = document.getElementById('mem_' + app);
            if (sel) sel.value = localStorage.getItem('pl_mem_' + app) || '3min';
        });
        
        const setVal = (id, val) => { const el = document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value = val || ''; };
        const savedUid = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.USER) || '';
        const hasSavedDeleteCode = !!localStorage.getItem(KEYS.PASS);

        setVal('accAvatarInput', storedAv);
        setVal('accGenNameInput', storedTitle);
        setVal('accUidField', savedUid);

        const tkInp = document.getElementById('lockModeToggle');
        if (tkInp) tkInp.checked = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.LOCK) === 'true';

        const deleteCodeInput = document.getElementById('accSafeCodeField');
        const confirmUidInput = document.getElementById('accUidConfirmField');
        const confirmUidWrap = document.getElementById('accUidConfirmWrap');
        const confirmDeleteCodeInput = document.getElementById('accSafeCodeConfirmField');
        const confirmDeleteCodeWrap = document.getElementById('accSafeCodeConfirmWrap');

        aifgEntryGateCredentialState.savedUid = savedUid;
        aifgEntryGateCredentialState.confirmedUid = savedUid;
        aifgEntryGateCredentialState.confirmedDeleteCode = '';

        if (deleteCodeInput) {
            deleteCodeInput.value = '';
            deleteCodeInput.placeholder = hasSavedDeleteCode
                ? 'Leave blank to keep current code'
                : 'Set delete code...';
        }

        if (confirmUidInput) confirmUidInput.value = '';
        if (confirmUidWrap) confirmUidWrap.hidden = true;
        if (confirmDeleteCodeInput) confirmDeleteCodeInput.value = '';
        if (confirmDeleteCodeWrap) confirmDeleteCodeWrap.hidden = true;
        window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation();

        const imgPrev = document.getElementById('modalAvatarPreview');
        const defaultAv = 'https://user.uploads.dev/file/bf29cb5cc0318957642cc2da85e4236d.webp';
        if (imgPrev) imgPrev.src = (storedAv && storedAv.trim() !== "") ? storedAv : defaultAv;

        // Load Sidebar settings (Positive Toggles)
        const toggles = ['showFurPost','showChat','showGallery','showEmojiMaker','showVnSpriteMaker','showConcept','showDraw','showEdit','showStudio','showBgRemove','showFinegrain','showVideo','showUpload','showFurdex','showFurai','showGuide','showScratchpad','showClip','showGenerate'];
        toggles.forEach(t => {
            const el = document.getElementById(t + 'Btn'); 
            // Default to true (checked) unless explicitly saved as 'false'
            if(el) el.checked = localStorage.getItem('pl_' + t) !== 'false'; 
        });
        
        const inputHintToggle = document.getElementById('showInputHintsToggle');
        if (inputHintToggle) inputHintToggle.checked = localStorage.getItem('pl_showInputHints') !== 'false';
        
        // Load 3 Custom Apps into Modal
        for (let i = 1; i <= MAX_CUSTOM_APPS; i++) {
            const elName = document.getElementById(`customAppName${i}`);
            const elUrl = document.getElementById(`customAppUrl${i}`);
            const elMem = document.getElementById(`customAppMemory${i}`);
            
            if (elName) elName.value = localStorage.getItem(`pl_customAppName${i}`) || '';
            if (elUrl) elUrl.value = localStorage.getItem(`pl_customAppUrl${i}`) || '';
            if (elMem) elMem.value = localStorage.getItem(`pl_customAppMemory${i}`) || '3min';
            
            const savedIcon = localStorage.getItem(`pl_customAppIcon${i}`) || 'fa-solid fa-mask';
            if (typeof selectCustomIcon === 'function') selectCustomIcon(i, savedIcon);
            
            const wrapper = document.getElementById(`appSlotWrapper${i}`);
            if (wrapper) {
               if (i === 1 || (elUrl && elUrl.value.trim() !== '')) {
                   wrapper.style.display = 'block';
               } else {
                   wrapper.style.display = 'none';
               }
            }
        }
        if (typeof checkAddMoreButton === 'function') checkAddMoreButton();

        const layoutBox = document.getElementById('galleryLayoutTransferBox');
        if (layoutBox) layoutBox.value = '';

        window.prepareAifgUiLanguageControl?.();
        window.updatePrivacyGateNoticeVisibility();

        const modal = document.getElementById('accountSettingsOverlay');

        if (modal) {
            modal.style.display = 'flex';

            const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();
            const target = normalizedTargetSection
                ? body?.querySelector(`:scope > .aifg-account-section[data-account-section="${normalizedTargetSection}"]`)
                : null;

            if (normalizedTargetOption && body) {
                const revealOption = () => {
                    revealAifgAccountOption(
                        normalizedTargetOption,
                        normalizedTargetOption === 'aifgLanguageSection'
                            ? '#aifgUiLanguageTrigger'
                            : ''
                    );
                };

                revealOption();
                requestAnimationFrame(revealOption);
            } else if (target && body) {
                const revealTarget = () => {
                    const bodyRect = body.getBoundingClientRect();
                    const targetRect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
                    const nextScrollTop = body.scrollTop + targetRect.top - bodyRect.top;
                    const maxScroll = Math.max(0, body.scrollHeight - body.clientHeight);

                    body.scrollTop = Math.min(Math.max(0, nextScrollTop), maxScroll);
                    saveAifgAccountScrollTop(body.scrollTop);
                };

                revealTarget();
                requestAnimationFrame(revealTarget);
            } else {
                const savedState = readAifgAccountSectionState();
                restoreAifgAccountScrollTop(
                    hasRememberedAifgAccountSectionState(savedState)
                        ? getAifgAccountSavedScrollTop()
                        : 0
                );
            }
        }
    };

    window.closeAccModal = function() {
        window.closeAifgUiLanguageControl?.();

        const body = getAifgAccountScrollBody();
        if (body) saveAifgAccountScrollTop(body.scrollTop);

        const modal = document.getElementById('accountSettingsOverlay');
        if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
    };

    window.saveAccSettings = function() {
        const validation = window.getEntryGateCredentialValidation();

        if (!validation.valid) {
            window.updateEntryGateCredentialValidation();
            alert(validation.message);

            const invalidField = validation.uidPending
                ? document.getElementById('accUidConfirmField')
                : document.getElementById('accSafeCodeConfirmField');

            invalidField?.focus();
            return;
        }

        const u = document.getElementById('accUidField')?.value.trim() || '';
        const p = document.getElementById('accSafeCodeField')?.value.trim() || '';
        
        const rawChatName = document.getElementById('autoSaveDestName')?.value.trim() || '';
        const rawGalName = document.getElementById('gallerySaveDestName')?.value.trim() || '';
        const rawGenName = document.getElementById('genSaveDestName')?.value.trim() || '';
        const rawPromptHistoryName = document.getElementById('promptHistorySaveDestName')?.value.trim() || '';
        const nameRegex = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$/;

        if (rawChatName !== '' && !nameRegex.test(rawChatName)) {
            alert("Auto-Save page name can only contain letters, numbers, and underscores (_).\nNo spaces, symbols, or emojis allowed!");
            return;
        }
        if (rawGalName !== '' && !nameRegex.test(rawGalName)) {
            alert("Gallery Save page name can only contain letters, numbers, and underscores (_).\nNo spaces, symbols, or emojis allowed!");
            return;
        }
        if (rawGenName !== '' && !nameRegex.test(rawGenName)) {
            alert("Generator Save page name can only contain letters, numbers, and underscores (_).\nNo spaces, symbols, or emojis allowed!");
            return;
        }
        if (rawPromptHistoryName !== '' && !nameRegex.test(rawPromptHistoryName)) {
            alert("Prompt History page name can only contain letters, numbers, and underscores (_).\nNo spaces, symbols, or emojis allowed!");
            return;
        }

        localStorage.setItem(KEYS.USER, u);
        if (p) localStorage.setItem(KEYS.PASS, p);
        
        const av = document.getElementById('accAvatarInput')?.value.trim() || '';
        const ti = document.getElementById('accGenNameInput')?.value.trim() || '';
        
        localStorage.setItem(KEYS.AVATAR, av);
        localStorage.setItem(KEYS.TITLE, ti);
        localStorage.setItem(KEYS.LOCK, document.getElementById('lockModeToggle')?.checked);
        const chatLinkPrefs = readChatLinkPrefs();
        chatLinkPrefs.mode =
            document.getElementById('linkAutoSaveMode')?.value || 'off';
        writeChatLinkPrefs(chatLinkPrefs);
        localStorage.setItem('pl_showAppCloseHints', document.getElementById('showAppCloseHintsToggle')?.checked ? 'true' : 'false');
        localStorage.setItem('pl_showInputHints', document.getElementById('showInputHintsToggle')?.checked ? 'true' : 'false');
        window.___applyInputHintVisibility();
        
        localStorage.setItem('pl_rememberGallerySearch', document.getElementById('rememberGallerySearchToggle')?.checked ? 'true' : 'false');
        localStorage.setItem(
            'pl_workspaceLayout',
            document.getElementById('workspaceLayoutSelect')?.value === 'dock' ? 'dock' : 'classic'
        );
        localStorage.setItem(
            'pl_workspaceStudioSplit',
            document.getElementById('workspaceStudioSplitSelect')?.value === 'off' ? 'off' : 'on'
        );
        localStorage.setItem('pl_focusedModeIntroDoneV1', '1');
        try { sessionStorage.removeItem('pl_workspaceLayoutPreviewV1'); } catch {}
        window.dismissFocusedModeIntro?.();

        const chatDestSpecific = document.getElementById('autoSaveDestSpecific')?.checked;
        localStorage.setItem('autoSaveDestType', chatDestSpecific ? 'specific' : 'current');
        localStorage.setItem('autoSaveDestName', rawChatName);

        const galStorageMode = document.getElementById('gallerySaveStorageFile')?.checked ? 'file' : 'link';
        localStorage.setItem('gallerySaveStorageMode', galStorageMode);

        const galDestSpecific = document.getElementById('gallerySaveDestSpecific')?.checked;
        localStorage.setItem('gallerySaveDestType', galDestSpecific ? 'specific' : 'current');
        localStorage.setItem('gallerySaveDestName', rawGalName);

        const genDestType = document.getElementById('genSaveDestSpecific')?.checked
            ? 'specific'
            : 'current';
        localStorage.setItem('genSaveDestType', genDestType);
        localStorage.setItem('genSaveDestName', rawGenName);

        localStorage.setItem(
            'pl_autoSavePromptHistory',
            document.getElementById('autoSavePromptHistoryToggle')?.checked ? 'true' : 'false'
        );
        localStorage.setItem(
            'pl_autoSavePromptHistoryFirstImage',
            document.getElementById('autoSavePromptHistoryFirstImageToggle')?.checked ? 'true' : 'false'
        );

        const promptHistoryDestType = document.getElementById('promptHistorySaveDestSpecific')?.checked
            ? 'specific'
            : 'current';
        localStorage.setItem('promptHistorySaveDestType', promptHistoryDestType);
        localStorage.setItem('promptHistorySaveDestName', rawPromptHistoryName);

        ['comments', 'gallery', 'furpost', 'tldraw', 'imageEdit', 'imageToVideo', 'bgRemove', 'finegrain', 'uploader', 'furdex', 'furai', 'emojiMaker', 'vnSpriteMaker'].forEach(app => {
            const sel = document.getElementById('mem_' + app);
            if (sel) localStorage.setItem('pl_mem_' + app, sel.value);
        });

        const loginImg = document.querySelector('#privacyLockOverlay img');
        if (loginImg && av) loginImg.src = av;
        
        const titleEl = document.getElementById('lockScreenTitle');
        if (titleEl && ti) titleEl.textContent = ti;

        // Save Sidebar settings (Positive Toggles)
        const toggles = ['showFurPost','showChat','showGallery','showEmojiMaker','showVnSpriteMaker','showConcept','showDraw','showEdit','showStudio','showBgRemove','showFinegrain','showVideo','showUpload','showFurdex','showFurai','showGuide','showScratchpad','showClip','showGenerate'];
        toggles.forEach(t => {
            localStorage.setItem('pl_' + t, document.getElementById(t + 'Btn')?.checked);
        });

        // Save 10 Custom Apps
        for (let i = 1; i <= MAX_CUSTOM_APPS; i++) {
            let rawUrl = document.getElementById(`customAppUrl${i}`)?.value || '';
            let safeUrl = typeof formatPerchanceUrl === 'function' ? formatPerchanceUrl(rawUrl) : rawUrl.trim();

            // --- THE WARNING CHECK ---
            if (safeUrl && typeof isLikelyBlocked === 'function' && isLikelyBlocked(safeUrl)) {
                const proceed = confirm(`⚠️ Warning for App Slot ${i}:\n\nWe detected a URL that usually blocks being embedded (${safeUrl}).\n\nIt will likely show a "Refused to connect" error unless you are using a specific embed link. Do you still want to save it anyway?`);
                if (!proceed) return; // Stops the saving process so they can fix it
            }
            // -------------------------

            localStorage.setItem(`pl_customAppName${i}`, document.getElementById(`customAppName${i}`)?.value.trim() || '');
            localStorage.setItem(`pl_customAppUrl${i}`, safeUrl);
            localStorage.setItem(`pl_customAppIcon${i}`, document.getElementById(`customAppIcon${i}`)?.value || 'fa-solid fa-mask');
            localStorage.setItem(`pl_customAppMemory${i}`, document.getElementById(`customAppMemory${i}`)?.value || '3min');
            
            if(safeUrl) {
                const urlInput = document.getElementById(`customAppUrl${i}`);
                if(urlInput) urlInput.value = safeUrl; 
            }
        }

        window.applySidebarSettings(); // Apply instantly
        window.applyWorkspaceLayout?.();
        window.__syncSocialPanelMounts?.();
        const uiLanguageChanged = window.commitAifgUiLanguage?.() || false;
        window.closeAccModal();
        if (uiLanguageChanged) location.reload();
    };

    window.applySidebarSettings = function() {
        const map = {
            'showFurPost': 'furPostBtn', 'showChat': 'chatBtn', 'showGallery': 'galleryBtn',
            'showEmojiMaker': 'emojiMakerBtn', 'showVnSpriteMaker': 'vnSpriteMakerBtn', 'showConcept': 'conceptBoardBtn',
            'showDraw': 'tldrawBtn', 'showEdit': 'imageEditBtn', 'showStudio': 'studioBtn',
            'showBgRemove': 'bgRemoveBtn', 'showFinegrain': 'finegrainBtn', 'showVideo': 'imageToVideoBtn', 'showUpload': 'uploaderBtn', 'showFurdex': 'furdexSidebarBtn', 'showFurai': 'furaiBtn', 'showGuide': 'guideBtn', 'showScratchpad': 'scratchpadBtn', 'showClip': 'clipboardsBtn', 'showGenerate': 'generateBtn'
        };
        
        // Apply Toggles
        for (const [key, btnId] of Object.entries(map)) {
            const btn = document.getElementById(btnId);
            if (btn) {
                // If it equals 'false', hide it. Otherwise, show it.
                btn.style.display = localStorage.getItem('pl_' + key) === 'false' ? 'none' : 'block';
            }
        }

        // Apply 10 Custom Apps
        let hasVisibleCustomApp = false;
        
        for (let i = 1; i <= MAX_CUSTOM_APPS; i++) {
            const cName = localStorage.getItem(`pl_customAppName${i}`);
            const cUrl = localStorage.getItem(`pl_customAppUrl${i}`);
            const cBtn = document.getElementById(`customAppBtn${i}`);
            
            if (cName && cUrl && cBtn) {
                hasVisibleCustomApp = true;
                cBtn.style.display = 'block';
        
                const nameDisplay = document.getElementById(`customAppNameDisplay${i}`);
                if (nameDisplay) {
                    nameDisplay.textContent = cName;
                    nameDisplay.title = cName;
                }
        
                const iconDisplay = document.getElementById(`customAppIconDisplay${i}`);
                if (iconDisplay) {
                    const savedIcon = localStorage.getItem(`pl_customAppIcon${i}`);
                    if (savedIcon === '__none__') {
                        iconDisplay.className = '';
                        iconDisplay.style.display = 'none';
                    } else {
                        iconDisplay.className = savedIcon || 'fa-solid fa-paw';
                        iconDisplay.style.display = '';
                    }
                }
            } else if (cBtn) {
                cBtn.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }
        
        const customSlot = document.getElementById('customAppBtnsSlot');
        if (customSlot) {
            customSlot.style.display = hasVisibleCustomApp ? 'flex' : 'none';
        }
    };

    // --- 5. LOCK SCREEN HANDLER ---
    window.handleLockSubmit = function() {
        const inputAcc = document.getElementById('lockAcc').value.trim();
        const inputPass = document.getElementById('lockPass').value.trim();
        
        const realUser = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.USER);
        const realPass = localStorage.getItem(KEYS.PASS);

        // FIX 2: password for clean up
        if (inputPass === realPass && inputPass !== "") {
            // Trigger the deep clean logic via the confirmation dialog
            window.triggerReset(); // <--- FIXED (Now asks "Are you sure?")
            return;
        }

        // FIX 1: account for log in (Removed requirement for password)
        if (inputAcc === realUser) {
            document.getElementById('privacyLockOverlay').style.display = 'none';
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            return;
        } 

        // Friendly error (Context B)
        alert("Invalid Account or Password.");
    };

    // UI Helper to toggle the "No Data" warning text
    window.checkAccInputs = function() {
        const inputs = ['accAvatarInput', 'accGenNameInput', 'accUidField', 'accSafeCodeField'];
        const hasData = inputs.some(id => {
            const el = document.getElementById(id);
            return el && el.value.trim() !== "";
        });
        const warn = document.getElementById('accWarningInfo');
        if (warn) warn.style.display = hasData ? 'none' : 'block';
    };
})();
</script>

<script>
(function() {
  // 1. SINGLETON TIMER & UI SETUP
  let chipTipTimer = null;

  // 2. THE NOTIFICATION LOGIC
  function showChipTooltip(name, isOn) {
    // Check if the user muted this specific chip
    const muteKey = 'mute_chip_' + name;
    if (localStorage.getItem(muteKey) === 'true') return;

    // Streamlined text - no emojis, straight to the point
    const messages = {
      "Furry": {
        true: "<b>Furry Mode ON:</b> Anthro tags appended.",
        false: "<b>Furry Mode OFF:</b> Anthro tags removed."
      },
      "SFW": {
        true: "<b>SFW Mode:</b> Visible in public PG-13 gallery.",
        false: "<b>NSFW Mode:</b> Hidden from PG-13 feed."
      },
      "Comments": {
        true: "<b>Comments ON:</b> Replies enabled.",
        false: "<b>Comments OFF:</b> Replies disabled."
      },
      "Private": {
        true: "<b>Private Mode:</b> Visible only to you & in Admin mode.<br><span style='font-size: 11px; display: inline-block; margin-top: 4px;'><i class='fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation'></i> Warning: Advanced feature.</span>",
        false: "<b>Public Mode:</b> Visible to everyone."
      }
    };

    const msg = messages[name] ? messages[name][isOn] : `${name} turned ${isOn ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}.`;

    // Grab or create the single notification box
    let tip = document.getElementById('chipNotifySingleton');
    if (!tip) {
      tip = document.createElement('div');
      tip.id = 'chipNotifySingleton';
      tip.style.cssText = `
        position: fixed; bottom: 80px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(20px);
        background: #131516; color: #d8d4cf; padding: 12px 16px;
        border: 1px solid var(--floatingpanel-border-color, #333); border-radius: 8px;
        box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index: 999999;
        font-family: 'Open Sans', sans-serif; font-size: 13px;
        display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; min-width: 260px; max-width: 320px;
        opacity: 0; pointer-events: none; transition: opacity 0.2s ease, transform 0.2s ease;
      `;
      document.body.appendChild(tip);
      
      // Event delegation for both the Checkbox AND the Dismiss Button
      tip.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        // Handle Dismiss X
        if (e.target.closest('#chipDismissBtn')) {
          tip.style.opacity = '0';
          tip.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(20px)';
          tip.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
          if (chipTipTimer) clearTimeout(chipTipTimer);
        }
      });

      tip.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        // Handle Mute Checkbox
        if (e.target.id === 'chipMuteCb') {
          localStorage.setItem('mute_chip_' + e.target.dataset.chip, e.target.checked);
          if (e.target.checked) {
            tip.style.opacity = '0';
            tip.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(20px)';
            tip.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
          }
        }
      });
    }

    // Inject the specific message, close button, and simplified checkbox
    tip.innerHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:flex-start; gap:12px;">
        <div style="line-height:1.4; flex-grow:1;">
          ${msg}<br>
          <span style="font-size:11px; opacity:0.6; display:inline-block; margin-top:4px;">
            (Tap <i class="fa-solid fa-circle-info"></i> in menu for details)
          </span>
        </div>
        <button id="chipDismissBtn" style="background:none; border:none; color:#aaa; cursor:pointer; padding:0; font-size:14px; line-height:1;" title="Dismiss">✕</button>
      </div>
      <label style="font-size:12px; color:#aaa; cursor:pointer; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; margin-top:2px; padding-top:8px; border-top:1px solid #333;">
        <input type="checkbox" id="chipMuteCb" data-chip="${name}" style="cursor:pointer; margin:0;">
        Don't show this again
      </label>
    `;

    // Clear old timeout if they spam-clicked
    if (chipTipTimer) clearTimeout(chipTipTimer);
    
    // Show the box
    tip.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      tip.style.opacity = '1';
      tip.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(0)';
    });

    // Auto-dismiss after 5 seconds
    chipTipTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      tip.style.opacity = '0';
      tip.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%) translateY(20px)';
      tip.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    }, 5000);
  }

  // 3. THE HOOK
  const hookInterval = setInterval(() => {
    if (typeof window.___chipClickEvent === 'function') {
      clearInterval(hookInterval);
      const originalClick = window.___chipClickEvent;
      
      window.___chipClickEvent = function(chipEl) {
        originalClick.apply(this, arguments);
        const chipName = chipEl.getAttribute('data-chip-name');
        const isOn = chipEl.classList.contains('on');
        showChipTooltip(chipName, isOn);
      };
    }
  }, 50);

})();
</script>
